We further show that Mutual Information (MI) objectives capture complementary notions of inter-class structure: Graph Cut MI is closely related to centroid separation and Fisher-style discrimination, LogDet MI captures covariance-aware separation through Mahalanobis distance, and Facility Location MI measures nearest-mode representational overlap.
  We validate these theoretical characterizations using controlled synthetic experiments that independently vary variance, covariance, class imbalance, class separation, and multimodal overlap. Across all settings, the empirical behavior closely matches the proposed theory. Our results provide the first unified geometric and statistical understanding of submodular information measures and offer principled guidance for selecting and designing SIM-based objectives for representation learning.

</details>


### [58] [FedOGL: Combating Catastrophic Forgetting in Federated Open-World Multimodal Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27665)
*Zekai Chen,Haodong Lu,Shihao Li,Weiwei Ji,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedOGL，一个面向联邦多模态图学习的语义-结构记忆保持框架，通过在客户端进行重放与任务起始蒸馏保留历史决策行为，并通过投影到全局共享结构基上保护图传播记忆；在服务端维护并传递紧凑类别原型以促进跨客户端知识共享。实验表明相比最优基线，FedOGL将灾难性遗忘导致的性能退化降低42.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图学习中，客户端需从私有多模态图流中学习新类别、保留历史类别并拒绝未知类别样本，核心挑战是灾难性遗忘。该问题在联邦多模态图场景中不仅是分类器层面的失败，还包括模态语义覆盖、拓扑诱导的结构侵蚀以及联邦记忆碎片化三类深层遗忘机制，现有方法未能全面解决。

Method: 提出FedOGL框架，包含客户端与服务端两方面机制：(1) 客户端通过重放与任务起始蒸馏保留历史决策行为；(2) 客户端通过将图传播记忆投影到全局共享结构基上加以保护；(3) 服务端维护并传递紧凑类别原型，实现跨客户端知识共享且不暴露原始图数据。

Result: 与最佳性能基线相比，FedOGL将灾难性遗忘导致的性能退化降低了42.67%，同时在下游任务上保持或提升了性能。

Conclusion: FedOGL通过语义与结构双重视角的记忆保持策略，有效缓解了联邦多模态图学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，为联邦图学习的持续学习场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated graph learning enables collaborative training over decentralized graph data without sharing raw graph information. As such risks evolve, clients must learn emerging classes from private multimodal graph streams, retain historical categories, and reject samples outside the known class space. In this setting, clients must learn emerging classes from private multimodal graph streams while preserving historical categories and rejecting samples outside the current known class space. The core challenge is catastrophic forgetting, which in federated multimodal graphs is not merely a classifier-level failure: old knowledge can be erased through modality-semantic overwriting, topology-induced structural erosion, and federated memory fragmentation. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{FedOGL}, a semantic-structural memory preservation framework. On the client side, FedOGL preserves historical decision behavior through replay and task-start distillation, while protecting graph-propagation memory via projection onto a globally shared structure basis. On the server side, FedOGL maintains and transfers compact category prototypes to facilitate cross-client knowledge sharing without exposing raw graph data. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared with the best-performing baselines, FedOGL reduces performance degradation caused by catastrophic forgetting by \textbf{42.67\%}, while maintaining or improving performance on downstream tasks.

</details>


### [59] [Tight Sample Complexity for Low-Rank Adaptation: Matching Bounds and Rank Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27680)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立LoRA（低秩微调）的完整统计复杂度理论：在目标适配秩不超过r的假设下，ERM的上界为O~(rd/n)（通过局部Rademacher论证），并配以匹配的极小极大下界Ω(rd/n)（通过Fano型填充论证）。两者结合产生秩选择二分法：约束ERM的最优秩等于内在秩r*，过秩会带来惩罚；而核范数-再截断的自适应估计器对过秩免疫。理论预测（验证损失的U形曲线）在合成数据和DistilBERT/RoBERTa真实微调中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已成为大模型微调的标准机制，但其统计性质尚未被完全理解。现有泛化结果只给出了上界O~(sqrt(rd/n))或O~(rd/n)，缺乏匹配的下界，且如何选择LoRA秩r这一问题没有正式的答案。本文的目标是同时填补这两个空白，并对秩选择的统计行为给出理论刻画。

Method: （1）利用局部Rademacher论证，为秩不超过r的LoRA类上的经验风险最小化建立O~(rd/n)的过量风险上界；（2）通过Fano型填充构造R^{d×d}中秩r子空间的填充集，证明任何输出落在秩-r LoRA类中的估计器的极小极大下界为Ω(rd/n)；（3）整合上下界得到秩选择二分法：对约束ERM，最优秩等于内在秩r*，过秩有害；对核范数-再截断型自适应估计器，过秩无害，速率饱和于Θ~(r* d/n)。

Result: 三个主要结果：上界O~(rd/n)、极小极大下界Ω(rd/n)、以及秩选择二分法。理论预测了过参数化惩罚是未正则化ERM的性质而非LoRA类本身的属性。合成trace回归基准和三种（模型，任务）配置（DistilBERT/RoBERTa在SST-2/MRPC）上的真实LoRA微调均呈现预测的U形验证损失曲线，其中两种配置在大秩处显示出统计显著的损失膨胀（配对排列检验p=0.016）。

Conclusion: 本文在良性规范化的局部二次型区间内完整刻画了LoRA微调的统计复杂度，明确了未正则化经验风险最小化是过参数化惩罚的根源，而LoRA类本身并不具有内在的过秩惩罚。理论为LoRA秩选择提供了正式依据：对于约束ERM应选内在秩；对于核范数-截断型方法则无需担忧过秩。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard mechanism for fine-tuning large pretrained models, yet its statistical properties remain only partially understood. Existing generalization results provide upper bounds of the form O~(sqrt(rd/n)) or O~(rd/n), but a matching lower bound is missing, and the question of how to choose the LoRA rank r has no formal answer. Both gaps are closed here. A local Rademacher argument establishes an upper bound of O~(rd/n) on the excess risk of the empirical risk minimizer over rank-r LoRA, whenever the target adaptation has rank at most r. A matching minimax lower bound of Omega(rd/n) is then proved via a Fano-type packing of the rank-r subspace of R^{d x d}; the bound applies to any estimator whose output lies in the rank-r LoRA class. Combining the two yields a rank-selection dichotomy. For the constrained empirical risk minimizer, the optimal rank equals the intrinsic rank r*, and over-ranking strictly hurts. For adaptive estimators of the nuclear-norm-then-truncate type, over-ranking is harmless and the rate saturates at Theta~(r* d / n) regardless of r. Taken together, the three results characterize the statistical complexity of LoRA fine-tuning within the well-specified locally quadratic regime, and identify the empirically observed over-parameterization penalty as a property of unregularized empirical risk minimization rather than of the LoRA class itself. Predictions of the theory are verified on a synthetic trace-regression benchmark and on real LoRA fine-tuning across three (model, task) configurations covering DistilBERT and RoBERTa on SST-2 and MRPC. All configurations exhibit the predicted U-shape in validation loss, with two showing statistically significant loss inflation at large ranks (paired permutation p = 0.016).

</details>


### [60] [Event-Structured Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Differentiable Critical Clearing Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27681)
*Baoli Hao,Chenxi Hu,Ming Zhong,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种事件结构化物理信息神经网络（ES-PINN），对电力系统暂态稳定评估中的关键清除时间（CCT）进行精确且高效的估计。该方法将网络表征与故障前、故障中、故障后三段推进动力学对齐，通过事件接口精确状态链式传递保证物理一致性，并利用平滑轨迹诱导稳定裕度实现CCT边界的可微逼近。在IEEE 9、14和30节点系统上的实验证明了其在轨迹精度、稳定性边界提取和计算效率上优于传统神经代理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暂态稳定评估对于防止发电机跳闸和连锁停电至关重要，而关键清除时间（CCT）是衡量系统故障后能否恢复同步的核心指标。然而，CCT的可靠估计具有挑战性：复杂的故障清除动力学要求在不同故障严重程度和清除时间下进行大量重复仿真，计算代价高昂。现有方法难以在精度与效率之间取得平衡，因此需要一种既能精确建模故障动力学又能高效估计CCT的框架。

Method: 提出事件结构化物理信息神经网络（ES-PINN），核心思路包括：(1) 将网络表征与pre-fault、fault-on、post-clearing三段动力学对齐，并在事件界面上施加精确的状态链式约束；(2) 构造平滑的轨迹诱导稳定性裕度，以可微方式逼近CCT边界，支持边界提取、局部灵敏度分析和蒸馏读出头的直接CCT预测；(3) 理论上推导了残差-轨迹-CCT的局部误差界，证明精确事件链式消除了单独的状态接口缺陷项；(4) 在IEEE 9、14和30节点系统上对机械和电气故障场景进行多故障实验验证。

Result: 在IEEE 9、14和30节点系统上，ES-PINN在留出测试集的轨迹精度和稳定边界精度上一致优于匹配的神经代理基线；完整的DAE网络验证、多故障实验和运行时间分析进一步证明了框架的有效性和计算效率。

Conclusion: ES-PINN通过事件结构化的神经表示和精确接口链式传递，为电力系统暂态稳定评估提供了一种高精度、可微分且计算高效的CCT估计框架，兼具理论误差保障和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Transient-stability assessment determines whether a power system can recover after a disturbance and is therefore essential to preventing generator trips and cascading outages. A key metric is the critical clearing time (CCT), which specifies the maximum time available to clear a fault before synchronism is lost. Reliable CCT estimation is challenging because complicated fault-clearing dynamics require repeated simulations over many fault severities and clearing times. We propose an event-structured physics-informed neural network (ES-PINN) that aligns its representation with the pre-fault, fault-on, and post-clearing swing dynamics and enforces exact state chaining across event interfaces. A smooth trajectory-induced stability margin defines a differentiable approximation of the CCT boundary, enabling accurate boundary extraction, local sensitivity analysis, and optional direct CCT prediction through a distilled readout. We further prove a local residual-to-trajectory-to-CCT error estimate, in which exact event chaining eliminates separate state-interface defect terms. Experiments on IEEE 9-, 14-, and 30-bus systems show that ES-PINN consistently improves held-out trajectory and stability-boundary accuracy over matched neural-surrogate baselines across mechanical and electrical contingencies with multiple clearing configurations. Additional full-network DAE validation, multi-fault experiments, and runtime analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and computational efficiency of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [61] [NMINE: Normalized Mutual Information Neural Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27710)
*Petra Eerikinharju,Marko Tuononen,Ville Hautamäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全神经网络的连续变量归一化互信息估计器，结合MINE的互信息估计与MI-NEE启发的边缘熵估计，在1到8维高斯数据上相比KSG基线方法提升了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互信息是无界的，无法在不同数据集、维度或应用间直接比较。归一化互信息虽有实际应用价值（如分子动力学、可解释机器学习），但现有估计器对维度和数值稳定性敏感，需要一个更稳健的估计方法。

Method: 提出全神经网络归一化互信息估计器：1) 使用Donsker-Varadhan表示通过MINE方法估计互信息；2) 通过MI-NEE启发的神经边缘熵估计器，学习每个边缘分布与均匀参考分布之间的散度来恢复熵；3) 将两者结合得到归一化的依赖得分。

Result: 在1到8维高斯数据上的实验表明，所提估计器相比基于KSG的归一化互信息基线方法在准确性上有所提升。

Conclusion: 神经估计是连续多维设置中归一化依赖测量的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Mutual information is a general measure of statistical dependence that captures both linear and nonlinear relationships between random variables. For continuous and multidimensional variables For continuous multidimensional variables, mutual information must be estimated from samples. Because mutual information is unbounded, its values are not directly comparable across datasets, dimensions, or applications. Normalized mutual information addresses this limitation by converting mutual information into a normalized dependency score. Recent work has demonstrated the practical value of normalized mutual information in applications such as molecular dynamics {arXiv:2405.04980} and interpretable machine learning {arXiv:2409.16768}, but existing estimators remain sensitive to dimensionality and numerical stability {arXiv:2410.07642}.
  In this paper, we propose a fully neural normalized mutual information estimator for continuous variables. The proposed approach combines a MINE-based neural mutual information estimator {arXiv:1801.04062} with MI-NEE-inspired neural marginal entropy estimators {arXiv:1905.12957}. Mutual information is estimated using the Donsker--Varadhan representation, while marginal entropies are estimated by learning the divergence between each marginal distribution and a uniform reference distribution, from which entropy is recovered. The resulting estimator provides a neural alternative to k-nearest-neighbor-based normalized mutual information estimation {arXiv:2405.04980}.
  Experiments on Gaussian data from one to eight dimensions show that the proposed estimator improves accuracy over a KSG-based normalized mutual information baseline. These results indicate that neural estimation is a promising direction for normalized dependency measurement in continuous multidimensional settings.

</details>


### [62] [VESTIGE: A Knowledge-Guided Masking Strategy for Corruption-Aware Fine-Tuning of Genomic Transformers, Validated on Ancient DNA Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27712)
*Angshuman Chakravertty,Rahul Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VESTIGE是一种无需参数、即插即用的掩码语言模型（MLM）collator替代方案，将掩码分布与经验测量的每位置退化谱对齐，取代标准MLM假设的均匀掩码概率。在古DNA（aDNA）重建任务中，该方法在模型、数据、种子和超参数完全固定的条件下，仅通过将C/G掩码率重新缩放至与标准MLM相同的15%均值来隔离空间重分布效应。在DNABERT-2上的猛犸象CDS语料库实验中，VESTIGE在所有六个末端区域宽度和626个配对窗口中均优于标准MLM（Delta = +4.18至+10.35个百分点，p < 10^-8），验证交叉熵降低13%（3.274 vs 3.757），并在10-30倍放大的损伤条件下仍获得TM-score > 0.95的ESMFold重建。该方法为领域无关原则，可推广至FFPE、亚硫酸盐、宏基因组或纳米孔测序等任何可测量的位置或上下文特定退化谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码语言模型微调假设重建难度与位置无关，对所有token位置施用统一掩码概率。但当退化过程具有特征性且集中在可预测位置时（如古DNA中胞嘧啶脱氨基产生的C-to-T/G-to-A梯度），该假设失效——在峰值损伤位点，模型表现甚至不如频率匹配的随机预测器。作者旨在解决这一假设缺陷，使掩码分布与实际损伤分布对齐。

Method: VESTIGE是一个参数无关、即插即用的MLM collator替代品。核心方法是用mapDamage2定量的每位置损伤谱（C-to-T/G-to-A梯度）代替均匀掩码分布。具体实现为：将平均C/G掩码率重新缩放至15%（与标准MLM相同），从而隔离空间重分布作为唯一变量。实验设置保持模型（DNABERT-2）、数据（猛犸象CDS语料库）、种子和超参数在所有对照运行中完全固定。评估涵盖六个末端区域宽度和626个配对窗口，并额外采用10-30倍放大的损伤条件测试稳健性，以及1D CNN生物安全分类器进行安全评估。

Result: VESTIGE在所有六个末端区域宽度下均优于标准MLM，准确率提升+4.18至+10.35个百分点（全部p < 10^-8）。验证交叉熵从3.757降至3.274，降低了13%。即使在损伤放大10-30倍（远超真实PMD率）的条件下，所有六个基因重建的ESMFold TM-score仍> 0.95。生物安全评估方面，1D CNN分类器达到AUC = 0.935，清除98.2%的重建窗口，剩余1.76%可归因于参考基因组特征而非重建伪影。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了知识引导的训练范式：将掩码分布与经验测量的位置相关退化谱对齐能显著提升降级序列的重建性能。VESTIGE的核心贡献在于它不改变模型架构或目标函数，仅通过改造数据整理（collation）过程即可实现性能提升。其领域无关性（PMD数组可替换为FFPE、亚硫酸盐、宏基因组或纳米孔退化谱）使其成为处理退化或噪声序列输入的通用训练工具。该方法在生物安全评估中表现出色，重建序列不会引入可被分类器利用的伪影特征。

Abstract: Standard masked-language-model fine-tuning applies a uniform masking probability across every token position, assuming reconstruction difficulty is position-agnostic. When the degradation process is characterised and concentrated at predictable positions, this assumption fails: at peak damage sites the model can underperform a frequency-matched random predictor. We introduce VESTIGE, a parameter-free, drop-in replacement for the standard MLM collator that aligns the masking distribution with an empirically measured per-position corruption profile. We apply it to ancient DNA (aDNA) reconstruction, where cytosine deamination produces a position-dependent C-to-T / G-to-A gradient quantified per-position by mapDamage2. Rescaling so the mean C/G masking rate equals 15% - identical to standard MLM - isolates spatial redistribution as the sole variable, with model, data, seed, and hyperparameters held fixed across both DNABERT-2 runs on a mammoth CDS corpus (two specimens, seven genes). Across six terminal-zone widths and 626 paired windows, VESTIGE leads standard MLM at every width (Delta = +4.18 to +10.35 pp, all p < 10^-8), cuts validation cross-entropy by 13% (3.274 vs. 3.757), and yields ESMFold reconstructions with TM-score > 0.95 across all six reconstructions (three genes) even under damage amplified 10-30x beyond authentic PMD rates. A 1D CNN biosecurity classifier returns AUC = 0.935 and clears 98.2% of reconstructed windows, the 1.76% remainder attributable to reference-genome features, not reconstruction artefacts. The principle is domain-agnostic: any measurable position- or context-specific corruption profile - FFPE, bisulfite, metagenomic, or nanopore - substitutes directly for the PMD array, making VESTIGE a knowledge-guided training routine for intelligent systems operating on degraded or noisy sequence inputs.

</details>


### [63] [Improving the Robustness/Accuracy Tradeoff Against Adversarial Attacks Using Information Bottleneck Distillation Through Dual Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27737)
*Vincent Ryusuke Takahashi,Yoshinari Takeishi,Jun'ichi Takeuchi,Kave Salamatian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在信息瓶颈蒸馏（IBD）框架的基础上，引入一个仅用干净样本训练的额外教师模型（干净教师），并利用跨层注意力矩阵将干净教师和鲁棒教师的特征同时蒸馏给学生模型。在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，该方法相比原始IBD在干净样本上提高了分类准确率，同时保持了相近的对抗样本准确率，在与包括B-MTARD在内的最新方法对比中具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络易受对抗攻击影响，而现有防御方法（如IBD）虽然提升了鲁棒性，但在干净输入上的分类准确率会显著下降，因此需要在干净准确率与鲁棒准确率之间取得更好的平衡。

Method: 扩展IBD框架，在原有对抗训练得到的鲁棒教师模型基础上，额外引入一个仅用干净数据训练的干净教师模型。通过跨层注意力矩阵（cross-layer attention matrix）将两个教师模型的特征同时传输给学生模型，实现双教师蒸馏。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，所提方法相比原始IBD在干净样本上的分类准确率得到提升，同时对抗样本上的准确率保持近似。方法与包括B-MTARD在内的最新双教师蒸馏方法相比具有竞争力，尤其在干净准确率与鲁棒准确率的调和平均方面表现优异。此外还分析了不同训练设置对注意力模块的影响。

Conclusion: 通过在IBD框架中引入干净教师模型并采用跨层注意力矩阵进行特征蒸馏，可以在提升干净样本准确率的同时保持对抗鲁棒性，从而在鲁棒性和准确率之间实现更好的权衡，达到与最先进方法相当的性能。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have achieved remarkable success in classical machine learning problems. However, they are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Countermeasures proposed in the literature, notably Information Bottleneck Distillation (IBD) introduced by Kuang et al., degrade the classification accuracy on clean inputs while improving the robustness to adversarial inputs. In this work, we extend the IBD framework by introducing an extra teacher model (clean teacher) trained with only clean inputs, into the distillation process from a robust teacher model trained by adversarial training. The features of both clean and robust teachers are transferred to the student through a cross-layer attention matrix. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 datasets show that the proposed method improves classification accuracy on clean samples compared to the original IBD, while maintaining similar accuracy on adversarial samples. Furthermore, our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art approaches, including the recent dual-teacher distillation framework B-MTARD, particularly in terms of the harmonic mean between clean and robust accuracy. We also analyze the impact of different training settings that have different influences on the attention module.

</details>


### [64] [ROCS: Request-Oriented Compute Sharing for Efficient Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27744)
*Yuxin Chen,Liang Luo,Buyun Zhang,Jian Jiao,Boda Li,Haoyu Wang,Tongyi Tang,Ao Cai,Zijian Shen,Zhengkai Zhang,Wenyi Xie,Ryan Dick,Han Liu,Neng Shi,Bin Yu,Jianbo Xiao,Shuyao Bi,Hongtao Yu,Yuanwei Fang,Zhuoran Zhao,Sijia Chen,Yang Chen,Shuqi Yang,Qianru Li,Zikun Liu,Wei Ling,Sihan Zeng,Longhao Jin,Jiaxin Lu,Yinbin Ma,Jiawei Li,Yichen Ruan,Yong Ler Lee,Birmingham Guan,Zijian Li,Jianbo Sun,Zhengyu Zhang,Zeliang Chen,Xiaohan Wei,Yuchen Hao,GP Musumeci,Venkatesh Ranganathan,Yantao Yao,Chunqiang Tang,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Ellie Dingqiao Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROCS（面向请求的计算共享）范式，利用推荐推理中"一次请求、多个候选"的特性，将计算从"每个候选"迁移到"每个请求"执行，在不牺牲预测质量的前提下显著提升推理效率，并已在大规模推荐系统中部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型通过扩展特征交互和序列模块来提升预测质量，但生产环境的成本约束限制了系统的可扩展性。需要在保持预测质量的同时提高推理效率，以突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出ROCS推理范式，利用每个用户请求对多个候选进行评估、且请求侧特征在候选间共享的特性，尽可能延迟请求-候选交互，隔离候选相关表征，将模型大部分计算从"每个候选一次"改为"每个请求一次"。为实现该范式，开发了：(1) 广义层掩码（GLM）用于在特征交互架构中强制候选隔离；(2) 深度交叉注意力（DCA）将请求导向共享扩展到序列架构；(3) 内核内广播优化（IKBO）用于加速GPU部署。

Result: 在公开基准上，ROCS在多种推荐骨干网络上持续改善质量-效率权衡。在生产规模负载上：检索模型实现最高3倍QPS提升且无质量下降；短视频排序模型在50% QPS增益下实现0.5%相对LogLoss改进。已部署于涵盖广告和自然内容、检索和排序阶段的多个大规模推荐系统，推理复杂度跨越两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: ROCS通过充分利用推荐推理中请求-候选共享特性，实现了效率与质量的双重提升，在降低基础设施成本的同时带来显著的在线收益，验证了该范式在大规模推荐系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Modern recommendation models gain prediction quality by scaling feature-interaction and sequence modules, but production cost constraints cap how far systems can scale.
  In this work, we propose Request-Oriented Compute Sharing (ROCS), a modeling and inference paradigm that exploits a unique property of recommendation inference: each user request is evaluated against many candidates, while request-side features are shared across candidates. ROCS defers request-candidate interactions as late as possible, isolates candidate-dependent representations, and evaluates substantial portions of the model once per request rather than once per candidate, significantly improving inference efficiency while maintaining or improving prediction quality. To realize this paradigm, we develop Generalized Layer Masking (GLM) to enforce candidate isolation in feature-interaction architectures, and Deep Cross Attention (DCA) to extend request-oriented sharing to sequence architectures. To support efficient GPU deployment, we co-design In-Kernel Broadcast Optimization (IKBO) that significantly accelerates ROCS model execution.
  Experiments on public benchmarks show that ROCS consistently improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff across recommendation backbones. On production-scale workloads, ROCS achieves up to a 3x QPS improvement on retrieval models without quality degradation and a 0.5% relative LogLoss improvement with a 50% QPS gain on a short-form video ranking model. ROCS has been deployed across large-scale recommendation systems spanning ads and organic surfaces, retrieval and ranking stages, and more than two orders of magnitude in inference complexity, delivering significant online gains at reduced infrastructure cost.

</details>


### [65] [DAS-PMVC: A Framework for Partial Multi-View Clustering via Dual Alignment and Structure Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27761)
*Shubin Ma,Liang Zhao,Chuanye He,Zhenjiao Liu,Liang Zou,Lin Yuanbo Wu,Yu Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DAS-PMVC的部分多视图聚类框架，通过锚点图结构对齐、结构增强特征学习和双重对齐策略来解决视图间不对齐（PVAP）问题，实验证明其在多个数据集上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图聚类近年受到广泛关注，但数据采集设备的限制导致不同视图数据之间存在不对齐问题（即部分视图对齐问题PVAP）。视图不对称和无关样本的存在严重影响了聚类性能，因此需要一种有效的方法来缓解这些问题。

Method: DAS-PMVC框架包含三个核心部分：(1) 锚点图结构对齐——通过锚点关系获得具有一致潜在空间的样本联合嵌入表示，实现初始视图对齐；(2) 结构增强特征学习——通过预训练学习视图结构信息，并结合多视图图卷积网络从对齐的图结构中提取深层潜在特征以提升表示判别力；(3) 双重对齐策略——预训练阶段通过锚点图进行初始对齐，训练阶段引入对比学习损失和匈牙利算法进一步优化潜在特征的对齐。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，DAS-PMVC框架在聚类性能上优于现有的最先进方法，证明了其有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: DAS-PMVC通过结合视图结构一致性和语义相关性，有效地缓解了部分多视图聚类中的视图不对称和无关样本问题，为部分视图场景下的多视图聚类提供了一种性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, multi-view clustering has attracted widespread research interest. However, due to limitations in data collection devices, data across different views often suffer from misalignment, leading to the partial view alignment problem (PVAP). To mitigate the impact of view asymmetry and irrelevant samples, this paper proposes a framework for partial multi-view clustering via dual alignment and structure enhancement (DAS-PMVC), which leverages view structure consistency and semantic relevance. Specifically, DAS-PMVC includes three parts: \textbf{anchor graph structure alignment}, where sample joint embedding representations with consistent latent space are derived from anchor point relationships for initial view alignment; \textbf{structure-enhanced feature learning}, where the model learns view structure information through pretraining and combines multi-view graph convolutional networks to further extract deep latent features from the aligned graph structure to improve the discriminative power of representations; and \textbf{a dual alignment strategy}, where initial alignment is performed through the anchor graph in the pretraining phase, and contrastive learning loss and the Hungarian algorithm are introduced in the training phase to further optimize the alignment of latent features. Experimental results on various datasets demonstrate that the DAS-PMVC framework outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in clustering performance, showcasing its effectiveness and superiority.

</details>


### [66] [Train Small, Deploy Large: Zero-Shot GNN Transfer Through Geometric Renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27767)
*Robert Jankowski,Pedro Almagro-Blanco,Marián Boguñá,Melanie Weber,M. Ángeles Serrano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本迁移协议，通过在几何重整化（GR）缩小的图副本上训练GNN模型，然后直接将权重迁移到原始全分辨率图上，无需重新训练即可部署。实验表明该方法在保持预测性能的同时显著降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN虽然能处理大图，但在百万节点规模下对基础设施敏感，需要可扩展的训练技术来处理更大规模的图。核心问题在于：能否在缩小的图副本上训练模型，然后直接部署到全分辨率图上而无需重新训练？这旨在解决大规模图训练的昂贵成本问题。

Method: 采用几何重整化（Geometric Renormalization, GR）方法对图进行粗粒化，生成缩小的图副本。在此缩小副本上训练GNN，训练完成后将学习到的权重直接零样本迁移到原始全分辨率网络上，无需任何微调或重新训练。

Result: 在合成网络和真实世界网络上，基于GR缩小副本训练的模型保持了原始规模下的大部分预测性能，同时显著降低了训练成本。此外，跨尺度的学习表征和预测轨迹保持一致对齐。结果表明结构相似性比网络规模对GNN可迁移性的影响更为关键。

Conclusion: 结构相似性可能是决定GNN可迁移性的关键因素，而非网络规模本身。这一发现为构建尺度等变的图架构（scale-equivariant graph architectures）开辟了新的路径。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can operate on large graphs but become infrastructure-sensitive at the scale of millions of nodes and typically require scalable training techniques for even larger graphs. This raises a central question: when can a model trained on a smaller, scaled-down replica of a graph be deployed on the full-resolution graph without retraining? We introduce a zero-shot transfer protocol in which a GNN is trained on a graph coarse-grained by geometric renormalization (GR), and the resulting weights are transferred directly to the original network. Across synthetic and real-world networks, training on GR scaled-down replicas preserves much of the original-scale predictive performance while significantly reducing training cost. We further find that learned representations and predictive trajectories remain aligned across scales. These findings suggest that structural similarity may be more important than network size in determining GNN transferability, opening a path toward scale-equivariant graph architectures.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond the Best Teacher: Expanding and Compressing the Reasoning Solution Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27770)
*Songshuo Lu,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"先扩展后压缩"框架，通过多教师策略蒸馏训练出比最强单个教师更强的学生模型。扩展阶段使用RGRPO训练互补教师序列，压缩阶段使用TU-OPD让学生从可靠教师的采样token中学习，最终在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随三个领域均超越最强单个教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次强化学习训练得到的策略虽然能成为强推理器，但往往只放大部分有效解模式，作为教师不够完整。作者因此认为RL训练的策略应被视为多模态推理解流形的局部探针，而非全局可靠的监督者，需要构建互补教师集合来覆盖更完整的解空间。

Method: 提出expand-then-compress框架：(1) 扩展阶段使用RGRPO，从共同初始化训练教师序列，每轮将未被已有教师联合覆盖的样本重定向给后续教师；(2) 压缩阶段使用TU-OPD，学生从其自身响应前缀学习，仅可靠教师贡献，其采样token的OPD损失按每个样本的质量加权；(3) 引入Consensus-Residual Decomposition保留最优教师在可靠同伴之上的额外token偏好，防止专家行为在教师聚合时被抑制。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随三个领域，Qwen3-1.7B学生模型始终优于最强单个教师，相对改进分别为2.0%、8.3%和6.9%，同时保持单模型推理。

Conclusion: 更强的学生不是通过选择单个更好的教师获得，而是通过刻意构建和压缩互补教师联合来实现。这确立了"构建互补教师联合优于选择单一教师"的简单而有力的原则。

Abstract: A single reinforcement-learning run can produce a strong reasoner yet an incomplete teacher: it often amplifies only a subset of the valid solution modes. We argue that reinforcement learning (RL)-trained policies should therefore be viewed as local probes of a multi-basin reasoning solution manifold, rather than as globally reliable supervisors. Based on this view, we propose an expand-then-compress framework that couples teacher construction with multi-teacher policy distillation. In the expansion stage, Residual Group Relative Policy Optimization (RGRPO) trains a sequence of teachers from a common initialization and redirects each later round toward examples not yet covered by the accumulated teacher union. In the compression stage, reliability-gated Teacher-Union On-policy Distillation (TU-OPD) lets the student learn from its own response prefixes. For each example, only reliable teachers contribute, and their sampled-token OPD losses are weighted by their per-example quality. We further introduce Consensus-Residual Decomposition, which preserves a winner teacher's excess token preferences over its reliable peers, preventing specialist behavior from being suppressed during teacher aggregation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction following show that the resulting Qwen3-1.7B student consistently outperforms the strongest individual teacher across all three domains, yielding relative improvements of 2.0%, 8.3%, and 6.9%, respectively, while retaining single-model inference. These results establish a simple but powerful principle: stronger students can be obtained not by selecting a single better teacher, but by deliberately constructing and compressing a complementary teacher union.

</details>


### [68] [RIPPLE: Generating Multi-Channel Phase, Not Recovering It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27775)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Yeajin Lee,Dayeon Shin,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RIPPLE（Rectified Inter-channel Phase with Prior-based LEarning）框架，将Griffin-Lim算法从最终相位估计器重新诠释为相位先验，通过整流流（rectified flow）在显式通道间相位损失下细化相位，从而在多通道波形应用中联合生成幅度与相位，优于传统逐通道相位恢复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽能以高保真度合成幅度谱，但相位被委托给独立逐通道恢复模块（如Griffin-Lim、声码器或潜在解码器）。然而多通道波形（空间音频、三分量地震图）的物理内容恰恰蕴含在通道间的相位关系中，逐通道独立恢复无法产生这种关系。此外，常用的基于幅度谱的指标对通道间相位相干性崩塌几乎不敏感，导致管线虽丢失物理信息仍能取得高分，这一"隐性代价"被现有评估体系掩盖。

Method: RIPPLE将Griffin-Lim重新解释为相位先验而非最终估计器：从源相位初始化，先验携带待保留的通道间结构；随后使用整流流（rectified flow）在显式通道间相位损失函数指导下将相位细化为目标。该框架在显式学习机制中集成了通道间相位一致性约束。

Result: 在一阶环境声（ambisonics）环境迁移和地震跨台站翻译两个物理上不相关的领域上，RIPPLE在相干性指标上优于基于恢复的管线。地震案例尤为关键：在不同架构生成器上，逐通道恢复的S波偏振误差接近57.3°的随机期望值，而学习得到的相位将其降至33.8°。

Conclusion: 研究表明多通道波形的相位应当被生成而非恢复，RIPPLE通过在显式通道间相位损失的指导下学习相位，能够保留下游分析所需的物理相干信息，显著优于传统逐通道恢复方案。

Abstract: Generative models synthesize magnitude spectra with high fidelity, while phase is delegated to a recovery module---Griffin--Lim, a vocoder, or a latent decoder---applied independently to each channel. For multi-channel waveforms this delegation is costly: the physical content of spatial audio and three-component seismograms lives in the phase relationships between channels, precisely what channel-independent recovery cannot produce. The cost is also invisible, since the magnitude-based metrics common to both fields barely move when inter-channel phase coherence collapses---so a pipeline can discard the physical information in its output while still scoring well. We argue that phase should be generated, not recovered, and present RIPPLE (Rectified Inter-channel Phase with Prior-based LEarning), which reinterprets Griffin--Lim as a phase **prior** rather than a final estimator: initialized from the source phase, this prior carries the inter-channel structure to be preserved, and a rectified flow refines it toward the target under an explicit inter-channel phase loss. Tested on first-order ambisonics environment transfer and seismic cross-station translation---two physically unrelated domains---RIPPLE outperforms recovery-based pipelines on the coherence metrics that downstream analyses consume. The seismic case is decisive: across architecturally distinct generators, per-channel recovery leaves S-wave polarization error near the $57.3^\circ$ random expectation, whereas learned phase reduces it to $33.8^\circ$.

</details>


### [69] [LoRA Scaffolded Policy Optimization (LSPO): A Sampling-Time Low-Rank Scaffold for Recovering Reinforcement-Learning Gradient on Zero-Reward Cliff Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27787)
*Ken Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LSPO（LoRA Scaffolded Policy Optimization），一种采样时机制，用于解决RLVR在数学推理中"悬崖提示"（cliff prompts）上的梯度消失问题：当所有采样均失败时，组归一化优势为零，GRPO在这些提示上无梯度。LSPO通过临时LoRA适配器辅助重采样，并用重要性采样校正将成功轨迹拼回RL批次，最终仅训练基础模型。在DeepMath-103K上以DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B验证，16个评测单元中15个严格胜出、1个平局，平均提升+3.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在数学推理中有一个结构性盲点：对"悬崖提示"（每组所有采样均失败），组归一化优势恒为零，导致GRPO在这些处于模型能力前沿的提示上不产生任何梯度，从而浪费了最关键的训练信号。

Method: LSPO每个RL步执行如下流程：(1) 检测悬崖提示；(2) 用小型低秩（LoRA）适配器对这些提示的真值解做简短的监督微调；(3) 用基础模型加适配器重新采样悬崖提示；(4) 用重要性采样校正将成功的完成拼接回RL批次；(5) 仅对基础模型执行GRPO步；适配器只接收监督梯度且在校验点处丢弃，最终得到纯基础模型。

Result: 在DeepMath-103K上以DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B、n=5配对种子、1000步报告时域评估：LSPO在全部16个（benchmark, pass@k）单元上匹配或超过DAPO基线（15个严格胜出，1个精确平局），AIME24/pass@4最高+10.7分，AIME24和AIME26 pass@16均+6.7分，MATH500/pass@1 +2.4分，16个单元平均提升+3.8分。

Conclusion: LSPO通过采样时的LoRA脚手架机制有效恢复了悬崖提示上丢失的梯度，在不引入额外推理开销（最终模型为纯基础模型）的前提下显著提升数学推理性能，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) for mathematical reasoning suffers from a structural blind spot: on "cliff" prompts-those on which every sampled rollout in a group fails-the group-normalized advantage is identically zero, so GRPO produces no gradient on precisely the prompts at the frontier of the model's capability. We introduce LoRA Scaffolded Policy Optimization (LSPO), a sampling-time mechanism that recovers this lost gradient. Each RL step, LSPO detects cliff prompts, fits a small low-rank (LoRA) adapter by a brief supervised step on their ground-truth solutions, re-rolls the cliffs with the base-plus-adapter model, splices the now-successful completions back into the RL batch with an importance-sampling correction, and takes a GRPO step on the base alone; the adapter receives only the supervised gradient and is discarded at checkpoint, yielding a base-only model. On DeepMath-103K with DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B, evaluated over n=5 paired seeds per arm at a matched 1000-step reporting horizon, LSPO's 5-seed mean matches or beats a DAPO baseline on all 16 (benchmark, pass@k) cells (15 strict wins and one exact tie), with gains of up to +10.7 points on AIME24/pass@4, +6.7 points on AIME24 and AIME26 at pass@16, and +2.4 points on MATH500/pass@1; averaged over the 16 cells the improvement is +3.8 points.

</details>


### [70] [Revisiting Predictive Process Monitoring in the Age of Foundation Models: A Comparative Study of Sequence, Tabular, and LLM Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27797)
*Lennart Fertig,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Tobias Sesterhenn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了预测性流程监控（PPM）中的三大建模范式：经典序列模型（如LSTM）、大型语言模型（LLM）以及表格式基础模型，通过跨多个数据集和预测任务的控制基准测试得出：序列模型在下一个活动预测中表现最佳，表格式基础模型在时间类任务上具有竞争力，而LLM通常表现落后且成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPM领域近年被从头训练的深度序列模型（如LSTM）主导，但基础模型方法（特别是LLM）正被越来越多地探索。表格式基础模型具备上下文学习能力，是颇具前景的替代方案，但尚未在PPM中得到系统性基准测试。因此，经典序列模型在这种不断演进的格局中是否仍具竞争力尚不明确，需要明确的对比研究。

Method: 本文从概念和实证两个层面比较三大建模范式：序列模型（LSTM等）、大型语言模型（LLM）以及表格式基础模型。通过设计受控基准测试，在多个流程挖掘数据集上覆盖多个预测任务（如下一个活动预测、剩余时间预测、下一个事件时间预测）进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：序列模型在下一个活动预测任务上始终表现最佳；表格式基础模型在时间类任务（剩余时间、下一事件时间）上具有竞争力；LLM在这两类任务上通常表现落后，但其运行成本却更高。

Conclusion: 在PPM任务中，经典序列模型仍是最优选择之一，表格式基础模型在时间类任务中是可替代的竞争者，而LLM在当前阶段性价比不高，尽管其作为基础模型方法仍有探索潜力。

Abstract: Predictive process monitoring (PPM) leverages event logs to forecast the future of running process instances, for instance, predicting the next activity, the remaining time until case completion, or the time to the next event. While PPM research in recent years has been dominated by deep sequence models trained from scratch, such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models, foundation-model approaches---particularly large language models (LLMs)---are increasingly explored for PPM. At the same time, tabular foundation models with in-context learning capabilities offer a promising alternative but have not yet been systematically benchmarked for PPM. Thus, it remains unclear whether classical sequence-based models remain competitive in this evolving landscape. This paper compares the three modeling paradigms both conceptually and empirically through a controlled benchmark across multiple datasets and prediction tasks. The results show that sequence models consistently perform best for next activity prediction, whereas tabular foundation models are competitive on temporal tasks, with LLMs usually lagging behind despite higher cost.

</details>


### [71] [Learning-Augmented and Randomized Algorithms for Line Aggregation with Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27807)
*Tianhang Lu,Runtian Ren,Shengcai Liu,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究直线度量上带延迟的在线聚合问题，提出了学习增强（learning-augmented）的确定性Balance算法、经典对抗模型下的随机化算法，以及二者的结合——随机化学习增强算法，并给出相应的竞争比、鲁棒性和一致性保证，同时通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线聚合问题是计算机网络和分布式系统中的经典问题。已有工作主要关注确定性算法的竞争比分析（如Balance算法的5-竞争比），但对随机化算法和学习增强算法的研究尚不充分。本文旨在通过引入建议服务长度的在线建议机制，同时提升算法在最坏情况下的鲁棒性和在良好建议下的一致性。

Method: 对于每个λ∈(0,1]，提出：(1) 确定性学习增强Balance算法，具有(4/λ+1/λ²)-鲁棒性和(4+λ)-一致性；(2) 对抗模型下针对盲目对手的随机化算法，竞争比为e+1，优于确定性Balance的5-竞争比下界；(3) 将两者结合得到随机化学习增强算法，具有(e/λ+1/λ²)-鲁棒性和(e+λ)-一致性；(4) 建立随机化在线算法竞争比的下界e（改进了此前e/(e-1)的下界）；(5) 通过数值实验验证算法性能。

Result: 提出了三种新算法并给出严格的理论保证：确定性学习增强算法达到(4/λ+1/λ²)-鲁棒和(4+λ)-一致；随机化算法达到(e+1)-竞争比，不仅突破了确定性算法的5-竞争比基准，甚至低于确定性算法4的下界；随机化学习增强算法达到(e/λ+1/λ²)-鲁棒和(e+λ)-一致；并改进随机化下界至e。数值实验验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上证明了随机化策略和学习增强策略能显著改善带延迟在线聚合问题的性能，特别是在竞争比下界上取得了突破性进展（从e/(e-1)提升到e），同时为实际应用提供了有效的算法设计和实验验证。

Abstract: This paper studies learning-augmented and randomized online aggregation with delays on a line metric. We consider advice given as online suggested service lengths, and evaluate the algorithms in terms of robustness and consistency. For each $λ\in (0,1]$, we first propose a deterministic learning-augmented \textsc{Balance} algorithm that is $(4/λ+1/λ^2)$-robust and $(4+λ)$-consistent. We also propose a randomized algorithm for the problem in the classical adversarial model, which is $(e+1)$-competitive against an oblivious adversary, improving over the deterministic $5$-competitive \textsc{Balance} benchmark~\cite{bienkowski2013chain}. Notably, this competitive ratio is even lower than the lower bound of $4$ for deterministic online algorithms. Moreover, we establish a lower bound of $e$ on the competitive ratio of randomized online algorithms, improving the previous lower bound of $e/(e-1)$. Besides, we combine the two ideas and obtain a randomized learning-augmented algorithm that is $(e/λ+1/λ^2)$-robust and $(e+λ)$-consistent. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to complement our theoretical analysis and evaluate the empirical performance of our algorithms.

</details>


### [72] [Contrastive Concept Importance: Explaining Pairwise Class Decisions Through Automatically Extracted Concept Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27904)
*Roel Visser,Isaac Roberts,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比式概念重要性（CCI）方法，用于解答"为什么是P而不是Q？"这类对比性归因问题，通过将目标类和对照类之间的logit差值归因到自动提取的视觉概念基上，揭示模型在类别对区分中的细粒度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于概念的解释方法只回答非对比性的"为什么是P？"问题，但在模型错分类、类别混淆和低置信度预测等场景中，更自然的提问方式是"为什么是P而不是Q？"。现有方法无法揭示具体影响类别对区分的概念，因此需要一种对比式归因框架。

Method: CCI方法将目标类P和对照类Q之间的logit差值归因到自动提取的视觉概念基中。归因分数带有正负号，能指示概念是支持目标类还是支持对照类，并可进一步分解为目标logit效应和对照logit效应，从而区分全局重要概念与类别对特定概念，判断其效应是共享、单侧还是直接对比的。

Result: 在ImageNet类别对上使用CRAFT风格概念基进行评估，通过插入/删除曲线、logit分解分析和语义类层次结构验证。结果表明CCI能揭示普通概念重要性无法捕捉的类别对特定模型行为，且高对比度的概念可以对照语义超类结构评估，以判断其影响的是细粒度区分还是广泛类别证据。

Conclusion: 对比式概念重要性提供了一种更自然的对比归因框架，能有效揭示类别对区分中特有的概念级模型行为，弥补了传统非对比式概念归因的不足。

Abstract: Concept-based explanations are a prevalent way to explain the decisions of complex black-box methods through semantically meaningful, human-interpretable concepts. To attribute the contribution of such concepts to a model's decisions, feature attribution methods are used to quantify how strongly each concept contributes to a model output. These attributions are typically computed for a single output class and therefore answer a non-contrastive "why P?" question. In many situations, however, such as cases of misclassification, class confusion, and low-margin predictions, the more natural question to ask is "why P rather than Q?".
  We introduce contrastive concept importance (CCI), which attributes the logit margin between a target class and a contrast, or foil, class to concepts in an automatically extracted visual concept basis. The resulting scores are signed, indicating whether a concept supports the target over the foil or the foil over the target, and can be decomposed into target-logit and foil-logit effects. This makes it possible to distinguish globally important concepts from concepts that specifically influence a class-pair distinction, including whether their effect is shared, one-sided, or directly contrastive.
  We evaluate the method on ImageNet class pairs using CRAFT-style concept bases, insertion and deletion curves, logit-wise decomposition analysis, and semantic class hierarchy. The results show that contrastive concept importance reveals class-pair-specific model behavior that is not captured by ordinary concept importance alone, and that highly contrastive concepts can be evaluated against semantic superclass structure to assess whether they affect fine-grained distinctions rather than broad category evidence.

</details>


### [73] [Class-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Counterfactual Explanation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27905)
*Muhammad Adil Saleem,Syed Ali Raza,Mary-Anne Williams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在强化学习（RL）生成反事实解释（CFE）时，将实例的预测类别信息纳入RL状态表示，与仅使用预测器特征的类盲RL方法进行对比。实验表明，类感知RL在收敛速度、奖励优化、回合长度缩减和CFE有效性方面均显著优于类盲RL，且类别特征在动作选择中具有高影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究在RL生成CFE时，状态表示仅由监督数据集中的预测器特征构成，忽略了实例的预测类别信息。作者假设加入类别信息能提升探索效率和策略最优性，因此提出类感知RL并验证其优势。

Method: 提出类感知RL方法，在RL状态表示中同时包含预测器特征和实例的预测类别；与排除类别信息的类盲RL进行对比，在7个不同领域、不同规模的数据集上评估；使用SHAP和LIME分析各特征在RL动作选择中的影响力。

Result: 类感知RL在训练中表现出更快的收敛速度、更优的奖励优化和更短的回合长度；生成的CFE有效性显著高于类盲RL；SHAP和LIME分析显示类别特征始终是RL动作选择中最具影响力的预测因子之一。

Conclusion: 将实例的类别信息纳入RL状态表示能够显著提升CFE生成的清晰度、学习速度、有效性和整体效果，类别感知在RL生成CFE任务中具有重要意义。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CFEs) enhance the interpretability of black-box models by generating alternative instances with adjusted feature values that achieve a contrastive outcome. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising approach for CFE generation, enabling efficient exploration of counterfactual instances while ensuring control over key metrics like validity, sparsity, and proximity. Previous studies have formulated RL states exclusively using features derived from the predictors in the supervised dataset. This study explores the impact of including an instance's predicted class, alongside features derived from the predictors, in the RL state representation for generating CFEs. The hypothesis is that class-awareness enhances exploration efficiency and improves policy optimality. We compare the proposed class-aware RL method with the class-blind RL method, which is similar but excludes the instance's class information from the state representation. The comparison was conducted using seven datasets from diverse domains, varying in size. The results show that during training, class-aware RL offers benefits in terms of convergence speed, reward optimization, and episode length reduction. Moreover, it generates significantly more valid CFEs compared to class-blind RL. Finally, the instance's class-based feature consistently ranks among the most influential predictors in RL's action-selection, as shown by the SHAP and LIME values, underscoring the significance of class-awareness in RL for CFE generation. The impact is heightened clarity, faster learning, improved validity, and more effective counterfactual generation across diverse datasets.

</details>


### [74] [S-CEReBrO: Breaking the Memory Barrier in Continuous EEG Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27913)
*Glenn Anta Bucagu,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Yawei Li,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-CEReBrO是CEReBrO架构的流式演进版本，通过新颖的窗口交替注意力机制将注意力计算分解为固定大小的时空窗口，保证KV缓存内存恒定，解决了Transformer在连续EEG监测中因全局注意力导致的内存溢出问题。该模型在超过25,000小时、12,000+受试者的数据上预训练，在11个下游任务中7个达到SOTA，参数减少多达60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构的全局注意力机制将注意力内存状态与信号时长耦合，导致在连续脑电（EEG）监测场景中发生内存溢出，阻碍了基础模型在实时、流式神经信号分析中的实际应用。

Method: 提出窗口交替注意力机制（Windowed Alternating Attention），将注意力计算分解为固定大小的时空窗口，仅活动窗口需要驻留注意力图，从而保证KV缓存内存恒定。S-CEReBrO在超过25,000小时、12,000+受试者的EEG数据上进行预训练。

Result: 经验扩展分析表明，窗口交替注意力可处理比全自注意力长100倍、比低秩线性注意力长3倍的信号；在长上下文场景下，相比低秩线性注意力仅需55%的内存，推理吞吐量提升2.1倍。在11个下游任务中7个达到SOTA，参数最多减少60%。

Conclusion: 这项工作显著推动了高效、可泛化的连续EEG监测的实现，为流式神经信号分析提供了兼具内存效率和模型性能的可行方案。

Abstract: Foundation models offer a promising paradigm for Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis, leveraging generalizable representations from vast unlabeled datasets. Yet, Transformer-based architectures face a critical bottleneck: global attention mechanisms couple the attention memory state to the signal duration, causing memory overflow during continuous monitoring. To address this, we introduce S-CEReBrO (Streaming CEReBrO), an evolution of the CEReBrO architecture designed for continuous monitoring. Our novel Windowed Alternating Attention mechanism factorizes attention computation into fixed-size spatiotemporal windows, guaranteeing constant KV cache memory as only the active window requires resident attention maps. Empirical scaling analysis confirms that windowed alternating attention can process signals 100X longer than full self-attention and 3X longer than low-rank linear attention. Compared to low-rank linear attention on long contexts, windowed alternating attention requires 55% of the memory while increasing inference throughput by 2.1X. Pre-trained on >25,000 hours of recordings from >12,000 subjects, S-CEReBrO achieves state-of-the-art performance on 7 of 11 downstream tasks, with up to 60% fewer parameters. This work represents a significant step toward the realization of efficient, generalizable, and continuous EEG monitoring. An accompanying code repository is available.

</details>


### [75] [Exact Action Values Are Not Enough: Rollout-Verified Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of a Reasoning Model for Multi-Zone VAV Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27914)
*Takumi Shioda,Kohei Terashima,Tatsuo Nagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多区域变风量（VAV）空调控制问题，比较了GPT-5等前沿推理模型与TD3引导的强化微调（RFT）方法的控制性能。结果显示GPT-5无需建筑特定训练即可将HVAC用电降低6.2%，但牺牲了通风裕量；TD3虽降低用电4.5%并改善舒适度合规，但审计发现其评论家模型在状态的排序可靠性上存在缺陷（即使跨时间相关性r=0.9998），200步RFT训练未见持续性收益，且开放权重模型预测能力不如持续性基线。研究建议在价值型RFT之前先进行以转换预测为目标的监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多区域变风量控制需要平衡热舒适、室内空气质量和用电量，模型预测控制和强化学习虽被广泛研究，但部署通常需要建筑特定建模或训练，限制了可扩展性。作者旨在探索无需建筑特定训练的前沿推理模型能否实现有竞争力的VAV控制，并进一步测试能否将控制知识转移到本地可部署的开放权重模型中。

Method: 首先测试前沿推理模型（LLM）能否仅基于文本实现竞争性VAV控制；随后测试TD3引导的强化微调（RFT）能否将控制知识转移到开放权重模型。在物理仿真四区域仿真器中评估五种控制器，跨越三个夏季日。RFT采用确定性回放：恢复保存状态、应用一个候选动作、跟随TD3为每个动作评分。同时使用回放验证器审计学习的评论家模型。

Result: 相对于Guideline 36基线，TD3降低HVAC用电4.5%并改善温度和CO2合规；GPT-5无需建筑特定训练实现最大降幅6.2%但降低通风裕量。审计发现评论家的跨时间相关性r=0.9998掩盖了其状态内排序不可靠的问题——在10个状态中仅5个选择了回放最优候选。即使使用回放验证器，200步RFT训练未产生持续的样本动作收益提升；开放权重控制器训练前后用电量均高于基线，五分钟预测劣于持续性基线，而GPT-5的转换预测显著更优。

Conclusion: 精确的回放分数虽然能为采样动作排序，但未能揭示下一状态影响或改进方向。转换误差的不变性表明，在价值型RFT之前应优先进行面向转换预测的监督微调（SFT），以提升开放权重模型的预测能力，从而为后续强化微调奠定更可靠的基矗础。

Abstract: Multi-zone variable-air-volume control must balance thermal comfort, indoor air quality, and electricity use across several continuous actuators. Model predictive control and reinforcement learning are widely studied, but deployment typically requires building-specific modeling or training, limiting scalability. We first test whether a frontier reasoning model (an LLM trained to use additional inference-time computation) can achieve competitive VAV control from text without building-specific training. With that capability established, we then test whether TD3-guided reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) can transfer control knowledge into a locally deployable open-weight model. Five controllers are evaluated over three summer days in a physics-based four-zone emulator. Relative to a Guideline 36-based baseline, TD3 reduced HVAC electricity by 4.5% while improving temperature and CO$_2$ compliance. Without building-specific training, GPT-5 achieved the largest reduction (6.2%) but reduced the ventilation margin. For RFT, deterministic rollouts restore a saved state, apply one candidate, and follow TD3 to score each action. Auditing a learned critic against these rollouts exposed a failure hidden by its near-perfect across-time correlation ($r=0.9998$): within-state ranking was unreliable; the critic selected the rollout-best candidate in only 5 of 10 states. Even with the rollout verifier, 200 RFT steps produced no sustained improvement in sampled-action return; the open-weight controller used more electricity than the baseline before and after training, and its five-minute predictions remained worse than persistence. GPT-5 predicted transitions far better. Exact rollout scores rank sampled actions but reveal neither next-state effects nor an improvement direction. The unchanged transition errors motivate transition-focused supervised fine-tuning before value-based RFT.

</details>


### [76] [ODEWorld: A Continuous Predictive Architecture via Physical-Time Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27924)
*Dongxiu Liu,Haoyi Niu,Peng Cheng,Yuan Gao,Xirui Kang,Sangli Teng,Koushil Sreenath,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理时间流（PT-Flow），学习一个在物理时间中运行的连续潜变量速度场，其底层动力学由嵌入在结构化表示空间中的常微分方程（ODE）参数化。在此基础上构建了ODEWorld——一个连续时间潜空间世界模型，支持任意时间分辨率预测、反向预测，并解决潜世界模型中的表示坍缩问题，在视频生成和机器人控制任务上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界中的空间和时间本质上是连续的，但现有机器学习世界建模范式大多局限于离散时间预测，在捕捉物理世界动力学方面效率低下。这一根本性的不匹配促使作者探索连续时间的世界模型。

Method: PT-Flow学习连续的潜空间速度场，将序列数据的底层动力学参数化为嵌入在结构化表示空间中的ODE。预测未来被转化为通过ODE求解器在压缩潜空间中执行时间积分。ODEWorld在此基础上提取时变特征，并在动力学表示空间和潜速度场上强制施加ODE性质，以缓解表示坍缩问题。

Result: ODEWorld在视频生成和机器人控制任务上均表现优异，成功调和了利于规划的动力学抽象与视觉真实感之间的矛盾。连续特性使其支持任意时间分辨率、长时程预测后的高质量图像重建以及反向预测，这些都是离散时间模型无法实现的。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了连续时间世界模型的可行性和优越性，它不仅能提供丰富的信息以促进下游策略学习，还能在长期预测后保持高质量重建，为世界模型的进一步发展开辟了新方向。

Abstract: In the physical world we inhabit, space and time are fundamentally continuous. However, existing machine learning paradigms for world modeling are largely confined to discrete-time prediction, thereby exhibiting significant inefficiency in capturing the dynamics of physical world. We introduce Physical-Time Flow (\textbf{PT-Flow}), a novel approach that learns a continuous latent velocity field operating in physical time. Crucially, the underlying dynamics of sequential data are parameterized by an ordinary differential equation (ODE) embedded in a well-structured representation space. Under this paradigm, the prediction of future can be recast as temporal integration via an ODE solver in the compressed latent space. Building upon PT-Flow, we construct \textbf{ODEWorld}, a continuous-time latent world model that is both efficient and versatile. By extracting time-variant features and enforcing ODE properties on both the dynamical representation space and the latent velocity field, ODEWorld effectively addresses the long-standing representation collapse issue in latent world model literature. This also enables high-quality image reconstruction even after long-horizon prediction. Moreover, its continuous nature allows for arbitrary temporal resolution and even backward prediction, which is impossible for most discrete-time models. Lastly, ODEWorld can provide rich planning-oriented information to facilitate downstream policy learning. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that ODEWorld successfully reconciles planning-conducive dynamics abstraction with visual realism, excelling in both video generation and robotic control. \href{https://dstate.github.io/odeworld_website/}{Project Website}.

</details>


### [77] [Harnessing the Potential of Optimizing Data Mixtures via Bayesian Domain Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27928)
*Xiang Yuan,Kaiqing Lei,Zhenyu Jin,Jun Shu,Deyu Meng,Zongben Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯推断的域加权方法，通过引入从观测中学习的Gamma先验信息，从Dirichlet分布中推断域权重，实现稳定、高效且数据高效的域混合优化，优于现有的启发式方法和函数拟合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练数据的多域分布组成从根本上影响模型性能。早期依赖人工启发式的方法无法捕捉域间复杂协同效应；现有的基于代理函数拟合的方法依赖强结构假设（如秩不变性或缩放定律），这些假设常常被违背，导致不可忽略的估计偏差；而直接优化加权方案的方法虽然前景广阔，但存在优化轨迹不稳定和计算开销过高的问题。因此需要一种既稳定高效又能避免强假设的域权重学习方法。

Method: 提出一种贝叶斯域加权方法（Bayesian Domain Weighting），将域权重从Dirichlet分布中推断，并通过引入从观测数据中学习的Gamma先验信息来对推断过程进行约束和引导。该方法结合了贝叶斯推断的稳定性与数据驱动学习的效率，避免了函数拟合方法所需的强结构假设。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够实现稳定且高效的域权重学习，可以识别最优的数据混合方案，同时相比基于搜索的函数拟合方法消耗显著更少的数据。该方法使得基于优化的域加权方法在大规模应用中重新焕发活力。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯域加权方法通过利用Gamma先验和Dirichlet分布，成功解决了现有域权重学习方法中存在的估计偏差、优化不稳定和计算开销高等问题，实现了大规模预训练数据混合的高效优化，为LLM多域数据配置提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: The performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) is fundamentally influenced by the distributional composition of multi-domain pre-training data. While manual heuristics were prevalent in early models, they increasingly fail to capture the intricate synergies between domains as data complexity grows. To overcome the issue, a dominant approach seeks to fit a proxy function mapping between domain weights and their corresponding validation losses, and then find the optimal domain weights to minimize validation losses. These methods rely on strong structural assumptions, such as rank invariance or scaling laws, which are often violated, resulting in non-negligible estimation bias. A promising approach is to directly optimize the weighting scheme from data. However, it suffers from unstable optimization trajectory and prohibitive computational overhead, limiting its potential to search better domain weights configurations. This paper presents a Bayesian domain weighting method to infer the weights from a Dirichlet distribution via introducing Gamma prior information learned from observations. Experimental results demonstrate that proposed method could achieve stable and efficient domain weights learning, and identifies optimal mixtures while consuming substantially less data than search-based function-fitting methods, revitalizing optimization-based domain weighting for large-scale applications.

</details>


### [78] [TriShield: Zero-Utility-Loss Defense Against Privacy Backdoors in Federated Language Model Fine-Tuning via Orthogonal Gradient Projection and Optimizer State Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27940)
*Cheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriShield是一个三层确定性防御框架，通过参数构件检测、有状态虚拟迭代和零效用正交投影，在零模型效用损失且不增加通信轮次的情况下，完全阻止NeuroImprint风格的联邦学习隐私后门攻击，将重建率降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调LLM虽能协作训练但不暴露原始数据，然而NeuroImprint攻击表明恶意参数服务器可通过将PEFT适配器污染为隐私后门，用专用记忆神经元以高语义保真度重建59%-79%的客户端训练数据。现有防御（如LDP和梯度裁剪）要么无法对抗该攻击，要么导致不可接受的效用退化，亟需一种在不牺牲模型性能的前提下彻底防护该攻击的防御方案。

Method: TriShield由三层组成：(1) 参数构件检测器（Parameter Artifact Detector），在本地训练开始前识别分布式模型参数中的记忆神经元签名；(2) 有状态虚拟迭代（Stateful Virtual Iteration），强制Adam/AdamW的动量状态在虚拟步骤间不可逆地纠缠梯度，使NeuroImprint的闭式反演失效；(3) 零效用正交投影算子（Zero-Utility Orthogonal Projection），通过SVD将梯度投影到主任务语义子空间，物理消除携带隐私记忆的梯度分量。

Result: 在GPT-2 (117M)和Llama-Guard-3-1B上的实验表明，TriShield将所有测试攻击变体的NeuroImprint重建率降至0%，同时保持或提升训练精度，额外GPU计算开销低于5%。理论证明在第2和第3层之后，上传梯度与任何个体训练样本之间的互信息为零。

Conclusion: TriShield在理论上和实验上都验证了能够以零模型效用损失、零额外通信轮次和极低计算开销的条件，完全消除NeuroImprint风格的隐私重建攻击，为联邦LLM微调提供了一种强健且实用的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) enables collaborative training without exposing raw data. However, a recent attack, NeuroImprint [1] (arXiv:2606.20553), demonstrates that a malicious parameter server can corrupt a PEFT adapter into a privacy backdoor: by assigning a dedicated memorization neuron to each training sample and ensuring each neuron updates at most once, the server can analytically reconstruct 59\%--79\% of client training data with high semantic fidelity. Existing defenses---including local differential privacy (LDP) [8] and gradient clipping---either fail against this attack or impose unacceptable utility degradation. We present \textbf{TriShield}, a three-layer deterministic defense that completely prevents NeuroImprint-style reconstruction with \textbf{zero model utility loss} and \textbf{no additional communication rounds}. TriShield consists of: (1) a \textbf{Parameter Artifact Detector} that identifies memory-neuron signatures in distributed model parameters before local training begins; (2) a \textbf{Stateful Virtual Iteration} mechanism that forces Adam/AdamW's momentum state to irreversibly entangle gradients across virtual steps, invalidating NeuroImprint's closed-form inversion; and (3) a \textbf{Zero-Utility Orthogonal Projection} operator that projects all local gradient updates onto the main-task semantic subspace computed via SVD, physically eliminating any gradient components that carry private memorization. We prove theoretically that after Layers 2 and 3, the mutual information between the uploaded gradient and any individual training sample is zero. Experiments on GPT-2 (117M) and Llama-Guard-3-1B verify that TriShield reduces NeuroImprint reconstruction rate to \textbf{0\%} across all tested attack variants, while maintaining or improving training accuracy, with less than 5\% additional GPU computation overhead.

</details>


### [79] [AutoPref: Automatic Discovery of Task-Specific Preference Objectives for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27953)
*Shengda Gu,Kai Li,Xinyi Ke,Haobo Fu,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoPref是首个由LLM引导的自动化偏好目标发现框架，用于神经组合优化（NCO）。它将偏好目标分解为逐对损失程序和集合感知加权程序，通过分阶段条件搜索策略与行为门控来高效搜索程序化目标空间，在TSP、CVRP、FFSP和JSSP等多个问题上均优于手工设计的强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好目标方法将两个独立的设计选择——从每对解中提取何种学习信号以及如何相对于采样集合对每对解加权——都手动指定为统一公式，缺乏针对特定问题的灵活性，且难以覆盖多样化的场景。需要一种自动化方法来发现更适合的偏好目标。

Method: AutoPref将偏好目标分解为两个程序：逐对损失程序（定义学习信号）和集合感知加权程序（定义每对解的相对贡献），二者组合构成统一程序化目标空间，并将现有偏好目标作为其特例。为使搜索可操作，引入分阶段条件搜索策略，配合行为门控在短时训练和评估前过滤不可行的程序。

Result: 在TSP、CVRP、FFSP和JSSP四个问题上，AutoPref在不同问题规模下均一致优于强手工设计的基线方法，证明了自动化目标发现在NCO中的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: AutoPref展示了从手工设计偏好目标转向自动化程序化目标发现的可行性，不仅提升了NCO性能，还建立了一个通用的目标发现框架，可扩展到更广泛的组合优化问题。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization problems (COPs) underpin many real-world decisions, but their exponentially large search spaces make high-quality solutions costly to obtain. Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) learns fast construction policies, typically with reinforcement learning (RL), while preference-based NCO improves sample efficiency by learning from relative solution quality. However, existing preference objectives combine two distinct design choices in manually specified, one-size-fits-all formulations: what learning signal to extract from each solution pair and how to weight each pair relative to the sampled set. We present AutoPref, the first LLM-guided framework for automated preference-objective discovery in NCO. AutoPref factorizes the objective into a pairwise loss program, which defines the learning signal, and a set-aware weighting program, which determines each pair's relative contribution. Their composition forms a unified programmatic objective space containing existing preference objectives as special cases. To make its search tractable, we introduce a staged conditional search strategy with behavioral gates that filter inadmissible programs before short-horizon training and evaluation. Across TSP, CVRP, FFSP, and JSSP, AutoPref consistently outperforms strong hand-designed baselines across problem scales, demonstrating the benefits and scalability of automated objective discovery for NCO.

</details>


### [80] [What Makes Graph Unified? Principles and Generative Sliding-Window Transformer for Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27966)
*Dongxiao He,Siqi Liu,Jitao Zhao,Yawen Li,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SliGFM，一种基于拓扑感知滑动窗口特征编码和生成式重建的图基础模型，通过四个核心原则（形式统一、跨域可迁移性、信息保留、主干兼容性）解决异构节点特征跨域统一问题，将不同维度和语义的节点特征转化为有序的固定维度特征token，实现跨图领域的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFMs）旨在实现跨领域的通用图学习，但异构节点特征在维度和语义上的显著差异构成了输入层面的根本障碍。现有方法虽将异构特征映射到固定维度空间，但维度一致并不保证特征语义的有效统一和可迁移模式的捕获，缺乏对跨域特征统一概念的清晰界定。

Method: SliGFM首先按拓扑平滑度对特征维度进行排序，然后使用共享的滑动窗口特征编码器扫描重排后的特征，将异构特征转换为有序的固定维度特征token。该框架使平滑度感知的Transformer能够捕获每个节点内特征token间可迁移的关系模式，同时通过生成式重建目标促进原始特征信息的保留，并提炼了四个跨域特征统一的期望准则：形式统一、跨域可迁移性、信息保留和主干兼容性。

Result: 论文提出并有SliGFM模型，基于拓扑感知滑动窗口编码和生成式重建实现跨域图特征统一，但目前摘要中未给出具体实验定量结果。

Conclusion: SliGFM通过拓扑平滑度排序、滑动窗口编码和生成式重建三者结合，为图基础模型的跨域特征统一提供了新的解决思路，弥补了现有方法仅追求维度一致性而忽视语义信息保留和可迁移性的不足。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose graph learning, aiming to learn reusable knowledge that generalizes across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks, reducing the need for specific model development. Achieving this goal requires reconciling the substantial heterogeneity in node features, graph structures, and semantic information across domains. Among them, heterogeneous node features constitute a fundamental input-level barrier, as their dimensionality and semantics vary substantially across datasets. Existing studies typically project or map heterogeneous node features into a fixed-dimensional space, often implicitly equating dimensional uniformity with effective feature unification. Yet dimensional consistency alone does not ensure that the unified features preserve informative semantics and capture transferable patterns that can support cross-domain knowledge transfer. To bridge this conceptual gap, we distill four desiderata for cross-domain graph feature unification: formal uniformity, cross-domain transferability, information preservation, and backbone compatibility. Guided by these principles, we propose SliGFM, a graph foundation model built upon topology-aware sliding-window feature encoding and generative reconstruction. SliGFM orders feature dimensions by topological smoothness and scans the reordered features with a shared sliding-window feature encoder, transforming heterogeneous features into a common space of ordered fixed-dimensional feature tokens. This formulation enables a smoothness-aware transformer to capture transferable relational patterns among feature tokens within each node, while the generative reconstruction objective encourages preservation of the original feature information.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Binary Rewards: A Comparative Study of Reward Design for Reinforcement Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27968)
*Efstratios Zaradoukas,Davide Gabrielli,Bardh Prenkaj,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器遗忘任务中奖励设计对遗忘效率的影响。作者提出了一个奖励分解框架，将可验证性与稀疏性解耦，并设计了两种新奖励函数（指数奖励和PageRank启发式奖励），在RWKU基准上验证了它们比传统二值奖励收敛快3倍且保持模型通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘在隐私法规（如GDPR和欧盟AI法案）背景下日益重要，但现有基于RLVR的遗忘方法依赖稀疏二值奖励，只提供最小学习信号（仅指示是否避免禁区内容），导致收敛速度受限。奖励设计如何影响遗忘效率这一关键问题尚未被系统地研究。

Method: 提出一个原则性的奖励分解框架，将可验证性与稀疏性解耦；在此基础上提出两种新奖励函数：(1) 指数奖励，根据禁止概念出现次数提供梯度惩罚；(2) PageRank启发式奖励，根据语义重要性对惩罚进行加权。实验在Real World Knowledge Unlearning (RWKU)基准上进行。

Result: 两种新奖励在RWKU基准上持续优于二值奖励设置，在达到相似遗忘性能的前提下收敛速度快达3倍，同时保持模型通用效用。

Conclusion: 奖励设计是驱动机器遗忘效率的关键因素，为可扩展和高效的机器遗忘提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to selectively remove specific knowledge from trained language models without full retraining, a growing necessity under privacy regulations such as GDPR and the EU AI Act. Recent work has reformulated unlearning as a Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) problem, where models are optimized against verifiable rewards computed directly from their outputs. However, existing methods rely on sparse binary rewards that provide minimal learning signal, indicating only whether forbidden content was avoided, and limiting convergence speed. In this paper, we study how reward design affects unlearning efficiency within the Reinforcement Unlearning (RUL) framework. We introduce a principled reward decomposition framework that decouples verifiability from sparsity, and propose two new reward functions: an exponential reward that provides graded penalties based on the count of forbidden-concept occurrences, and a PageRank inspired reward that weights penalties by semantic importance. We conduct experiments on the Real World Knowledge Unlearning (RWKU) benchmark, demonstrating that both rewards consistently outperform the binary setting, while reaching similar forgetting performance up to $3\times$ faster and preserving general model utility. Our results show that reward design is a key driver of unlearning efficiency offering a practical path toward scalable and efficient machine unlearning.

</details>


### [82] [TAPO: Transition-Aware Policy Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27973)
*Cong Li,Peixi Peng,Yisen Zhao,Xinyu Hu,Shudong Liu,Zhan Su,Zhuojian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAPO（Transition-Aware Policy Optimization）是一个面向LLM智能体的统一训练框架，在标准策略优化之外，利用在线交互中产生的动作-条件"下一观测预测"监督信号对共享骨干模型进行训练，从而增强模型对环境转移动态和动作后果的敏感性，作为即插即用的轻量增强模块，在WebShop和ALFWorld上显著提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的强化学习后训练主要依赖稀疏的任务奖励进行策略优化，未能充分利用在线交互中天然存在的一类密集监督信号——动作执行后的环境反馈。理论研究表明，多步目标导向任务的泛化依赖于对环境后果的预测性知识，因此需要一种能同时利用稀疏奖励和密集环境反馈的优化方法。

Method: TAPO采用策略优化与转移监督交替进行的统一训练框架。在标准RL更新之外，将rollout数据重用，在共享骨干模型上施加动作条件的"下一观测预测"监督（action-conditioned next-observation prediction supervision），使模型在优化策略的同时增强对环境转移动态和动作后果的敏感性。该方法无需额外专家数据、额外采样成本或推理开销，可作为现有智能体RL算法的即插即用增强模块。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld基准上，集成不同规模的基础模型（foundation models）和不同策略优化算法进行系统实验，结果表明TAPO相比纯策略优化基线在任务性能上一致性地取得提升。

Conclusion: TAPO证明了通过利用在线交互中天然存在的密集环境反馈进行转移监督，可以有效增强LLM智能体的推理智能和策略优化效果，是一种计算上轻量、无需额外数据或采样成本的通用增强方法，适用于多种规模的基础模型和不同的策略优化算法。

Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for the post-training of Large Language Model (LLM) agents. However, existing methods predominantly rely on sparse task rewards for policy optimization, failing to fully exploit another class of inherently dense supervisory signals naturally present during online interaction: environmental feedback following action execution. Recent theoretical studies suggest that generalization in multi-step, goal-oriented tasks hinges on predictive knowledge of environmental consequences. Inspired by this, we propose TAPO: Transition-Aware Policy Optimization for LLM Agents, a unified training framework that alternates between policy optimization and transition supervision. Beyond standard RL updates, TAPO repurposes rollout data to apply action-conditioned next-observation prediction supervision on a shared backbone model. This approach enhances the model's sensitivity to environmental transition dynamics and action consequences while concurrently optimizing the policy. It serves as a computationally lightweight, plug-and-play enhancement module for existing agent RL algorithms, requiring no additional expert data, extra sampling costs, or inference-time overhead. We conduct systematic experiments on WebShop and ALFWorld, integrating foundation models of various scales with different policy optimization algorithms. Empirical results demonstrate that TAPO consistently improves task performance over pure policy optimization baselines.

</details>


### [83] [It's All Just Vectorization: einx, a Universal Notation for Tensor Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27987)
*Florian Fervers,Sebastian Bullinger,Christoph Bodensteiner,Michael Arens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 einx，一种基于向量化（vectorization）概念的通用张量运算表示法，将现有张量框架庞大的 API 简化为少量基本操作，并提供一致的规则，使张量编程更加可读、可写且不易出错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量框架（如 NumPy）的表示法难以阅读和编写，容易产生 shape 错误；einsum 和 einops 等替代方案虽受欢迎，但仅支持少数操作，缺乏通用性，无法作为张量编程的普适模型。

Method: 将向量化重新视为一种用于变换张量操作的函数，利用它既可以将低阶运算提升为高阶运算，也可以将高阶运算概念性地分解为低阶运算及其向量化。在此基础上构建 einx——一种声明式、点式（pointful）的通用张量运算表示法，其定义与循环表示法类比，并作为张量运算的向量化形式。该表示法嵌入 Python，与现有张量框架无缝集成。

Result: einx 将现有框架的大型 API 缩减为少量基本操作，对所有操作应用一致的规则，并在代码中提供了清晰、可读、可写的表示。实现了 Python 内嵌版本，开源地址为 https://github.com/fferflo/einx。

Conclusion: einx 利用向量化的普适性提供了一种通用、简洁、一致且可读性强的张量运算表示法，有望成为超越现有 NumPy 风格和 einsum/einops 局限性的下一代张量编程范式。

Abstract: Tensor operations represent a cornerstone of modern scientific computing. However, the Numpy-like notation adopted by predominant tensor frameworks is often difficult to read and write and prone to so-called shape errors, i.a., due to following inconsistent rules across a large, complex collection of operations. Alternatives like einsum and einops have gained popularity, but are inherently restricted to few operations and lack the generality required for a universal model of tensor programming.
  To derive a better paradigm, we revisit vectorization as a function for transforming tensor operations, and use it to both lift lower-order operations to higher-order operations, and conceptually decompose higher-order operations to lower-order operations and their vectorization.
  Building on the universal nature of vectorization, we introduce einx, a universal notation for tensor operations. It uses declarative, pointful expressions that are defined by analogy with loop notation and represent the vectorization of tensor operations. The notation reduces the large APIs of existing frameworks to a small set of elementary operations, applies consistent rules across all operations, and enables a clean, readable and writable representation in code. We provide an implementation of einx that is embedded in Python and integrates seamlessly with existing tensor frameworks: https://github.com/fferflo/einx

</details>


### [84] [Building a User Foundation Model for the Open Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28019)
*Solal Vernier,Ivan Can Arisoy,Merwan Barlier,Blaž Škrlj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向开放网络实时竞价（RTB）场景的用户基础模型，该场景下用户身份碎片化、非持久且历史数据稀疏。作者采用掩码语言建模和序列级对比学习对Transformer编码器进行预训练，并在点击预测任务上进行微调，同时引入LLM-as-optimizer范式优化预训练流水线。该模型在工业生产的竞价胜率和CTR排序器上分别带来+1.197%和+1.354%的RIG提升，7天线上A/B测试确认CTR提升+2.13%、eCPC降低-1.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户基础模型大多假设用户身份稳定持久，但开放网络RTB场景中用户身份碎片化、非持久，浏览历史受隐私选择制约，导致大量流量无历史数据、可用记录为短而不连续的会话。传统方法将历史信号表示为聚合计数器和时效桶，未充分利用序列结构，因此需要一种能在该场景下有效建模用户行为序列的用户基础模型。

Method: 预训练阶段使用Transformer编码器，采用掩码语言建模和序列级对比学习两种自监督目标；随后在点击预测任务上进行微调。预训练流水线通过LLM-in-the-loop搜索（LLM-as-optimizer）从可审查的代码级编辑目录中优化，在工业环境中实例化该优化范式。编码器表示同时服务生产环境的竞价胜率模型和CTR排序器两个下游任务。

Result: 同一编码器表示在生产的竞价胜率模型上带来+1.197%的RIG提升，在生产CTR排序器上带来+1.354%的RIG提升；为期7天的线上A/B测试确认CTR提升+2.13%、eCPC降低-1.13%，两项指标的80%置信区间均不包含零。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在用户身份碎片化、历史数据稀疏的开放网络RTB场景中，通过自监督学习用户浏览历史序列来构建用户基础模型是可行且有效的，同时验证了LLM-as-optimizer范式在工业预训练流水线优化中的实用性。

Abstract: User foundation models have demonstrated strong results in e-commerce and social recommendation, but most industrial deployments assume environments where user identity is stable and persistent. Open-web real-time bidding (RTB) operates on a structurally different data distribution: user identity is fragmented and non-persistent across browsing sessions, and the availability of browsing history depends on user privacy choices. Consequently, a significant portion of traffic carries no historical data, and available records often consist of relatively short, disjointed sessions. As a result, historical signals in this domain are typically represented as aggregated counters and recency buckets, leaving the sequential structure unexploited. To address this limitation, we present a user foundation model that applies self-supervised learning on user browsing histories and show that the learned representation improves multiple downstream production tasks, demonstrating the viability of this approach on the open web. We pre-train a Transformer encoder with masked language modeling and a sequence-level contrastive objective, then fine-tune it on the click prediction task. We optimize the encoder's pre-training pipeline with an LLM-in-the-loop search over a curated catalog of reviewable, code-level edits (lifters), instantiating the LLM-as-optimizer paradigm in an industrial setting. The same encoder representation yields +1.197% RIG on the production bid win-rate model and +1.354% RIG on the production CTR ranker; a 7-day live A/B test confirms +2.13% CTR, -1.13% eCPC (80% CI excluding zero on both metrics).

</details>


### [85] [Flux-OPD: On-Policy Distillation with Evolving Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28022)
*Yuran Wang,Zekun Wang,Bohan Zeng,Ruixu Zhang,Wenxuan Liu,Liu Yang,Yifan Dai,Yang Shi,Bozhou Li,Chengzhuo Tong,Daili Hua,Yuanxing Zhang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flux-OPD，一种在线偏好蒸馏（OPD）范式，利用随时间演化的上下文作为训练监督信号来捕捉开放领域大语言模型训练中的任务偏好，并通过逆向KL目标分解发现学生被蒸馏到上下文条件教师的几何均值，且目标中存在冲突项，据此将上下文差异信号注入上下文无关教师锚点，并用冲突项加权校正强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放领域的大语言模型训练缺乏可验证的奖励信号，任务偏好难以形式化为有效监督。虽然上下文可以传达偏好，但一旦蒸馏到学生中便不再提供额外监督，因此需要能够随学生表现演化的上下文。然而直接使用演化上下文作为训练监督会导致蒸馏目标不稳定和分布冲突，需要机制来稳定目标并降低冲突。

Method: 首先通过逆向KL目标的分解分析上下文的效应，发现两个结论：(1) 学生被蒸馏向上下文条件教师的几何均值；(2) 目标中包含一个衡量教师间冲突的冲突项。基于此分解，提出Flux-OPD：将上下文条件教师与上下文无关教师之间的差异视为"上下文差异信号"，将其作为上下文校正注入上下文无关教师锚点，并使用冲突项作为指示器来加权校正强度。

Result: 在开放领域任务上的实验表明，Flux-OPD优于现有的OPD范式，展示了将教师监督与演化上下文相结合的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过理论分解揭示了演化上下文作为训练监督的内在机制，提出的Flux-OPD有效解决了不稳定蒸馏目标与冲突分布的问题，为开放领域任务偏好捕捉提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large language model training in open-ended domains lacks verifiable rewards, making task preferences difficult to formalize as effective supervision. Contexts can convey such preferences, yet provide little additional supervision once distilled into the student, motivating contexts that evolve with student performance. However, directly using evolving contexts as in-training supervision results in an unstable distillation target and conflicting distributions, requiring mechanisms to stabilize target and downweight conflicts. In this paper, we analyze the effect of contexts through a decomposition of the reverse KL objective, revealing two findings: the student is distilled toward the geometric mean of context-conditioned teachers, and the objective contains a conflict term that measures conflicts among these teachers. Based on this decomposition, we propose Flux-OPD, an OPD paradigm that uses evolving contexts as in-training supervision to capture task preferences in open-ended domains. Flux-OPD treats the differences between context-conditioned and context-free teachers as contextual difference signals, injects them as contextual corrections into the context-free teacher anchor, and weights their correction strength using the conflict term as an indicator. Experiments on open-ended tasks show that Flux-OPD outperforms existing OPD paradigms, highlighting the potential to combine teacher supervision with evolving contexts.

</details>


### [86] [Contrastive Reinforced Policy Optimization via Privileged Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28026)
*Xingjian Wu,Junlin Liu,Xingchen Liu,Xuhang Zhu,Jianing Wang,Linsen Guo,Xiaoyu Li,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比强化策略优化（CRPO），从对比学习视角重新审视智能体场景下的在线自蒸馏（OPSD），通过预测熵区分正位置（反思性探索）和负位置（曝光偏差），进行组间对比以保留可靠、细粒度的优化信号，在13个推理和深度搜索基准上优于现有RL和自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练大语言模型常依赖RLVR或OPSD。OPSD提供稠密、logit级别的监督，但因自教师特权信息存在固有权衡偏差（exposure bias），在多轮智能体场景中会导致推理路径收敛以及优化方向不明确的问题，亟需新的方法解决。

Method: CRPO将智能体OPSD从对比学习视角重新表述：利用预测熵区分正位置（反思性探索）与负位置（曝光偏差），并对样本进行组间对比（group-wise contrast），以保留可靠的细粒度优化信号，同时规避OPSD的暴露偏差问题。

Result: 在13个具有挑战性的推理与深度搜索基准上，CRPO一致性地优于现有强化学习与自蒸馏基线，显著提升了长程交互中的训练稳定性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: CRPO通过对比学习视角重构智能体自蒸馏训练范式，有效克服了曝光偏差带来的优化退化问题，为多轮智能体场景下的LLM后训练提供了更鲁棒和可泛化的优化方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly rely on Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) or On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD). While OPSD provides dense, logit-level supervision, it inherently suffers from exposure bias due to the privileged information of the self-teacher. In multi-turn agentic settings, this leads to reasoning route convergence and the loss of clear optimization directions. To tackle these challenges, we introduce Contrastive Reinforced Policy Optimization (CRPO), which reformulates agentic OPSD from a contrastive learning perspective. By leveraging predictive entropy to distinguish between positive positions (reflective exploration) and negative positions (exposure bias), CRPO conducts group-wise contrast to preserve reliable, fine-grained optimization signals. Extensive evaluations across 13 challenging reasoning and deep-search benchmarks demonstrate that CRPO consistently outperforms existing reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines, significantly enhancing training stability and generalization in long-horizon interactions.

</details>


### [87] [Enhancing Irregular Time Series Forecasting with Continuous-Time Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28035)
*Tianen Shen,Zhengyu Li,Yutong Li,Xiangfei Qiu,Xingjian Wu,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WrapFlow是一个面向不规则多元时间序列预测的连续时间建模框架，通过连续时间Token化直接编码原始观测事件并用gap-aware token建模长未观测区间，配合基于残差流匹配的无模拟训练范式，在多个真实数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不规则时间序列处理方法存在两难：基于离散化的方法（插值、填补、聚合）会破坏连续时间语义；基于ODE的连续时间方法需要专门架构且因数值求解器带来巨大计算开销。本文旨在克服这两种范式的缺陷，实现兼顾连续时间建模能力与计算效率的预测框架。

Method: WrapFlow包含两部分核心创新：（1）输入侧——连续时间Token化，直接编码原始观测事件，并通过gap-aware tokens显式建模长未观测区间，生成连续时间token后由标准Transformer骨干处理以捕获长程时间依赖；（2）输出侧——基于残差流匹配（Residual Flow Matching）的无模拟训练范式，在基础预测周围学习条件残差向量场，训练阶段避免数值求解器模拟和反向传播，推理阶段仅需少量固定 rollout 步骤实现高质量连续预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，WrapFlow在预测性能上达到了当前最优（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: WrapFlow验证了将连续时间Token化与流匹配相结合的可行性，在保持连续时间语义建模能力的同时，通过无模拟训练和少量推理步骤有效降低了计算开销，为不规则多元时间序列建模提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series are widely encountered in applications such as healthcare monitoring, human activity recognition, and environmental sensing. Their core challenges stem from asynchronous observations, non-uniform sampling intervals, and the fact that temporal patterns themselves carry critical dynamic information. Existing approaches either rely on discretization-based preprocessing (e.g., interpolation, imputation, or aggregation), which disrupts the underlying continuous-time semantics, or adopt continuous-time modeling via ODE-based frameworks, which typically require specialized architectures and incur substantial computational overhead due to numerical solvers. To address these limitations, we propose WrapFlow, a continuous-time modeling framework for irregular time series forecasting. On the input side, WrapFlow introduces Continuous-Time Tokenization, which directly encodes raw observation events and explicitly models long unobserved intervals via gap-aware tokens. The resulting continuous-time tokens are then processed by a standard Transformer backbone to capture long-range temporal dependencies. On the output side, we develop a simulation-free training paradigm for Residual Flow Matching, which learns conditional residual vector fields around base predictions while avoiding numerical-solver simulation and backpropagation during training. This design enables high-quality continuous forecasting using only a small number of fixed rollout steps at inference. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that WrapFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [88] [ClawTrack: Towards Trace-Level Evaluation and Improvement of Real-World Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28037)
*Xingjian Wu,Xuhang Zhu,Xingchen Liu,Junlin Liu,Jianing Wang,Linsen Guo,Xiaoyu Li,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClawTrack，一个双评估基准，同时衡量智能体"做什么"（任务得分）和"怎么做"（过程得分）。它包含320个任务、8个领域、25+确定性模拟服务，通过12,541条rubric对每个推理回合从目标对齐、效率、信息利用和结果验证四个维度打分。评估21个模型、16,000+次试验后发现：过程得分能有效归因成功/失败原因，过滤仅靠运气成功的案例；四个维度互补，结果验证是系统性瓶颈；框架对评估器选择稳健；基于过程的轨迹过滤在各模型规模上带来一致的训练后提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准仅评估最终结果，无法区分"真推理"与"侥幸成功"，也难以将失败归因到具体过程缺陷，尤其在长时程任务上存在严重的评价缺口。

Method: 提出双评估框架ClawTrack：(1) Task Score衡量任务达成情况；(2) Process Score通过Process Grader从目标对齐、效率、信息利用、结果验证四个维度对每个推理回合评分，基于12,541条任务特定的rubric条目，覆盖320个任务、8个领域，配备25+确定性模拟服务。

Result: 评估21个模型、16,000+试验发现：(1) 过程得分可有效归因成败，过滤仅凭结果无法识别的"侥幸通过"；(2) 四个评估维度互补，结果验证是系统性瓶颈；(3) 框架对不同评判LLM具有稳健性；(4) 基于过程的轨迹过滤在多个模型规模上带来一致的训练后改进。

Conclusion: ClawTrack填补了LLM智能体过程级评估的空白，证明过程评分不仅可解释失败原因，还能作为数据筛选手段提升模型性能，为长时程智能体任务的细粒度归因与优化提供了可行框架。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed in complex, multi-step workflows, a critical evaluation gap has emerged: most existing benchmarks judge only final outcomes, unable to distinguish reliable reasoning from lucky success or attribute failures to specific process deficiencies, hindering attribution in long-horizon tasks.
  In this work, we present ClawTrack, a dual-assessment benchmark that simultaneously measures what an agent achieves (Task Score) and how it achieves it (Process Score). ClawTrack comprises 320 tasks across 8 domains with 25+ deterministic mock services. A Process Grader scores each reasoning turn along four dimensions (goal alignment, efficiency, information utilization, and result verification), anchored by 12,541 task-specific rubric items. Evaluating 21 models over 16,000+ trials, we find that: (1) process scores effectively attribute success and failure to specific reasoning dimensions, filtering lucky passes invisible to outcome-only evaluation; (2) the four dimensions are complementary, with result verification as the systematic bottleneck; (3) the framework is robust to evaluator choice across different judge LLMs; and (4) process-based trajectory filtering yields consistent post-training improvements across model scales.

</details>


### [89] [GVR-Coder: A Visual-Feedback Framework for Structured SVG Generation in Complex Document and Meeting Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28073)
*Yiming Xu,Jihua Kang,Chunsai Du,Qifan Zhang,Wangqiu Zhou,Yiting Wu,Tianqi Li,Qi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GVR-Coder框架，用于从冗长专业文本生成高质量逻辑可视化图形（SVG格式）。针对现有Text-to-SVG生成面临的三大挑战（数据集稀缺、缺少布局先验、缺乏细粒度视觉反馈），作者从数据、模型和训练策略三个层面提出综合解决方案：构建DocMeetSVG-100K大规模数据集，采用课程式拒采微调和布局约束注入，设计双渲染反馈强化学习机制，并引入"生成-验证-修复"智能体循环。实验证明GVR-Coder在逻辑连贯性和视觉美观性上优于多个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
