Hybrid unstructured meshes enable one to use the immersed boundary technology in a new class of flow problems where the full geometry is decomposed into a fixed geometry part and a changing geometry part. A body-fitted mesh is generated for the fixed geometry while for the changing one is used the immersed boundary method. We simulate several flow problems to test the new meshes, including subsonic flow past a cylinder and subsonic flow past an NACA0012 airfoil, both using finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin methods and solving the Navier--Stokes equations. As an industrial example of our mesh generation, we consider the simulation of a multi-element airfoil: in this case, a mesh generation software generates an unstructured conforming background mesh for the slat and main airfoil, while the flap is placed as immersed geometry in this body-fitted mesh. As accurate and efficient results are sought, this mesh is refined around the flap and then the subsonic flow at high-lift flow conditions is simulated with a finite volume method coupled with an immersed boundary method and using the Reynolds--averaged Navier--Stokes equations. The reported numerical simulations are in good agreement with experimental data.

</details>


### [122] [Compressible solved-volatility stochastic fluid thermodynamics: source-consistent energy, finite-correlation reservoirs, entropy admissibility and boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28333)
*Hsieh-Chen Tsai*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对arXiv:2607.25536中的"可解挥发性随机流体"公式，提出了一个变密度热力学扩展。该扩展将质量、动量和总能量守恒分解为时间演化偏微分方程与鞅兼容性约束，并整合了随机Gibbs恒等式、Gaussian相对熵、Green-Kubo标定、平衡反项和伴随的解析-未解析交换，最终在零挥发性极限下恢复经典可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前的工作中，随机流体公式主要处理了"已解决挥发性"问题，但缺乏对变密度体系和完整热力学效应的处理。本文旨在扩展该框架，使其能够描述变密度可压缩流体的随机动力学，包括密度、温度、压力等原生动热力学场的演化以及机械压力与状态方程压力波动的区分。

Method: 采用源包含的随机输运框架，将守恒律分离为PDE部分和鞅约束部分；以密度和温度为原始热力学场，通过质量守恒确定密度、内能确定温度、状态方程决定压力演化。结合有限相关水库、Green-Kubo校准、平衡反项和伴随的解析-未解析交换，利用随机Gibbs恒等式和Gaussian相对熵建立条件熵可容许性，并通过冻结构描述符分析识别混合双曲-抛物型漂移子系统。

Result: 在零挥发性极限下恢复经典可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程；证明了状态方程压力波动与机械应力冲量是两类不同的物理量，规则有限Mach数波动不会产生独立的白噪声体压力冲量；解析计算验证了压力载体、声学色散、粘性-热能量平衡和低Mach数标度。同时指出非线性适定性、激波可容许性和developed湍流不在本文声称范围内。

Conclusion: 该变密度热力学扩展为随机流体公式提供了完整的热力学一致性框架，实现了从随机公式到经典确定性方程的收敛，并明确了其适用范围边界——主要适用于具有解析解的随机可压缩流动，而不适用于非线性适定性、激波和充分发展的湍流领域。

Abstract: A variable-density thermodynamic extension is developed for the solved-volatility stochastic-fluid formulation of arXiv:2607.25536. Source-inclusive stochastic transport separates mass, momentum and total-energy conservation into time-evolution partial differential equations and martingale compatibility constraints. Density and temperature are the primitive thermodynamic fields: mass conservation determines density, internal energy determines temperature, and the equation of state determines pressure evolution along stochastic particle paths. The resolved kinetic-energy identity is combined with a finite-correlation reservoir, Green--Kubo calibration, an equilibrium counterterm and adjoint resolved-unresolved exchange. A stochastic Gibbs identity and Gaussian relative entropy yield a conditional entropy-admissibility result for a Hencky-reservoir formulation. Equation-of-state pressure fluctuations are distinguished from mechanical stress impulses; regular finite-Mach fluctuations produce no independent white-noise bulk pressure impulse, while fast mechanical pressure is represented by a causal finite-correlation carrier. Conservative boundary conditions and a calorically perfect ideal-gas specialization are given. In the zero-volatility limit, the classical compressible Navier--Stokes--Fourier equations are recovered. A frozen descriptor analysis identifies a mixed hyperbolic--parabolic drift subsystem coupled to algebraic martingale constraints, with closure-dependent elliptic blocks and a singular low-Mach pressure limit. Canonical calculations verify the pressure carrier, acoustic dispersion, viscous-thermal energy balance and low-Mach scaling. Nonlinear well-posedness, shock admissibility and developed turbulence are not claimed.

</details>

### [123] [Effect of biharmonic coupling in frequency-weighted Kuramoto oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27642)
*R. Senthamizhan,R. Gopal,V. K. Chandrasekar*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文研究了频率加权双谐波耦合的全局耦合相振子集体动力学，通过数值分支延拓和解析稳定性分析，在双参数耦合平面内构建了完整相图，发现三种集体态（非相干态、反极多分支态、对称破缺多分支态）及其共存区，并解析推导了非相干态的线性失稳阈值和SBMS的存在性及局域稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kuramoto模型的频率加权扩展和双谐波扩展已分别被独立研究，但二者的联合效应尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示频率加权与双谐波耦合相互作用下系统的完整动力学图景。

Method: 结合数值分支延拓与解析稳定性分析两种手段：数值上通过分支延拓构建双参数耦合平面内的完整相图，解析上推导非相干态的线性失稳阈值以及对称破缺多分支态（SBMS）的存在性和局域稳定性条件，并与大规模数值模拟进行对照验证。

Result: 发现三种截然不同的集体态：非相干态（IC）、反极多分支态（APMS）和对称破缺多分支态（SBMS），由三个双稳区域和一个窄三稳区域分隔。从非相干态到两种有序态的转变均为不连续且带滞后的，体现了频率加权诱导的爆炸式同步；APMS与SBMS之间的转变同样是突变性的。解析预测与数值模拟高度一致。

Conclusion: 频率加权与双谐波耦合的联合效应显著丰富了Kuramoto模型的集体动力学行为，产生了包含多稳和三稳在内的复杂相图。解析结果能够准确描述这些相变行为，为理解复杂耦合系统中爆炸式同步与多稳定性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the collective dynamics of globally coupled phase oscillators with frequency-weighted biharmonic coupling. Although frequency-weighted and biharmonic extensions of the Kuramoto model have each been studied independently, their combined effect has remained unexplored. By combining numerical branch continuation with analytical stability analysis, we construct the complete phase diagram in the two-parameter coupling plane. The competition between the first- and second-harmonic interactions gives rise to three distinct collective states: the incoherent state (IC), the antipodal multibranch state (APMS), and the symmetry-broken multibranch state (SBMS). These states are separated by three bistable regions and a narrow tristable region in which all three states coexist as stable attractors. The transitions from the incoherent state to both ordered states are discontinuous and hysteretic, reflecting the explosive synchronization induced by frequency weighting, while the transition between the APMS and SBMS is also abrupt. Analytically, we derive the linear instability thresholds of the incoherent state together with the existence and local stability conditions for the SBMS, and show that the theoretical predictions are in good agreement with extensive numerical simulations.

</details>

### [124] [Sharp Bounds for Totally Invariant Cycles of Projective Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27738)
*Wentao Chang,Yujie Luo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于光滑射影簇上的内部放大自同态，每个余维数下完全不变素圈的总体L-次数满足一个显式的希尔伯特函数界。特别地，当X为射影空间时，完全不变素(n-r)维圈的数量不超过组合数binom(n+1, r)，且该上界是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究内部放大自同态（int-amplified endomorphism）下完全不变素圈（totally invariant prime cycles）的数量上界，这是代数几何中动力系统与算术几何交叉领域的一个重要问题，旨在推广和精确化此类不变对象的结构限制。

Method: 利用希尔伯特函数的显式上界来约束完全不变素圈的总L-次数。在射影空间的情形下，通过组合计数方法推导出binom(n+1, r)这一精确上界，并给出达到该上界的构造以证明最优性。

Result: 证明了在任意余维数下，完全不变素圈的总L-次数满足显式的希尔伯特函数界；特别地，对P^n上的内部放大自同态，完全不变素(n-r)维圈的数量≤binom(n+1, r)，且该界是最优的。

Conclusion: 该结果为内部放大自同态下完全不变对象提供了精确的计数刻画，在射影空间情形给出了最优组合上界，深化了对代数簇自同态动力系统结构性的理解。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective variety, $f:X\to X$ an int-amplified endomorphism, and $L$ any ample line bundle on $X$. We prove that, in every codimension, the total degree of prime cycles that become totally invariant under an iterate of $f$ satisfies an explicit Hilbert-function bound on their total $L$-degree. In particular, when $X=\mathbf{P}^n$, the number of totally invariant prime $(n-r)$-cycle is bounded by $\binom{n+1}{r}$, and this bound is optimal.

</details>


### [125] [Families of curves separating points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27746)
*Wojciech Porowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对特征零代数闭域上的一条双曲曲线X及其有限个k-有理点S，经过有限étale覆盖替换后，存在X上的一个曲线族，使得S中各点纤维的Jacobian簇两两不同源（非同源）。该结果还应用于Galois截面问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数几何中曲线族纤维的Jacobian簇的同源关系，并希望将这一结果应用于算术几何中的Galois截面问题，从而加深对基本群与有理点之间关系的理解。

Method: 采用有限étale覆盖来替换基曲线X，构造一个X上的曲线族，使得在给定有限点集S上的各纤维的Jacobian簇满足两两非同源的性质。利用代数几何中的同源理论和覆盖空间的方法来证明该构造的存在性，并进一步将结果推广到Galois截面的应用场景。

Result: 成功证明了存在这样的曲线族构造，使得有限点集S上各纤维的Jacobian簇两两非同源；同时将该结论应用于Galois截面问题，给出了相关的应用成果。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是在双曲曲线上可以构造出具有给定点处Jacobian簇两两非同源性质的曲线族，并确认了这一结果对Galois截面问题的有效应用，为相关领域提供了新的工具和思路。

Abstract: We show that for a hyperbolic curve X over an algebraically closed field k of characteristic zero and a finite set S of k-rational points of X, after replacing X by a finite étale cover, there exists a family of curves over X whose fibres over points in S have pairwise nonisogenous Jacobians. We also give an application of our results to a problem concerning Galois sections.

</details>


### [126] [Logarithmic Lefschetz fixed point formulae and resonant boundary indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28288)
*Elaheh Shahsavaripour*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了紧致复流形（带简单正规交叉边界）上严格自映射的logarithmic Lefschetz不动点公式，针对孤立不动点建立了边界点的规范特化系数，并分析了de Rham特化中非共振项消失而共振项记录接触与切向重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Lefschetz不动点公式在带边界的紧致复流形上需要推广，因为边界点上的自映射不动点性质与内部点不同，需要新的方法（正规化尺度与相对对偶）来处理边界点的特化现象。

Method: 通过正规尺度化（normal rescaling）与相对对偶（relative duality）构造可容许不动点理想的规范特化系数；利用logarithmic de Rham复形进行特化，区分共振与非共振边界项。

Result: 得到了logarithmic Lefschetz不动点公式：在de Rham特化下，非共振边界项消失，共振项则记录了正规接触（normal contact）与切向重数（tangential multiplicity）的信息。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典Lefschetz不动点理论推广到带简单正规交叉边界的紧致复流形，并揭示了边界不动点的代数几何与解析性质。

Abstract: We prove logarithmic Lefschetz fixed point formulae for strict self-maps of compact complex manifolds with simple normal crossings boundary and isolated fixed points. At boundary points, normal rescaling and relative duality give canonical specialisation coefficients for admissible fixed-point ideals. In the de Rham specialisation, non-resonant boundary terms vanish, whereas resonant terms record normal contact and tangential multiplicity.

</details>


### [127] [Log Canonical Models and Positive Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28368)
*Benjamin Hollering,Dmitrii Pavlov,Elizabeth Pratt*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一大类开簇的对数典范紧化模型可以由正几何（positive geometries）的典范形式提供显式坐标，并利用这些坐标计算模型的方程。该理论适用于超平面排列的补集、移除直线的三次曲面以及带标记的三次 del Pezzo 曲面模空间等例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造开簇的对数典范紧化是双有理几何中的核心问题，但为这些模型寻找自然的坐标系并得到其方程通常非常困难。本文旨在为一大类簇解决这一难题。

Method: 作者证明了对于一大类簇，正几何的典范形式（canonical forms）可以提供对数典范模型的显式坐标，并利用这些坐标来计算相应模型的方程。

Result: 该理论成功应用于以下具体对象：超平面排列的补集、移除直线的三次曲面，以及带标记的三次 del Pezzo 曲面模空间。

Conclusion: 通过正几何的典范形式，可以显式地构造并计算一大类开簇的对数典范紧化模型，为双有理几何提供了一个有效的计算工具。

Abstract: Constructing log canonical compactifications of open varieties is a central problem in birational geometry. Finding a natural coordinate system and obtaining the equations of these models is difficult in general. We show that for a large class of varieties explicit coordinates for the log canonical model are provided by canonical forms of positive geometries, and use this to compute the equations of these models. Our theory applies, for instance, to complements of hyperplane arrangements, cubic surfaces with lines removed, and the moduli space of marked cubic del Pezzo surfaces.

</details>


### [128] [Stability conditions and moduli spaces on projective families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28411)
*Chunyi Li,Zhiyu Liu,Ziqi Liu,Emanuele Macrì,Alexander Perry,Paolo Stellari,Xiaolei Zhao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将稳定条件的构造从域上的射影概形推广到任意基上的射影族，并证明它们承认半稳定对象的正常相对模空间。同时证明了若干互补结果，包括Halpern-Leistner和Robotis猜想的mass-Hom界的存在性、与曲面上倾斜稳定性的比较、某些向量丛全空间（包括局部Calabi-Yau簇）上支撑导出范畴中稳定条件的构造，以及Bondal-Orlov重构定理的一个简洁新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将射影概形上稳定条件的理论推广到任意基上的射影族，建立相对模空间的正常性理论，并解决Halpern-Leistner-Robotis关于mass-Hom界的猜想，同时为相关几何问题（如倾斜稳定性比较、局部Calabi-Yau簇）提供统一框架。

Method: 论文采用的方法包括：将稳定条件的构造推广到相对/族的情形；运用导出范畴方法与moduli理论工具建立正常相对模空间；通过mass-Hom界的证明、与倾斜稳定性的比较以及支撑导出范畴上的构造等互补手段来完善该理论框架。

Result: 成功将稳定条件推广到任意基上的射影族并证明半稳定对象具有正常相对模空间；证明了mass-Hom界猜想；建立了与曲面/三维簇上倾斜稳定性的比较；构造了包括所有局部Calabi-Yau簇在内的向量丛全空间的支撑导出范畴上的稳定条件；给出了Bondal-Orlov重构定理的简洁新证明。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩展了稳定条件理论的适用范围，将模空间正常性从单点基推广到任意基，并通过多个互补结果展示了该理论的广泛适用性，为导出范畴几何与模空间理论提供了统一而强大的工具。

Abstract: We extend the construction of stability conditions on projective schemes over a field to projective families over an arbitrary base, and prove that they admit proper relative moduli spaces of semistable objects. We also prove a number of complementary results: the existence of mass-Hom bounds for these stability conditions, as conjectured by Halpern-Leistner and Robotis; a comparison with tilt-stability on surfaces and threefolds; a construction of stability conditions on the supported derived category of total spaces of certain vector bundles, including all local Calabi--Yau varieties; and a simple new proof of Bondal and Orlov's reconstruction theorem.

</details>


### [129] [Boundedness of polarized foliated surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28586)
*Yen-An Chen,Minzhe Zhu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文建立了klt伴随叶状曲面上的极小模型纲领（MMP），并利用它研究极化伴随叶状曲面的有界性。证明了具有nef伴随典范除子及nef且big的积分极化的ε-lc伴随叶状曲面，在其和的体积上有界时构成有界族。作为应用，建立了一般型伴随叶状曲面的有界性、有效双有理性以及体积的一致正下界。最后，对ε-lc Calabi-Yau伴随叶状曲面，证明了底层曲面上秩一叶状结构典范除子体积属于一个固定的离散集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶状曲面（foliated surfaces）的模空间和有界性研究是代数几何中的重要课题。已有工作处理了非伴随情形（如常规叶状曲面的MMP和有界性），但伴随叶状曲面（adjoint foliated surfaces）的极小模型纲领尚未建立，且极化伴随叶状曲面的有界性问题此前没有被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为更广泛的双有理几何理论提供基础。

Method: 作者首先建立klt伴随叶状曲面的极小模型纲领（MMP），这是后续所有论证的基础工具。接着利用MMP来研究极化伴随叶状曲面的有界性问题，结合体积上界条件和ε-lc条件，证明其构成有界族。在应用部分，通过有界性结果推导一般型伴随叶状曲面的有效双有理性和体积正下界；对Calabi-Yau情形，利用结构分析确定秩一叶状结构典范除子体积所属的离散集合。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) ε-lc伴随叶状曲面在典范除子nef且极大体积有上界时构成有界族；(2) 一般型伴随叶状曲面满足有界性、有效双有理性，且其体积存在一致的正下界；(3) ε-lc Calabi-Yau伴随叶状曲面上秩一叶状结构典范除子的体积属于某个固定离散集合。

Conclusion: 本文建立了伴随叶状曲面的MMP并获得了极化伴随叶状曲面的有界性定理，为叶状曲面理论中Birational Geometry的进一步研究提供了新工具。这些结果推广了非叶状（曲面）情形中的经典有界性结论，并为未来研究更一般维数的伴随叶状簇奠定了基础。

Abstract: We establish the minimal model program for klt adjoint foliated surfaces and use it to study the boundedness of polarized adjoint foliated surfaces. We prove that $ε$-lc adjoint foliated surfaces with nef adjoint canonical divisor and a nef and big integral polarization form a bounded family, provided that the volume of their sum is bounded from above. As applications, we establish boundedness and effective birationality for adjoint foliated surfaces of general type, as well as a uniform positive lower bound for their volumes. Finally, for $ε$-lc Calabi--Yau adjoint foliated surfaces, we prove that the volumes of the canonical divisors of rank one foliations on the underlying surfaces belong to a fixed discrete set.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在标准d-单纯形上，对于凸且关于负熵L-光滑的函数类，任何一阶方法的收敛速率下界为Ω(L/T)。这表明镜像下降在该函数类中是最优的（至多差一个对数因子），并首次为特定且结构良好的prox-function证明了不可加速性。结果还扩展到量子设置，即关于负von Neumann熵L-光滑的函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明在相对光滑性假设下加速可能是不可能的（Dragomir et al.，2022），但其构造的prox-function是病态的，与困难实例一起人工构造而成。本文旨在针对具有良好结构的特定prox-function（负熵）证明不可加速性，填补这一空白，并探索量子设置下的类似结果。

Method: 在标准d-单纯形上，针对凸且关于负熵L-光滑的函数类，考虑所有一阶方法，通过构造困难实例证明收敛速率下界Ω(L/T)，要求d = Ω(T²)。该下界适用于任意一阶方法，因此排除了加速的可能性。并将同样的证明技术扩展至量子设置，即关于负von Neumann熵L-光滑的谱面体上的函数类。

Result: 证明了在凸且关于负熵L-光滑的函数类中，任何一阶方法的收敛速率下界为Ω(L/T)，当d = Ω(T²)时成立。该结果表明镜像下降在该类中是最优的（至多差对数因子），且加速不可行。该结果同样在量子设置中成立，即关于负von Neumann熵L-光滑的函数类。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在最优化中，对于关于负熵相对光滑的凸函数类，即使使用结构良好的prox-function，一阶方法也无法加速，镜像下降已是最优选择。这一结果不仅在经典设置中成立，也推广到了量子设置，为相对光滑框架下的优化理论提供了重要的负面结果。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [131] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预测的混合规划方法，用于制定预测性维护策略，通过少量参数定义策略，既可通过理论推导也可通过运行失效数据优化获得，在预防性更换和预防性订购两个决策场景中，该策略性能接近优化基准策略，但更高效且对过拟合更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性维护需要将预后模型提供的系统未来状态信息与维护决策结合，但现有方法可能复杂、参数多或易过拟合，需要一种既高效又鲁棒的混合规划方法来制定维护策略。

Method: 提出混合规划方法，综合考虑决策选项、预后模型提供的未来系统状态信息以及更新-报酬过程的成本，通过理论推导或从运行至失效数据的优化来确定少量参数定义维护策略。

Result: 在预防性更换和预防性订购两种预测性维护决策场景中的数值研究表明，推导出的策略性能可与优化的基准策略相媲美，同时在效率和防止过拟合方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 该混合规划方法能够以少数参数生成高效且鲁棒的预测性维护策略，为实际工业维护决策提供了实用且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [132] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有集值目标的多源Weber问题在最小时间函数框架下的最优解存在性、解集的定性性质、Lipschitz连续性以及全局和局部解映射的稳定性，提供了该问题的定量与定性灵敏度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源Weber问题是经典的设施选址问题，但现有研究大多假设目标为单点集。当目标为集值目标（如不确定性下的区域需求）时，问题变得更加复杂，需要建立更一般的理论框架来保证解的存在性和稳定性。

Method: 在最小时间函数框架下建立广义多源Weber问题的数学模型；利用变分分析工具证明全局和局部最优解的存在性；通过集值分析研究解集的闭性、紧性及有界/无界条件；推导目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性；引入并分析全局与局部解映射的稳定性。

Result: 建立了全局和局部最优解的存在性定理，刻画了解集的闭性、紧性和有界/无界条件；证明了目标函数与最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz连续性；给出了全局和局部解映射的稳定性分析结果。

Conclusion: 这些结果为广义多源Weber问题在最小时间函数设置下提供了定量和定性的灵敏度分析框架，为不确定性环境下多设施选址问题奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [133] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: OptGraph 是首个将图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）引入自动化进化优化的智能体工作流，通过构建可复用的类型化图知识、利用图邻域信息增强检索，并支持自适应知识更新，在基准测试上平均精确准确率比现有最先进方法高8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的自动化进化优化方法存在三个关键局限：模式复用能力不足、缺乏错误感知的精细化修正、以及跨不同任务的检索鲁棒性较差。

Method: OptGraph 首先将可复用经验构建为类型化图，捕捉建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节与错误修正之间的关系；推理阶段利用图邻域信息丰富检索到的知识，为建模、验证与迭代精化提供结构化上下文；此外支持自适应知识更新，从执行轨迹和验证反馈中提炼可复用图知识，无需进行LLM参数微调。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，OptGraph 相比最先进的基于提示的自动化优化框架，平均精确准确率提升了8.9%。

Conclusion: OptGraph 通过图检索增强生成有效解决了现有LLM自动化进化优化方法在模式复用、错误感知精化和检索鲁棒性方面的不足，验证了图结构知识在进化优化智能体中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [134] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一维黏性Burgers方程在局部内部控制下的二次最优跟踪问题，建立了全局指数turnpike性质。对于任意初始数据，当周期跟踪目标足够小时，有限时域最优解逼近唯一的周期最优稳态；零目标情形则无需对初始数据施加小量假设即可获得原点处的全局稳态turnpike。这是黏性Burgers方程首次得到全局指数turnpike结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burgers方程作为描述黏性流体与非光滑非线性现象的经典模型，其最优控制问题中turnpike性质的验证对非线性PDE控制理论具有重要意义。此前针对Burgers方程的turnpike结果通常局限于局部或没有全局性保证，本文旨在填补这一空白，建立无需初始数据小量假设的全局指数turnpike性质。

Method: 证明方法分为两层：首先通过严格凸性和周期Riccati理论建立局部指数turnpike；其次利用抛物型耗散论证获得一个与时间水平无关的吸引时间（absorbing time），从而将局部结果提升为全局指数turnpike。

Result: 对任意初始数据，在周期跟踪目标充分小时，有限时域最优解指数逼近唯一的最优周期轨道；零目标情形下无需初始数据的小量假设即可获得全局稳态turnpike。这是黏性Burgers方程的首个全局指数turnpike结果。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合局部Riccati理论与抛物耗散机制，成功克服了Burgers方程非线性对流项带来的全局性困难，为更一般的非线性PDE最优控制问题的全局turnpike分析提供了新的方法论思路。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [135] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多维布朗运动与独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动下风险资产的组合效用最大化问题，允许策略包含跳跃信号。采用鞅最优性原理推导相关带跳倒向随机微分方程（BSDE），刻画值函数与最优策略，并证明了BSDE解的存在唯一性，同时处理了异质性跳跃信号的特例，最后给出对数效用下高斯信号的数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将组合效用最大化问题从一维情形推广到多维布朗运动和独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动的更复杂风险资产设定下，并允许投资策略利用跳跃信号，以更真实地建模金融市场中不连续的价格变动和信息异质性。

Method: 采用与一维指数效用情形相同的方法：首先将组合动态表征为半鞅过程，然后利用鞅最优性原理推导相应的带跳BSDE，通过该BSDE的解刻画值函数和最优策略；随后证明带跳BSDE解的存在唯一性，并考虑投资者收到异质性跳跃信号的特殊情形。

Result: 成功推导了多维布朗运动与跳跃过程共同驱动下的带跳BSDE，证明了该BSDE解的存在唯一性；针对异质性跳跃信号情形进行了专门分析，并为对数效用情形提供了高斯信号的数值验证。

Conclusion: 该方法将一维指数效用框架成功拓展至多维设定，带跳BSDE为组合效用最大化问题提供了统一的刻画工具，且数值结果验证了理论框架的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [136] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集合选择的强结构可控性（SSC）逆合成问题，提出了一个两阶段数学框架：识别结构根的可达性前提，以及三种对称破缺算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在矩阵加权网络中，选择最小领导者集合以保障强结构可控性是一个NP-hard挑战，目前尚未解决。

Method: 证明结构不可控性仅源于两类瓶颈：维度特定的可達性隔离和拓扑对称等价性。据此提出两阶段合成框架：第一阶段是可達性前提以识别结构根；第二阶段包含三种对称破缺算法（贪婪Weisfeiler-Lehman选择、子模界最大化、分区熵最大化）。

Result: 数学证明保证了对不变子空间和结构膨胀的免疫性，并在多种拓扑上进行了广泛的数值评估验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效求解最小领导者集合选择问题，通过理论证明和数值实验确保强结构可控性。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [137] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域劳动力规划问题，其中员工流失服从二项分布且参数依赖于招聘后员工水平。通过引入Binomial-K-convexity概念证明Bellman函数中K-convexity在二项传播下得以保持，进而证明最优招聘策略具有(s,S)结构，并开发了基于分段近似的MILP求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际劳动力规划中，员工流失率往往受当前员工规模影响，并存在固定的招聘启动成本。传统模型往往假设平稳分布或固定流失率，难以刻画决策依赖的转移概率和固定招聘成本带来的非凸性。

Method: 1) 建立有限时域随机动态规划模型，其中员工流失服从决策依赖的二项分布；2) 定义单期变量的离散凸性和期望总成本的K-convexity；3) 引入Binomial-K-convexity概念证明K-convexity在Bellman方程的二项传播下保持不变；4) 证明最优策略具有(s,S)结构；5) 提出分段近似方法将问题转化为MILP，用于求解各期(s,S)参数。

Result: 理论结果表明最优招聘策略呈现(s,S)型结构：当员工水平低于阈值s时招至S水平，否则不招聘。数值实验表明所提MILP方法计算速度快且最优性间隙小。

Conclusion: 该研究从理论上证明了决策依赖二项流失下(s,S)策略的最优性，并通过MILP方法实现了高效求解，为具有固定招聘成本和随机流失的劳动力规划问题提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [138] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了以传输距离（Kantorovich距离）作为正则化项的测度空间优化问题，利用弱星二阶次导数的理论推导无间隙的第二阶最优性条件，并证明其在一定的平滑性假设下与二次增长条件等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测度空间上的优化问题中，传输距离正则化广泛用于最优传输、最优控制等领域。然而，由于测度空间的非光滑性和弱星拓扑的复杂性，二阶最优性条件（尤其是无间隙的充分必要条件）此前缺乏系统的理论推导。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用弱星二阶次导数（weak-* second subderivative）框架来分析测度空间上的优化问题。具体包括：(1) 推导弱星二阶次导数的显式计算；(2) 证明弱星下降二阶可微性（weak-* epidifferentiability）；(3) 在对目标函数光滑部分和Kantorovich势（对偶传输问题的解）施加额外假设的条件下，建立弱星二阶次导数与二次增长条件之间的等价关系；(4) 将所得理论应用于测度空间上的最优控制问题。

Result: 成功推导出弱星二阶次导数的解析表达式，证明了弱星下降二阶可微性，并在附加正则性假设下建立了弱星二阶次导数条件与二次增长条件的等价性（即无间隙的二阶最优性条件），最后将该理论应用于测度空间最优控制问题以验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为测度空间中含传输距离正则项的优化问题提供了完整的无间隙二阶最优性条件理论框架，该框架通过弱星二阶次导数实现，并在最优控制问题中得到了成功应用。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [139] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实Hilbert空间中有限个真、下半连续、凸函数的邻近算子系统的精确解的存在性与唯一性问题，推广了经典投影问题，建立了近似解存在的充要条件，并引入逆邻近性质（IPP）及其多种刻画，应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影问题已被广泛研究，但将其推广到更一般的邻近算子系统，研究其解的存在唯一性，是一个自然且有意义的理论扩展。同时，逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）在优化与反问题中有重要应用，其推广形式IPP的引入有助于建立更广泛的理论框架。

Method: 本文通过将系统投影问题推广为邻近算子系统，利用凸分析的邻近算子理论，建立近似解存在性的充要条件；抽象出逆邻近性质（IPP）概念，并推导其多种等价刻画；最后将理论结果应用于可行性问题与信号恢复问题。

Result: 建立了邻近算子方程组精确解与近似解存在性和唯一性的充要条件；引入并系统刻画了逆邻近性质（IPP），作为IBAP的推广；在可行性和信号恢复问题中给出了应用示范。

Conclusion: 该研究为Hilbert空间中邻近算子系统的求解提供了完整的理论基础，IPP的概念及其刻画拓展了最优逼近理论，且在实际优化与反问题中具有应用价值。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于决策图（DD）的切割平面生成方法用于二元多项式优化（BPO）。针对多重线性集，该方法对任意选定的支持结构分离局部多重线性多胞形的面定义割并将其提升回原问题，避免了经典方法中针对固定支持模式手工推导割的过程。该方法还提供了多重线性多胞形的扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画以发现新的多项式可解超图类，以及所生成割的面性证书。实验表明该方法在根节点实现更大间隙闭合且使用更少切割，显著加速了分支定界过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的强不等式（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）是针对固定的支持模式手工推导的，缺乏通用性和自动化能力。作者希望提出一种能够对任意支持结构自动生成面定义切割的系统性方法。

Method: 提出决策图（DD）方法：设计一种基于递归公式的紧凑DD编码，仅表示顶点变量并将超边变量隐式编码在状态表示中。从该DD编码中可推导出：(i)多重线性多胞形的扩展公式；(ii)基于有效反链的宽度刻画，揭示新的多项式可解超图类；(iii)所生成切割面性的证书。探索了三种支持策略（复用、扩展、划分局部结构为截面超图）用于切割生成。

Result: 实验表明，DD-based方法在根节点实现更大的间隙闭合（gap closure），使用更少的切割数，并显著加速分支定界（branch-and-bound）过程，优于现有程序。

Conclusion: 决策图方法为BPO的切割平面生成提供了一种通用、自动化的框架，取代了传统手工推导强不等式的方式，同时具有理论上的扩展公式和宽度刻画等副产品，并在实践中展现出优异的性能。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>

### [141] [Performance Benchmarking: Software for the Density Matrix Renormalization Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28369)
*Per Sehlstedt,Paolo Bientinesi,Lars Karlsson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向性能的基准测试框架，用于系统比较DMRG算法的软件实现，并量化评估了八个实现之间的性能差异，发现性能差距可达两个数量级，强调了参数配置对性能的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMRG算法有超过50种软件实现，这些实现多方面存在差异且强烈影响性能，但现有性能评估缺乏一致性标准，很多评估不完整或目标不是直接性能比较，限制了人们对实现之间性能差异的理解。

Method: 构建了一个面向性能的基准测试框架，对八个DMRG实现进行系统评估；考察多种参数设置、优化策略和实现特有功能，分析参数配置如何影响性能以及揭示非显而易见的权衡关系。

Result: 不同实现间在参数对齐的情况下性能差异可达两个数量级；同一实现内不同参数配置也会导致显著性能差异，同样可达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 严格的性能评估能为用户和开发者提供有价值的决策信息，该框架和结果可作为未来更严格基准测试的起点，支持更好的软件开发和优化。

Abstract: The performance of scientific software often determines the scale of problems that can be solved in practice. As multiple implementations of the same algorithm emerge, systematic evaluation is needed to compare their strengths and limitations. The density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) algorithm, widely used to study quantum systems, has over 50 software implementations. These implementations vary in multiple aspects that can strongly affect performance. However, despite the need, performance evaluations of these implementations are scarce and lack a consistent standard; many existing evaluations are either too incomplete to enable meaningful comparisons or focus on objectives other than direct performance comparisons, thereby limiting understanding of how the implementations compare. Here, we present a performance-oriented benchmarking framework to facilitate meaningful comparisons of DMRG implementations, and we apply it to quantify the performance of eight implementations, highlighting similarities and differences among them. Furthermore, we examine multiple parameter settings, optimization strategies, and implementation-specific features to demonstrate how parameter configuration can affect performance and how systematic evaluation can reveal non-obvious trade-offs. The results show significant performance differences, up to two orders of magnitude in some cases, not only between different implementations when aligning parameters, but also within the same implementation when comparing different parameter configurations. Hence, our results demonstrate the significant value and insight that can be gained from conducting rigorous performance evaluations. Using our results and framework as a starting point, more rigorous benchmarking will ultimately help users and developers make informed decisions and support future development efforts to build better, more efficient software.

</details>


### [142] [Fast and Accurate Foundation Models for Equivariant Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28461)
*Seán R. Kavanagh,Chuin Wei Tan,Menghang Wang,Marc L. Descoteaux,Gabriel de Miranda Nascimento,Ulrik Unneberg,Laura Zichi,Francesco Libbi,Norma Rivano,Austin Glover,Vivek Bharadwaj,Anders Johansson,William C. Witt,Albert Musaelian,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了等变机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在追求高推理速度、高训练速度和精度方面的极限，提出了一族基于NequIP和Allegro架构的基础势能模型，在社区基准测试中实现了领先的推理速度、强可扩展性和优异精度，并通过NequIP基础设施的加速优化大幅降低了超大数据集上的训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用MLIP作为基础模型已被广泛部署用于下游微调，但许多科学应用（如分子动力学）需要同时满足高推理速度、高训练速度和精度。特别是在超大数据集场景下，数据效率不再关键，需要探索等变MLIP在准确性与效率之间的权衡边界。

Method: 在NequIP和Allegro等变MLIP架构中构建一族基础势能模型，通过架构优化和基础设施加速，解决精度与推理/训练速度之间的权衡问题，并在多个社区基准（材料发现、热导率预测、近平衡力学与热力学性质）上验证。

Result: 该族基础势在多个社区基准上达到领先的推理速度和出色的精度；NequIP基础设施的加速使超大数据集上的高精度基础势能训练成本大幅降低；同时发现提升材料发现模型精度的关键在于数据集多样性和对过渡金属化合物能量面的一致、改进的描述。

Conclusion: 等变MLIP可以在大数据集场景下同时实现高效率与高精度，但进一步提高模型在材料发现任务上的准确性应优先关注数据集多样性和过渡金属化合物能量面的高质量描述，而非单纯追求模型架构的复杂度。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have emerged as a transformative tool for computational materials science and chemistry, with universal potentials trained on large and diverse datasets now routinely deployed as 'foundation models' for downstream fine-tuning in targeted chemical spaces. Many scientific applications of the resulting models, such as molecular dynamics (MD), require high inference and training speeds as well as accuracy. In this work, we examine the limits of equivariant MLIPs, which directly encode physical symmetries in model architectures, to achieve these competing targets -- particularly in the regime of extremely large datasets where data efficiency is less critical. We show how this trade-off can be addressed, and present a family of foundation potentials in the NequIP and Allegro equivariant MLIP architectures which achieve leading inference speeds and strong scalability as well as excellent accuracies across a range of community benchmarks -- spanning materials discovery, thermal conductivity prediction, and near-equilibrium mechanical and thermodynamic properties. Accelerations implemented within the NequIP infrastructure now permit training of high-accuracy foundation potentials on ultra-large datasets with dramatically reduced computational cost. Alongside, we show that efforts to improve model accuracy for materials discovery should focus on dataset diversity and improved, consistent descriptions of transition metal compound energy surfaces.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Semilinear Elliptic Systems with Nonlocal Homogeneous Critical Nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27423)
*Colin Fried,Mathew Gluck*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一类推广经典Brezis-Nirenberg问题的非线性椭圆方程组变分系统，该问题在三个方向上进行推广：向量值未知函数取代标量值函数、非线性项由两个适当齐次度的齐次函数之和构成、Sobolev临界项为非局部的。论文建立了该系统存在非平凡解的条件以及仅有平凡解的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Brezis-Nirenberg问题是椭圆方程领域的奠基性工作，本研究旨在将该问题推广到更一般的情形——包括向量值函数、更一般的齐次非线性结构以及非局部Sobolev临界项，以探索更广泛情形下解的存在性与不存在性的边界条件。

Method: 采用变分方法，通过构造适当的变分框架来处理非局部临界项带来的紧性困难，分析能量泛函的几何结构（如山路引理等），结合齐次函数的性质研究系统在适当条件下解的存在性与非存在性。

Result: 给出了保证该系统存在非平凡解的充分条件，同时也给出了该系统仅存在平凡解的充分条件，刻画了解存在性与参数/维度等条件之间的临界关系。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了推广的Brezis-Nirenberg型椭圆系统在向量值、多齐次非线性与非局部临界项的组合下，解的存在性由特定条件决定，确立了存在非平凡解与仅有平凡解之间的阈值条件。

Abstract: This paper concerns a variational system of nonlinear elliptic equations that generalizes the classical Brezis-Nirenberg problem. In addition to considering vector-valued unknown functions in place of scalar-valued unknown functions, the problem under consideration generalizes the Brezis-Nirenberg problem in two directions. First, the nonlinearity is the sum of two homogeneous functions of suitable homogeneity degrees. Second, the Sobolev-critical term in the nonlinearity is nonlocal. We establish conditions under which a nontrivial solution to the system under consideration is guaranteed to exist and conditions under which the system under consideration admits only the trivial solution.

</details>


### [144] [Remarks on some quasi-linear fourth order parabolic equations arising in mathematical physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27451)
*Francesco Fanelli,Rafael Granero-Belinchón,Martina Magliocca,Matthieu Pageard*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类广泛的四阶拟线性抛物方程在Wiener空间类中强解的存在性和唯一性，改进了以往文献中特殊情形下解的存在唯一性范围不一致的问题，并讨论了解析性增益及向高阶方程和高正则性传播的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往文献中关于四阶拟线性抛物方程的强解存在性和唯一性结果仅覆盖了一些特例，且在这些特例中唯一性成立的解空间比存在性成立的空间更小，两者不一致。本文的动机是建立统一框架，使存在性和唯一性在同一个（更大的）Wiener空间类中成立。

Method: 在Wiener空间框架下，通过建立统一的理论框架来处理一类广泛的四阶拟线性抛物方程，证明强解的存在性与唯一性，并进一步探讨解的解析性增益、推广到高阶方程以及高正则性传播等性质。

Result: 成功证明了一类广泛的四阶拟线性抛物方程在Wiener空间中强解的存在性和唯一性，且唯一性成立的空间与存在性成立的空间一致，改进了此前的特例结果。此外还获得了解析性增益的结果，并将方法推广到高阶方程和更高正则性的传播。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的Wiener空间框架，解决了四阶拟线性抛物方程强解存在性与唯一性空间不一致的问题，并为高阶方程和正则性传播提供了推广途径，是该领域的一个系统性改进。

Abstract: This note proves existence and uniqueness of strong solutions to a broad class of fourth-order quasi-linear parabolic equations in the class of Wiener spaces. It improves upon previous results in the literature, devoted to some special cases falling within the general framework developed here, in which uniqueness held true in a smaller class than the space where existence was proven. Gain of analyticity, as well as generalisations to higher-order equations and to propagation of higher regularities, are also discussed.

</details>


### [145] [Rigorous justification of the hydrostatic-incompressible approximation for weakly stratified isothermal flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27477)
*Quyuan Lin,Xin Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了等温可压缩Navier-Stokes系统在小马赫数和小纵横比极限下的动力学行为，揭示了三维波分离现象，并证明了在无初始波大小限制下解的均匀存在唯一性，以及当小参数趋于零时收敛到不可压缩原始方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大气和海洋流动的一个关键数学问题是从可压缩Navier-Stokes系统推导出不可压缩原始方程。小马赫数和小纵横比的双极限问题具有高度的各向异性，导致慢波、快水平声波和更快垂直声波的三波分离现象，且这些波并非相互正交，投影依赖小参数，给非线性分析带来显著困难。

Method: 论文通过仔细分析能量演化和声波演化的耦合作用，建立了解对任意固定小参数的均匀存在性与唯一性。关键是处理三个非正交波之间的投影关系以及由此产生的非线性项，通过能量估计和声波分量的精细分析来克服小参数带来的奇异性。

Result: 在不限制初始波大小的情况下，证明了可压缩Navier-Stokes系统对任意固定小参数具有均匀的存在唯一性；并进一步证明当小参数趋于零时，解收敛到不可压缩原始方程的解，该方程描述了大气和海洋流动。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从各向异性可压缩Navier-Stokes系统到不可压缩原始方程严格极限的理论基础，对理解大气-海洋动力学模型的物理一致性提供了数学保证。

Abstract: We consider the limit of small Mach number and small vertical-to-horizontal aspect ratio for the isothermal compressible Navier-Stokes system. In addition, we consider the scale in which the stratification is weak. Owing to the anisotropic nature of the problem, the dynamics exhibit a three-wave separation phenomenon, consistent of a slow wave, a fast horizontal acoustic wave, and an even faster vertical acoustic wave. These three waves, unfortunately, are not mutually orthogonal, and the corresponding projections are parametrized by the small parameter, which significantly complicates the nonlinear analysis. Without any restriction on the size of the initial waves, we establish the uniform existence and uniqueness of solutions to the compressible Navier-Stokes system for any fixed small parameter, by carefully analyzing the evolutions of both the energy and the acoustic waves. Moreover, we prove that, as the small parameter tends to zero, the solutions converge to that of the incompressible primitive equations governing atmospheric and oceanic flows.

</details>


### [146] [Nonlinear stability and instability of rotating Riesz star solutions of the compressible Euler-Riesz equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27925)
*Samuel R. Charles*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究可压缩Euler-Riesz方程的旋转稳态（称为"旋转Riesz星"），在质量次临界和质量超临界两个区域建立了存在性以及非线性稳定性/不稳定性结果，并发现旋转的稳定化或失稳化效应取决于Riesz相互作用的奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩Euler-Riesz方程建模了恒星动力学、等离子体物理和数学生物学中的多种物理现象。理解其旋转稳态的存在性及其稳定性对于理解天体结构（如恒星、星系）的形成和演化至关重要，但目前关于旋转稳态的严格数学理论尚不完善。

Method: 1) 在质量次临界区域，利用角动量剖面的次齐次假设，通过适应轴对称设定的集中紧性论证建立旋转Riesz星的存在性与非线性稳定性；2) 在质量超临界区域，利用角动量剖面的超齐次假设，在多项（polytropic）设定下证明存在性，并仔细分析质量保持缩放（比非旋转情形更精细）；3) 通过分析自由能沿这些缩放的凹性来证明质量超临界下旋转Riesz星的不稳定性。

Result: 建立了两类质量区域中旋转Riesz星的存在性：次临界下证明非线性稳定，超临界下证明存在但非线性不稳定。处理了旋转带来的新紧性困难（极小化序列沿半径发散环的紧性问题），并超越了小角速度区域的限制。

Conclusion: 旋转对Euler-Riesz系统可产生稳定化或失稳化两种相反效应，具体取决于Riesz相互作用的奇异性（即在质量次临界与超临界区域分别表现不同）。研究结果表明了角动量对解的结构性影响。

Abstract: The compressible Euler--Riesz equations arise in the modelling of a wide range of physical phenomena, including stellar dynamics, plasma physics, and mathematical biology. We study rotating steady states of the attractive compressible Euler--Riesz equations, which we call rotating Riesz stars, and establish existence and nonlinear stability or instability results in both the mass-subcritical and mass-supercritical regimes. In the mass-subcritical regime, we prove the existence of rotating Riesz stars under suitable subhomogeneity assumptions on the angular momentum profile. We then establish their nonlinear stability through a concentration compactness argument adapted to the axisymmetric setting. Rotation creates new compactness difficulties, most notably the possibility that minimising sequences are tight along rings whose radii diverge to infinity. In the mass-supercritical regime, we prove existence in the polytropic setting under suitable superhomogeneity assumptions on the angular momentum profile, thereby extending the theory beyond the small angular velocity regime. The proof requires a careful analysis of mass-preserving scalings, which are more delicate than in the non-rotating case. Finally, by analysing the concavity of the free-energy along these scalings, we establish the instability of the resulting mass-supercritical rotating Riesz stars. Our results show that rotation can have either a stabilising or a destabilising effect, depending on the singularity of the Riesz interaction.

</details>


### [147] [On the upper bound for the vorticity growth of bi-rotational Euler flows without swirl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27560)
*Khakim Egamberganov,Deokwoo Lim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了$\mathbb{R}^{d}$（$d\geq 4$）中具有双旋转对称且无涡旋的可压缩欧拉流动。结果显示：第一个结果为Yudovich型解的局部适定性；第二个结果在$d\leq 6$时给出全局适定性，并证明涡度最大值的增长速率与轴对称无涡旋流动中的速率一致（后者由第二作者与Jeong及Shao-Wei-Zhang分别获得）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将轴对称无涡旋欧拉流动中的已知结果推广到更高维数（$d\geq 4$）具有双旋转对称性的情形，探索涡度最大值的增长速率是否在高维双旋转对称结构下保持一致。

Method: 针对$d\geq 4$维空间中具有双旋转对称且无涡旋的不可压缩欧拉方程，建立了Yudovich-type解的局部适定性理论，并在此基础上进一步分析全局适定性条件，通过比较不同对称结构下的涡度量估计推导增长速率。

Result: (1) 在$d\geq 4$时，双旋转对称无涡旋欧拉流的Yudovich型解是局部适定的；(2) 当$d\leq 6$时，解是全局适定的；(3) 涡度最大值的增长速率与轴对称无涡旋流动一致。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在低维区域（$4\leq d\leq 6$）双旋转对称性足以保证欧拉流动的全局适定性，且涡度增长速率不随对称结构的变化而改变，为高维欧拉流动的适定性理论提供了新的进展。

Abstract: For $d\geq 4$, we consider incompressible Euler flows in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ with bi-rotational symmetry and without swirl. Our first result gives the local wellposedness of the Yudovich-type solution. The second result provides global wellposedness up to $d\leq 6$. In particular, it shows that the rate of growth of the vorticity maximum coincides with the rate from axisymmetric flows without swirl, which was obtained in the paper by the second author and Jeong (Arch. Ration. Mech. Anal. 249(3):32, 2025) and Shao--Wei--Zhang (Acta Math. Sin. (Engl. Ser.), 42(3):663-679, 2026).

</details>


### [148] [Incompressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier limit from a nonlinear quantum Fokker-Planck equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27583)
*Young-Pil Choi,Byung-Hoon Hwang,Ju-Hwan Hyun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文从具有玻色-爱因斯坦或费米-狄拉克统计的非线性量子Fokker-Planck方程出发，在扩散标度下推导出不可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier极限系统，量子参数保持固定。作者通过对Knudsen数的均匀估计证明了微观弛豫、不可压缩条件、Boussinesq关系和紧致性，并通过求解线性化量子Fokker-Planck算子的辅助方程识别了极限粘性应力张量和热通量，最终得到的不可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统通过归一化常数和输运系数体现量子统计效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从微观动力学方程（含量子统计效应的非线性量子Fokker-Planck方程）严格推导宏观流体力学极限（不可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier系统），是动理学理论中的基础性问题。该研究的动机在于揭示量子统计（Bose-Einstein/Fermi-Dirac）如何通过输运系数和归一化常数影响宏观流体行为，并建立从量子动理学模型到经典流体力学极限的严格数学框架。

Method: 采用扩散标度并保持量子参数固定，在全局量子平衡附近工作。关键技术手段包括：(1) 对Knudsen数建立均匀估计，证明强微观弛豫；(2) 利用局部守恒律证明不可压缩条件与Boussinesq关系；(3) 通过色散估计证明声学模式局部消失；(4) 求解线性化量子Fokker-Planck算子的辅助方程识别粘性应力张量与热通量；(5) 展开局部量子平衡流形以获得极限输运结构。

Result: 成功推导出不可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier极限系统；证明了不可压缩条件、Boussinesq关系、无散速度分量及量子自适应热模态的强紧致性；识别了极限粘性应力张量和热通量的显式表达式。量子统计效应通过系统的归一化常数（如粒子数密度）和输运系数保留在宏观极限中。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从量子动理学（非线性量子Fokker-Planck方程）到经典不可压缩流体力学（Navier-Stokes-Fourier）的严格极限，证明量子统计效应在宏观层面仍可持续体现，并通过输运系数与归一化常数得以保留。这为量子动理学理论的流体动力学极限提供了统一的数学验证。

Abstract: We derive the incompressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier limit from a nonlinear quantum Fokker-Planck equation with Bose-Einstein or Fermi-Dirac statistics. The model has a self-consistent collision structure, with the local density acting as the collision frequency and the bulk velocity and temperature determined by nonlinear quantum-weighted moments of the distribution. We work near a global quantum equilibrium under the diffusive scaling and keep the quantum parameter fixed. Uniform estimates with respect to the Knudsen number yield strong microscopic relaxation and identify the limiting infinitesimal quantum equilibrium. Using the local conservation laws, we prove the incompressibility condition, the Boussinesq relation, and strong compactness of the divergence-free velocity component and a quantum-adapted thermal mode, while the acoustic modes vanish locally by a dispersive estimate. The limiting viscous stress tensor and heat flux are identified by solving auxiliary equations for the linearized quantum Fokker-Planck operator and by expanding the local quantum equilibrium manifold. The resulting incompressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier system retains the effect of quantum statistics through its normalization constants and transport coefficients.

</details>


### [149] [On Sirakov's equal-frequency uniqueness conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28279)
*Hong-Ge Chen,Yong Liu,Juncheng Wei,Wen Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在N=2,3维空间中，当参数满足0<β<μ₁时，证明了等频率双分量三次薛定谔系统在H¹×H¹空间中恰好存在一个正解（模去同时平移），即所有正解都是同步态的平移，从而解决了Sirakov的等频率唯一性猜想在弱耦合范围内的完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Sirakov提出的等频率唯一性猜想，该猜想关于双分量三次薛定谔系统正解的唯一性问题，此前在弱耦合范围内尚未完全解决。

Method: 归一化后使两个分量满足具有共同势函数的标量方程；构造系统加修正项的加权Pohozaev泛函，证明修正泛函和相关的加权泛函均严格为正；结合这些符号性质与径向通量恒等式，以及与分量之比相关的辅助商，迫使系统同步化。

Result: 证明了系统在N=2,3维、弱耦合条件0<β<μ₁下，模去同时平移恰好有一个正解，即所有正解都是同步态（由标量方程-Δw+w=w³的唯一正径向解构造）的同时平移。

Conclusion: 完整解决了Sirakov的等频率唯一性猜想在弱耦合范围内的证明，主要难点在于排除归一化分量之比非恒定的径向解，通过加权Pohozaev泛函和径向通量恒等式的组合成功地强制实现了同步化。

Abstract: Let $N\in\{2,3\}$, $0<μ_1\leqμ_2$, and $0<β<μ_1$. We prove that the equal-frequency two-component cubic Schrödinger system \[
  -Δu+u=μ_1u^3+βuv^2,
  \qquad
  -Δv+v=μ_2v^3+βu^2v
  \quad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^N \] has exactly one positive solution in $H^1(\mathbb{R}^N)\times H^1(\mathbb{R}^N)$ modulo simultaneous translations. More precisely, every positive solution is a simultaneous translate of the synchronized state constructed from the unique positive radial solution of $-Δw+w=w^3$ in $\mathbb{R}^N$. This settles Sirakov's equal-frequency uniqueness conjecture throughout the weak-coupling range.
  The main difficulty in the proof is to exclude radial solutions for which the ratio of the normalized components is nonconstant. After normalization, the two components satisfy scalar equations with a common potential. We construct a weighted Pohozaev functional for the system together with a correction term and prove that both the corrected functional and the associated weighted functional are strictly positive. Combining these sign properties with a radial flux identity and an auxiliary quotient associated with the component ratio forces synchronization.

</details>


### [150] [On The Minkowski Problem With Respect To A Mixed Euclidean-Gaussian Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27618)
*Stephanie Mui,Lei Qin,Sinan Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类与混合欧几里得-高斯体积对应的新Minkowski问题，利用流方法证明了对称情形下相应Monge-Ampère型方程光滑正规化解的存在性，并通过逼近方法得到了混合欧几里得-高斯Minkowski问题原点对称解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Minkowski问题是凸几何分析中的核心问题之一，而结合欧几里得体积与高斯体积构造混合型Minkowski问题，能够丰富Minkowski问题的研究框架，并建立混合凸几何测度理论。

Method: 本文采用两类方法：(1) 流方法（flow method），用于证明对称情形下对应Monge-Ampère型方程光滑正规化解的存在性；(2) 逼近方法（approximation），由此获得原点对称解的存在性。

Result: 在对称情形下获得了对应Monge-Ampère型方程的光滑正规化解，并进一步通过逼近得到了混合欧几里得-高斯Minkowski问题的原点对称解。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了混合欧几里得-高斯Minkowski问题解的存在性理论，为混合型Minkowski问题的研究提供了新的方法和结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we pose a new class of Minkowski problems corresponding to the mixed Euclidean-Gaussian volume. Using the flow method, we prove the existence of smooth normalized solutions for the corresponding Monge-Ampère-type equation in the symmetric case. Then, by approximation, we obtain the existence of origin-symmetric solutions to the mixed Euclidean-Gaussian Minkowski problem.

</details>


### [151] [A Critical Quantitative Landis Estimate for the One-Dimensional Quarter-Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27673)
*Adham Gudaimat*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对一维分数阶薛定谔方程(-Δ)^{1/4}u+V(x)u=0建立了定量Landis估计，证明若势函数有界且解在局部区间有非平凡下界，则解在远处半径为1的区间上的L∞范数下界为exp(-CR log R)。核心工具包括Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓、Grushin方程转化、算术谱分析以及具有线性权的Carleman估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landis猜测是椭圆方程反演/唯一延拓理论中的重要课题，定量版本的Landis估计可给出唯一延拓的显式控制率。本文旨在将经典Landis估计推广到非局部的一维分数阶薛定谔方程情形，通过局部化技术将非局部问题转化为局部Grushin方程来处理。

Method: (1) 通过Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓将分数阶方程转化为Grushin方程，并作变量替换y=z²/2；(2) 对Grushin方程在退化线（z=0）上施加弱Robin条件；(3) 分析角向算子的算术谱κ_n=2n+1/2（经半密度共轭后）；(4) 建立关键谱估计sup||C((τ+iξ)²-L₀)^{-1}C*||≤Cτ^{-1/2}，导出对可测Robin反馈的线性权Carleman估计，吸收阈值τ≥C(1+||V||∞²)；(5) 结合定量向内传播、固定尺度Grushin球传播和内部柱插值估计，将体内部非零性转移到边界；(6) 通过Landis重标度将局部势依赖C||q||∞²转化为全局速率CR log R。

Result: 建立了如下定量Landis估计：若||V||_{L∞}≤1，||u||_{L∞}≤C₀，且||u||_{L²(-1,1)}≥1，则对所有充分大的R，inf_{|x₀|=R}||u||_{L∞(x₀-1,x₀+1)}≥exp(-CR log R)。该下界给出了分数阶薛定谔方程解在无穷远处指数衰减（带对数修正）的禁止速率。

Conclusion: 本文通过将非局部分数阶问题转化为带有弱Robin边界的局部Grushin方程，成功建立了定量Landis估计。关键的技术贡献在于：角向算子算术谱的精确刻画、针对可测Robin反馈的线性权Carleman估计，以及将局部势依赖通过重标度转化为全局CR log R速率。这一结果为一维分数阶薛定谔方程的唯一延拓和衰减性质提供了定量控制。

Abstract: We establish a quantitative Landis estimate for the one-dimensional fractional Schrödinger equation $(-Δ)^{1/4}u+V(x)u=0$ in $\mathbb R$ with a real-valued bounded potential. If $\|V\|_{L^\infty}\le 1$, $\|u\|_{L^\infty}\le C_0$, and $\|u\|_{L^2(-1,1)}\ge 1$, then \[ \inf_{|x_0|=R}\|u\|_{L^\infty(x_0-1,x_0+1)} \ge \exp(-CR\log R) \] for all sufficiently large $R$. After the Caffarelli--Silvestre extension and the substitution $y=z^2/2$, the equation becomes a Grushin equation with a weak Robin condition on the degeneracy line. The corresponding angular operator has the arithmetic spectrum $κ_n=2n+\tfrac12$ after half-density conjugation. The central spectral estimate is \[ \sup_{ξ\in\mathbb R} \bigl\|C\bigl((τ+iξ)^2-L_0\bigr)^{-1}C^*\bigr\| \le Cτ^{-1/2} \] for parameters separated from the angular lattice. It yields a linear-weight Carleman estimate for measurable Robin feedback with absorption threshold $τ\ge C(1+\|V\|_\infty^2)$. Quantitative inward propagation, fixed-scale Grushin-ball propagation, and an interior-cylinder interpolation estimate then transfer bulk non-vanishing to the boundary. The Landis rescaling converts the local potential dependence $C\|q\|_\infty^2$ into the global rate $CR\log R$.

</details>


### [152] [The primitive equations on curved surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27724)
*M. Hieber,Y. Shao,G. Simonett,M. Wilke*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在三维欧氏空间中光滑闭曲面邻域的领口区域上研究原始方程（primitive equations），利用最大L_q正则性和流体静力学Helmholtz投影框架，建立了强解的存在性、唯一性和正则性，并在无小性假设条件下证明了解的全局存在性及时间-空间联合C^∞光滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始方程是描述大尺度海洋和大气动力学的基本数学模型，但现有研究大多局限于平坦或简单几何区域（如环带或圆柱域）。在光滑闭曲面（如球面）的邻域中，曲率效应和边界几何复杂性给分析带来了新的挑战，因此需要在一般黎曼流形背景下建立强解理论。

Method: 采用最大L_q正则性框架作为核心分析工具，并引入流体静力学Helmholtz（hydrostatic Helmholtz）投影算子来处理由于流体静力近似引入的不适定性问题。首先在局部得到强解的适定性结果，随后通过推导合适的先验估计，利用连续延拓方法将局部解延拓为全局解，最终通过正则性提升论证获得时间-空间联合C^∞光滑性。

Result: 在三维光滑闭曲面邻域的领口区域中，证明了原始方程强解的局部存在性、唯一性和正则性；进一步在无需对初始数据施加任何小性假设的前提下，证明了强解的全局存在性，并证明了所得到的解在时间和空间上是联合C^∞的。

Conclusion: 该工作将原始方程强解理论从平坦几何推广到一般光滑曲面邻域，在无小性条件下建立了全局适定性和高阶正则性，为理解和分析更复杂几何背景下的大尺度海洋动力学提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the primitive equations in a collar neighborhood of a smooth closed hypersurface in ${\mathbb R}^3$. Using the framework of maximal $L_q$-regularity and the hydrostatic Helmholtz projection, we establish existence, uniqueness, and regularity results for strong solutions. Building on these local well-posedness results, we derive suitable a priori estimates and prove the global existence of strong solutions without imposing any smallness assumptions for the initial data. Moreover, we show that the solutions are $C^\infty$ jointly in time and space.

</details>


### [153] [Neural Network Approximation of Solutions to Fractional Parabolic Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27781)
*Jae-Hwan Choi,Hyojae Lim,Jinsol Seo,Young-Jin Sim,Changhoon Song*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了分数阶抛物型方程（含低阶漂移项和势项）解的高维高效神经网络逼近理论，通过引入各向异性谱Barron空间来分别度量时间和频率空间的规律性，实现了维度无关的最大正则性估计，并推导出两层神经网络在混合Sobolev范数下的n^{-1/2}逼近界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶抛物型方程在许多实际应用中至关重要（如异常扩散、金融数学等），但传统数值方法面临维度灾难。神经网络逼近理论为高维偏微分方程求解提供了新途径，但此前缺乏针对含低阶漂移项和势项的分数阶抛物方程解的维度无关逼近理论。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 引入各向异性谱Barron空间，在频率空间中分别度量时间与空间的正则性；2) 利用维度无关的乘法估计和连续性方法处理低阶项，建立维度无关的最大正则性理论；3) 关键技术创新是将Vandermonde矩阵应用于有限时间分数阶热半群的全局时间扩展，利用全局时空Fourier结构分析前向时间演化；4) 证明了一般的谱Barron正则性在时间上一致估计不成立；5) 推导出两层神经网络在混合Sobolev范数下的n^{-1/2}逼近界，覆盖非恒定周期激活和满足多项式衰减条件的非周期激活函数。

Result: 获得了分数阶抛物方程解的维度无关谱Barron正则性理论，建立了n^{-1/2}的两层神经网络逼近界（在混合Sobolev范数下），并证明了均匀时间估计的谱Barron正则性一般会失败。

Conclusion: 本文为分数阶抛物型方程提供了一套完整的维度高效神经网络逼近理论，不仅建立了最大正则性估计，还给出了具体的逼近收敛率，为该类方程的高维数值求解提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We establish a dimension-efficient neural network approximation theory for solutions to fractional parabolic equations with lower-order drift and potential terms. By introducing anisotropic spectral Barron spaces, which measure temporal and spatial regularity separately in frequency space, we first develop a dimension-independent maximal regularity theory for these equations, using dimension-independent multiplication estimates and the method of continuity to incorporate the lower-order terms. A key technical novelty is the application of the Vandermonde matrix to the global-in-time extension of the finite-time fractional heat semigroup with sufficient regularity at the initial time, thereby enabling analysis of the forward-in-time evolution via the global space-time Fourier structure of anisotropic Barron norms. We also show that a corresponding uniform-in-time estimate of the spectral Barron regularity generally fails. Finally, we derive $n^{-1/2}$ two-layer approximation bounds in mixed Sobolev norms for non-constant periodic activations and, under additional anisotropic Barron regularity, for non-periodic activations satisfying a polynomial-decay condition.

</details>


### [154] [$L^\infty$ bounds and asymptotic behavior in a doubly degenerate chemotaxis system below six dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27949)
*Minh Le*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类双重退化的营养物-趋化系统，在三维到五维光滑有界凸区域中，证明了当退化参数α满足特定上界范围时，弱解的全局存在性、时间一致有界性及大时间行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营养物-趋化（nutrient-taxis）系统在生物数学中具有重要应用背景，但其双重退化结构使得经典理论难以直接适用，特别是高维情形（n=3,4,5）下解的全局存在性与有界性问题尚未解决，因此需要发展新的分析工具来克服退化性和非线性耦合带来的本质困难。

Method: 作者主要采用三种技术手段：(1) 若干新颖的函数不等式（novel functional inequalities）以处理双重退化结构；(2) bootstrap论证方法建立解的迭代估计；(3) Moser迭代方法（Moser iteration technique）以获得解的一致有界性估计。

Result: 在适当正则的初值条件下，当α ∈ [1, 5/2 - n/4)时，系统存在全局弱解且该解在时间上一致有界；同时确定了这些解得大时间渐进行为。

Conclusion: 该研究将双重退化营养物-趋化系统在高维情形下的适定性理论向前推进了一步，给出了全局弱解存在性和有界性的充分条件，并通过大时间行为分析揭示了系统的长期动力学特征。

Abstract: We investigate a doubly degenerate nutrient-taxis system of the form \begin{equation*} \begin{cases}
  u_t = \nabla \cdot (u v \nabla u) - χ\nabla \cdot (u^αv \nabla v) + \ell u v, \qquad &x \in Ω, \ t > 0,
  v_t = Δv - u v, \qquad &x \in Ω, \ t > 0, \end{cases} \end{equation*} subject to the homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions in a smoothly bounded convex domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^n$ with $n\in \left \{ 3,4,5 \right \}$, where $α\geq 1$, $χ>0$ and $\ell \geq 0$. For any suitably regular initial data, we establish the global existence of a weak solution that remains uniformly bounded in time, provided that $α$ lies in the range $\left[1, \frac{5}{2} - \frac{n}{4}\right)$, and we also determine the large-time behavior of these solutions. Our proof relies on several novel functional inequalities, a bootstrap argument, and a Moser iteration method.

</details>


### [155] [Isolated Singularities and Measure Data Problems for Semilinear Equations Driven by Stable Lévy Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27954)
*Kamil Dunst,Tomasz Klimsiak*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究由一致椭圆严格2s-稳定Lévy算子驱动的半线性方程的正解，首先证明了在带奇点的区域中每个正分布解都满足带有Dirac质量项的方程，并在p≥d/(d-2s)时该质量必须为零；随后研究了对应Dirichlet问题中Dirac质量替换为有界正测度时存在临界参数，低于阈值有最小正解，高于阈值无解，对称情形下还证明了阈值以下的多重性及阈值本身的解的存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由一般2s-稳定Lévy算子（不仅限于分数Laplacian）驱动的半线性椭圆方程的正解性质，特别是探讨在punctured域中解的奇性行为、Dirac质量的可去性以及测度值边界条件下临界参数的存在性，从而推广已有文献中关于分数Laplacian情形的经典结果。

Method: 首先通过分布意义下的分析证明punctured域中的正解在奇点处的Dirac质量可去性条件；然后利用有界正测度替换Dirac质量，通过构造次/超解、单调迭代或变分方法建立临界参数k_μ的存在性，并利用对称性和比较原理证明解的多重性以及临界情形下解的存在唯一性。

Result: 证明了对任意p≥d/(d-2s)时Dirac质量系数k必须为0；确立了临界参数k_μ的存在，低于该阈值问题存在最小正解，高于阈值无解；在对称情形下证明了阈值之下多重解的存在以及阈值本身解的存在和唯一性。

Conclusion: 该研究将经典的分数Laplacian半线性方程理论推广到更一般的2s-稳定Lévy算子框架下，建立了带测度数据问题的完整存在性与非存在性理论，揭示了临界参数在对称与一般非对称算子下的不同行为。

Abstract: We investigate positive solutions of semilinear equations driven by uniformly elliptic strictly $2s$-stable Lévy operators, where $s\in (0,1)$. We first prove that every positive distributional solution of $-Lu=u^p$ in a punctured domain $D\setminus\{0\}$ satisfies $-Lu=u^p+kδ_0$ in $D$ for some $k\ge0$, and that necessarily $k=0$ whenever $p\ge d/(d-2s)$. We then study the corresponding Dirichlet problem in which the Dirac mass is replaced by a bounded positive measure, and establish the existence of a critical parameter $k_μ$: below this threshold minimal positive solutions exist, whereas above it the problem admits no solution. In the symmetric case, we further prove multiplicity below the threshold, as well as existence and uniqueness at the threshold itself.

</details>


### [156] [A parametrix construction for time-fractional partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28028)
*Sandro Coriasco,Giovanni Girardi,Stevan Pilipović*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文详细构造了时间分数阶偏微分方程分析中出现的参数依赖伪微分算子族的参数化（parametrix），精确研究了渐近展开项和平滑余项对参数的依赖性，并提供了向量值分布拉普拉斯变换的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分数阶偏微分方程（如分数阶扩散方程、分数阶波动方程等）在数学物理中具有广泛应用，其分析需要处理参数依赖的伪微分算子族。构造这类算子的参数化是理解其可逆性与正则性理论的关键步骤，同时精确刻画参数依赖性对后续的定量分析至关重要。

Method: （1）对参数依赖的伪微分算子族系统地构造参数化（parametrix），采用渐近展开技术；（2）对渐近展开各项及平滑余项关于参数的依赖性进行精细的定量分析；（3）给出向量值分布的拉普拉斯变换相关结果，作为时间分数阶PDE分析的补充工具。

Result: 给出了参数依赖伪微分算子族参数化的详细构造过程，确定了渐近展开项和平滑余项对参数的精确依赖关系，并为向量值分布的拉普拉斯变换建立了相关定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为时间分数阶偏微分方程的理论分析提供了严格的伪微分算子工具框架，参数依赖性的精确刻画有助于后续对分数阶方程解的存在性、唯一性和正则性进行定量估计。

Abstract: We prove in detail how to construct the parametrix of a parameter-dependent family of pseudodifferential operators, appearing in the analysis of time-fractional partial differential equations. In particular, we perform a precise study of the dependence from the parameter of the corresponding asymptotic expansion terms, as well as of the smoothing remainders. Moreover, we provide some results about the Laplace transform of vector-valued distributions, also appearing in the analysis of time-fractional partial differential equations.

</details>


### [157] [Osgood meets Ambrosio-DiPerna-Lions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28118)
*Guido De Philippis,Leonardo Franchi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Ambrosio-DiPerna-Lions传输方程适定性理论，将其推广到向量场满足L^p Osgood条件的情形，证明有界分布解的唯一性与重整化性质，并引入基于Littlewood-Paley分解的加权能量守恒的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Ambrosio-DiPerna-Lions理论处理传输方程的适定性问题时，对向量场的正则性要求通常是对称导数的可积性条件。本文旨在将该理论推广到更一般的L^p Osgood条件，同时突破传统证明中对交换子消失这一技术性要求的依赖。

Method: 本文不依赖于经典证明中交换子消失的技巧，而是构造基于解的小波（Littlewood-Paley）分解的加权能量，通过证明该加权能量的守恒性来建立有界分布解的唯一性与重整化性质。

Result: 在向量场满足L^p Osgood条件的框架下，证明了传输方程有界分布解的唯一性，并由此得到解的重整化性质。

Conclusion: 该工作将Ambrosio-DiPerna-Lions理论中向量场的条件从对称导数可积性推广到了L^p Osgood条件，并提供了不依赖于交换子消失的替代证明路径，拓展了经典理论的适用范围。

Abstract: In this note we extend the Ambrosio-DiPerna-Lions theory on the well-posedness of the transport equation to the case in which the vector field satisfies an $L^p$ Osgood condition. In particular we show that bounded distributional solutions to the transport equation are unique and thus renormalized. As opposed to the classical proof, we do not rely on the vanishing of a commutator, but we show that a weighted energy built out of the Littlewood-Paley decomposition of the solution is conserved.

</details>


### [158] [A log-log upper bound on blow-up rates for the mass-critical half-wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28192)
*Taegyu Kim,Soonsik Kwon,Jeongheon Park*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维聚焦质量临界半波方程的有限时间爆破问题。对于具有负能量且质量略高于基态质量的偶初值，证明了log-log上界估计，即与质量临界非线性薛定谔方程相同的log-log定律上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半波方程作为非局部色散方程的代表，其爆破行为此前尚不清楚。由于缺少伪共形对称性且算子|D|是非局部的，经典的Merle-Raphaël策略不能直接适用，需要新的数学工具来建立爆破紧性框架并证明log-log爆破律。

Method: 采用Merle和Raphaël的调制分析策略，但核心创新在于：1）构造几乎自相似的爆破profile，通过将尾项计算进行到任意阶（依赖于动力学参数）并结合Λ-解析空间中的Borel积分求和来实现，使得误差呈指数级小；2）在调制分析中，利用伴随论文中证明的局部维里谱性质来处理非局部算子的困难。

Result: 证明了当t↑T时，‖u(t)‖_{H^{1/2}} ≲ (log|log(T-t)|/(T-t))^{1/2}的log-log上界，这与质量临界NLS方程的上界一致，是目前半波方程爆破率研究的突破性结果。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，尽管半波方程缺乏伪共形对称性且涉及非局部算子，其有限时间爆破仍遵循与质量临界NLS相同的log-log律，揭示了色散方程爆破行为在局部与非局部情形下的某种普适性。

Abstract: We study finite-time blow-up for the one-dimensional focusing mass-critical half-wave equation
  \begin{equation*}
  i\partial_tu=|D|u-|u|^2u.
  \end{equation*}
  For even initial data with negative energy and mass slightly above the ground-state mass, we prove the log-log upper bound
  \begin{equation*}
  \|u(t)\|_{\dot H^{1/2}}\lesssim \left(\frac{\log|\log(T-t)|}{T-t}\right)^{1/2}
  \quad \text{as}\quad t\uparrow T.
  \end{equation*}
  This gives, for the half-wave equation, the same log-log law upper bound as in the mass-critical nonlinear Schrödinger equation.
  The proof follows a similar strategy developed by Merle and Raphaël, but requires a new construction of the blow-up profile. Main difficulty arises from the nonlocal operator $|D|$ and the absence of pseudo-conformal symmetry. We construct an almost self-similar profile with exponentially small error by combining tail computations carried out to arbitrary order, depending on a dynamical parameter, with Borel integral summation in $Λ$-analytic spaces. Then, in the modulation analysis, we use a local-virial spectral property proved in the companion paper \cite{Park2026arXiv}.

</details>


### [159] [Finite-time blow-up for the mass-critical half-wave equation with negative energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28194)
*Jeongheon Park*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维聚焦质量临界半波方程，证明了在负能量且质量略高于基态质量的偶初始数据条件下，解的有限时间爆破，并给出了爆破速率上界。这是该方程在近基态区域负能量解有限时间爆破的首个结果，证明依赖于调制分析以及通过解析约化结合计算机辅助证明得到的强制不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半波方程是一类非局部色散方程，其质量临界情形下的爆破行为与局部NLS有本质不同。此前缺乏负能量解在近基态区域的有限时间爆破结果，主要障碍在于缺乏用于调制分析的强制（coercivity）估计，因为半波方程无法用局部NLS中的ODE方法建立该估计。

Method: 采用Merle-Raphaël发展的调制分析方法。核心创新在于通过两条路径证明强制不等式：(1) 解析约化部分：结合约束Morse指标论和Birman-Schwinger原理，将强制问题约化为有限个谱不等式的验证；(2) 计算机辅助部分：通过对实直线紧化后获得基态的验证近似解，利用区间算术严格验证上述谱不等式。两者结合给出强制定理。

Result: 对具有偶初值、负能量且质量略高于基态质量的解，证明了有限时间爆破，并得到爆破速率上界 ||u(t)||_{H^{1/2}} ≲ |log(T-t)|^{1/4} / √(T-t) (t↑T)。这是近基态区域半波方程负能量解的首次有限时间爆破结果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了质量临界半波方程近基态区域负能量解爆破理论的关键空白，展示了计算机辅助证明在非线性色散方程爆破理论中的可行性，为后续更一般的爆破结果（如任意符号能量或高维情形）提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional focusing mass-critical half-wave equation
  \[
  i\partial_tu=|D|u-|u|^2u.
  \]
  For even initial data with negative energy and mass slightly above the ground-state mass, we prove finite-time blow-up and obtain the upper bound \[
  \|u(t)\|_{\dot H^{1/2}}
  \lesssim
  \frac{|\log(T-t)|^{1/4}}{\sqrt{T-t}}
  \qquad\text{as }t\uparrow T. \]
  This is the first finite-time blow-up result for negative-energy solutions to the mass-critical half-wave equation in the near-ground-state regime.
  The proof uses the modulation analysis developed by Merle--Raphaël \cite{MerleRaphael2005AnnMath}. For the half-wave equation, the key missing ingredient is a coercivity estimate for a nonlocal quadratic form generated by the scaling direction. Unlike the local NLS, the half-wave equation does not admit the ODE methods used to establish the corresponding coercivity estimate. Instead, we prove the coercivity estimate by an analytic reduction followed by a rigorous computer-assisted proof.
  The spectral analysis part reduces the coercivity problem to a finite collection of spectral inequalities by combining constrained Morse index arguments with the Birman--Schwinger principle. The computer-assisted part certifies these inequalities by interval arithmetic using a validated approximation of the ground state obtained by compactifying the real line. Together with the modulation analysis, this coercivity theorem gives the finite-time blow-up result.

</details>


### [160] [The Follow-the-Leader scheme with non-monotone velocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28195)
*Marco Di Francesco*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维标量守恒律的熵解逼近问题，通过Follow-the-Leader粒子格式处理速度映射非单调的情形，利用离散最大值原理、近似密度的BV估计及Kružkov意义下熵解的表征来建立收敛性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Follow-the-Leader粒子格式通常假设速度映射是单调的，而实际物理应用中速度映射往往是非单调的（如交通流中的超车行为或非经典波动现象），扩展该格式到非单调速度映射情形具有重要的理论和应用意义。

Method: 采用Follow-the-Leader粒子逼近方案，通过三个关键技术建立收敛性：(1) 离散最大值原理控制数值解的有界性；(2) BV估计保证近似密度的总变差有界，从而得到紧性；(3) 利用Kružkov熵熵通量对来验证极限是熵解。

Result: 证明了在速度映射非单调的条件下，Follow-the-Leader粒子格式逼近的极限函数是原标量守恒律的Kružkov意义下的熵解，从而建立了该格式在非单调情形下的收敛性理论。

Conclusion: 该工作将Follow-the-Leader粒子方法的适用性从单调速度映射推广到非单调情形，丰富了粒子方法在守恒律数值逼近中的理论框架，为相关应用领域的数值模拟提供了数学保证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the approximation of entropy solutions to a one-dimensional scalar conservation law via the so-called Follow-the-Leader particle scheme in case the velocity map is \emph{non monotone}. The result is based on a discrete maximum principle, on $BV$ estimates of the approximated density, and on the characterization of the limit as entropy solution in the sense of Kru\v zkov.

</details>


### [161] [On boundary regularity for the fractional p-Laplacian with unbounded reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28436)
*Antonio Iannizzotto,Sunra Mosconi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究由s-分数阶p-拉普拉斯算子驱动的椭圆方程，在光滑有界区域Ω中带齐次非局部Dirichlet边界条件和L^q(Ω)反应项。证明了方程的独特解u在边界附近具有α-Hölder连续性：当N/ps<q≤N/s时，α为低于p'(s-N/pq)的任意值；当q>N/s时，α=s。此外，当q>N/s时，u/d_Ω^s可Hölder连续延拓到Ω的闭包。结果几乎最优，推广了线性情形的已有正则性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在填补分数阶p-拉普拉斯算子方程在低正则性反应项（L^q空间）下边界正则性的空白。线性情形的边界Hölder连续性已有成熟结果，但分数阶非线性情形下，可积性较弱的外部数据对解的全局正则性影响尚未充分研究，需要确定最佳可能的Hölder指数。

Method: 通过精细的势估计和迭代技术，分析分数阶p-拉普拉斯方程解在边界附近的行为。利用解的表示及其与距离函数d_Ω^s的比值，结合L^q可积性条件对解和梯度进行边界估计，从而推导出Hölder连续性的最优指数范围。

Result: 主要结果有两点：(1) 当N/ps<q≤N/s时，解u对于任何α<p'(s-N/pq)都是α-Hölder连续的；(2) 当q>N/s时，解u是s-Hölder连续的，且u/d_Ω^s可Hölder连续延拓到Ω的闭包。这些结果是几乎最优的。

Conclusion: 本文建立了分数阶p-拉普拉斯方程在L^q反应项下的全局Hölder正则性，将线性情形的经典结果推广到非线性分数阶情形，且所得Hölder指数几乎是最优的，为后续研究此类方程的最佳正则性和数值逼近提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider an elliptic equation driven by the $s$-fractional $p$-Laplacian, set in a smooth bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^N$ with homogeneous nonlocal Dirichlet conditions and a reaction $f$ lying in $L^q(Ω)$ for some $q\ge 1$. We prove that the unique solution $u$ is $α$-Hölder continuous up to the boundary, for any $α$ below $p'(s-N/pq)$ if $N/ps<q\le N/s$, and $α=s$ if $q>N/s$. Also, we prove that if $q>N/s$ then $u/{\rm d}_Ω^s$ admits a Hölder continuous extension to the closure of $Ω$, where ${\rm d}_Ω$ denotes the distance from the boundary. Our results are almost optimal and extend previous regularity theorems known in the linear case.

</details>


### [162] [Shallow water models for the dynamics of oscillating water columns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28515)
*Edoardo Bocchi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了振荡水柱（OWC）波能转换器的动力学问题。在浅水条件下，流体运动分别由一维非线性浅水方程或Boussinesq-Abbott方程控制，空气腔内的气压变化对流体施加弹簧力作用。研究将带有结构和空气腔约束的方程重构为开放水域与腔体之间的传输问题，并证明了局部适定性。文章还提出另一种建模方法，将腔体上部流体视为可自由垂直运动的刚性水柱，将该动力学重构为波-弹簧-质量系统，同样证明了局部适定性，并揭示有效浮力周期由附加质量与弹簧刚度的竞争决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡水柱波浪能转换器是一种重要的波浪能利用装置，其水波与固定部分浸没结构及空气腔的相互作用动力学具有复杂的耦合特性。理解其数学模型的适定性和动力学行为对于波浪能转换器的设计与优化具有重要意义。

Method: 在浅水假设下分别采用一维非线性浅水方程和Boussinesq-Abbott方程描述流体运动，空气腔内气压变化作为弹簧力。在无结构阻尼时假设总流体-弹性能量守恒，将带约束的方程重构为开放水域与腔体之间的传输问题；另一种方法将腔体上部流体视为刚性水柱，将动力学重构为波-弹簧-质量系统。

Result: 证明了重构后传输问题的局部适定性；对于波-弹簧-质量系统同样证明了局部适定性，并表明水柱运动的有效浮力周期由附加质量与弹簧刚度之间的竞争关系决定。

Conclusion: 该研究为振荡水柱波能转换器提供了两种可行的数学建模框架及其适定性理论保障，揭示了水柱运动周期受附加质量与弹簧力刚度共同调控的物理机制，为后续数值模拟和工程应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of an oscillating water column, which is a wave energy converter where water waves interact with a fixed partially immersed structure and an air chamber. Considering the shallow water regime, the fluid motion is governed by either the one-dimensional nonlinear shallow water equations or the Boussinesq-Abbott equations, while the air pressure variation in the chamber acts as a spring force on the fluid. The presence of the structure and the air chamber introduces constraints into the fluid equations. Assuming conservation of the total fluid-elastic energy in the absence of structural damping, we reformulate the constrained equations as transmission problems between the open water and the chamber and show their local well-posedness. We also consider a different modeling approach in which the upper part of the fluid in the chamber is treated as a rigid layer (water column) free to move vertically above the lower fluid. For this configuration, we reformulate the dynamics as wave-spring-mass systems, where the motion of the water column is driven by the shallow water waves outside the chamber. We establish their local well-posedness and show that the effective buoyancy period of the water column motion is determined by the competition between the added mass and the stiffness of the spring force.

</details>


### [163] [Harmonic, radial, and shell stability of the weighted Einstein constraints on the sphere at infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28599)
*Bruno Le Floch,Philippe G. LeFloch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究球面上任意维度的四阶和二阶偏微分算子，这些算子源自线性化爱因斯坦约束算子及其伴随算子的加权复合。文章通过加权Poincaré、Korn和Hardy不等式建立了调和、径向和壳稳定性性质，并在任意维度下研究了相关几何常数的行为，证明稳定性条件适用于广泛的局域化函数（从任意小的局域域到整个球面），从而完成了一般相对论中引力屏蔽问题（最优局域化问题）在任意维度、任意小孔径粘接锥上的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决爱因斯坦约束方程解的渐近行为控制问题，完成Carlotto和Schoen关于引力屏蔽和超调和衰减估计构造的纲领，特别是在任意维度下任意小孔径的粘接锥情形。

Method: 本文引入谐波稳定性（控制边界的调和模）、径向稳定性（控制球平均的径向演化）和壳稳定性（控制解的有耦径向-角度演化）三个概念，并通过加权Poincaré不等式、Korn不等式和Hardy不等式建立这些稳定性性质。作者还引入称为壳泛函的Hamiltonian和动量泛函，证明其具有单调性和半强制性。

Result: 在任意维度下，证明了稳定性条件对广泛类别的局域化函数成立，涵盖了任意小局域域（对应任意小孔径的粘接锥）以及整个球面（对应无局域化）两个极端情形，并研究了相关几何常数的行为。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了引力屏蔽问题在任意维度任意小孔径粘接锥情形下的纲领，壳泛函的结构还暗示了与其他曲率相关几何问题中泛函的潜在类比。

Abstract: We consider fourth-order and second-order partial differential operators localized on domains of the sphere in arbitrary dimension. These operators arise as weighted compositions of the linearized Einstein constraint operators and their adjoints, and played a key role in our resolution of the optimal localization problem in general relativity, also referred to as the gravitational shielding problem. To control the asymptotic behavior of solutions to Einstein's constraints in our companion paper (preprint arXiv:2312.17706), we introduced the notions of harmonic, radial, and shell stability. Harmonic stability controls the borderline harmonic modes, radial stability governs the radial evolution of spherical averages, and shell stability controls the coupled radial-angular evolution of solutions. In the present paper, we establish that these stability properties follow from weighted Poincaré, Korn, and Hardy inequalities. Furthermore, in arbitrary dimension, we investigate the behavior of the associated geometric constants, and conclude that the stability conditions hold for a broad class of localization functions; the theory applies to arbitrarily small localization domains, corresponding to gluing cones with arbitrarily small aperture. At the opposite extreme, our conditions also hold on the entire sphere, corresponding to the absence of localization. This completes, for gluing cones of arbitrarily small aperture in every dimension, the program initiated by A. Carlotto and R. Schoen on gravitational shielding and the construction of solutions enjoying super-harmonic decay estimates. In our proofs, we introduce Hamiltonian and momentum functionals, which we call shell functionals, and show that they enjoy monotonicity and semi-coercivity properties; their structure also suggests possible analogies with functionals arising in other curvature-related geometric problems.

</details>
