### [164] [Breaking Scale Separation in Metamaterials' homogenization: Interface-Inertia-Enhanced Relaxed Micromorphic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27385)
*Angela Madeo,Leonardo Perez,Mohammad Sarhil*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的均质化框架概念：宏观界面的惯性贡献。通过扩展松弛微形态模型，引入定义在边界上的动力学表面能，使均质化模型能够区分仅界面构型不同但体性质相同的有限尺寸超材料试件，从而捕捉微观结构在边界的截断效应对动态响应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的均质化连续介质模型依赖于尺度分离假设（波长远大于单胞尺寸），但在有限尺寸机械超材料中高频下该假设失效，结构在外部边界的截断方式会显著影响动态响应。现有均质化模型无法区分具有相同体性质但不同边界截断构型的试件，需要一个能够捕捉界面惯性效应的均质化框架。

Method: 扩展松弛微形态模型，在边界上引入动力学表面能（kinetic surface energy），生成边界条件中的附加惯性项，作为微观结构截断产生的界面惯性的均质化对应物。该公式保持了松弛微形态模型的变分结构。

Result: 均质化模型现在能够区分共享相同体性质但仅界面构型不同的有限尺寸试件，并能再现完整解析模拟中观察到的依赖于边界的动态响应，特别是在特定的频率区域中。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法首次在均质化描述中引入了宏观界面的惯性贡献这一概念，为机械超材料的边界依赖动态行为提供了新的建模工具，在超越经典尺度分离假设的频率范围内有效。

Abstract: Homogenized continuum models are widely used to describe wave propagation and band-gap behavior in mechanical metamaterials without explicitly resolving their microstructure. Their validity, however, typically relies on the classical separation of scales assumption, according to which the wavelength of the propagating disturbance is much larger than the characteristic size of the unit cell. In finite-size metamaterial samples and at higher frequencies, this assumption progressively breaks down, and the dynamic response becomes strongly influenced by the way the microstructure is truncated at the external boundaries. In this work we introduce a fundamentally new concept in the homogenized description of mechanical metamaterials: the inertial contribution of macroscopic interfaces. We show that different truncations of the same lattice generate boundaries with distinct mass distributions, which lead to measurable differences in the dynamic response of finite-sized specimens. To capture this complex mechanism in a homogenized framework, we extend the relaxed micromorphic model by introducing a kinetic surface energy defined on the boundary of the considered body. This generates an additional inertial term in the boundary conditions that can be seen as the homogenized counterpart of the interface inertia produced by the truncation of the microstructure. As a result, the homogenized model can now distinguish between finite-sized specimens that share identical bulk properties but differ only in the configuration of their interfaces. The proposed formulation preserves the variational structure of the relaxed micromorphic model while enabling the continuum to reproduce boundary-dependent responses observed in fully resolved simulations, particularly in frequency regimes ....... See the PDF for the full abstract.

</details>


### [165] [Exact Total Variation Minimizers as Non-Oscillatory Limiters in High-Order Methods for Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27416)
*Gabriel P. Langlois,Jerome Darbon,Rongjie Lai,Chi-Wang Shu,Xiangxiong Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全变差（TV）去噪的高阶数值方法后处理技术，用于抑制守恒律解在间断附近产生的伪振荡。针对一般分析算子下离散最小化问题不满足次模性、无法使用现有精确最大流算法的困境，作者采用微分包含算法精确求解TV最小化。一维算法有限步终止、无需调参且保持质量守恒；二维则逐维度应用。该方法作为傅里叶伪谱法和五阶有限差分格式的后处理限制器，在标量守恒律、可压缩Euler方程和二维不可压Euler方程上均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶数值方法求解守恒律时，在间断附近会产生伪振荡（Gibbs现象等）。现有的振荡抑制手段（如限制器）往往复杂或依赖问题设定，而TV去噪作为一种通用的后处理思路，其精确求解在一般分析算子下存在算法上的困难（次模性不满足），亟需一种无需调参且精确的求解方案。

Method: 提出在数值解后处理阶段加入TV去噪步骤以降低节点值的全变差。关键在于使用微分包含（differential inclusion）算法精确求解TV最小化问题，避开对次模性的要求。一维情况下算法有限步收敛、无需算法参数调节、保持总质量；二维情况通过逐维度应用一维算法实现。

Result: 该方法作为后处理限制器应用于傅里叶伪谱方法和五阶有限差分格式，对包含标量守恒律、可压缩Euler方程以及二维不可压Euler方程在内的多个算例均有效抑制了伪振荡，同时保持了数值解的精度和关键物理量（如质量）守恒。

Conclusion: TV去噪后处理为高阶守恒律数值方法提供了一种通用、鲁棒且无需调参的振荡抑制手段，微分包含算法克服了传统精确最大流算法的次模性限制，值得在高维和复杂系统上进一步推广。

Abstract: High-order numerical methods for conservation laws can generate spurious oscillations near discontinuities. We propose to suppress these oscillations by post-processing the numerical solution with a total variation (TV) denoising step that reduces the total variation of the nodal values. For a general analysis operator, the resulting discrete minimization problem need not satisfy the submodularity property that existing exact max-flow algorithms require, so we instead compute the TV minimizer exactly using a differential inclusion algorithm. In one dimension, the differential inclusion algorithm computes the TV minimizer in finitely many steps, requires no tuning of algorithmic parameters, and preserves the total mass. In two dimensions, we apply the 1D algorithm dimension by dimension. We test the method as a post-processing limiter for Fourier pseudospectral methods and a fifth-order finite difference scheme applied to scalar conservation laws, compressible Euler equations, and the two-dimensional incompressible Euler equations.

</details>


### [166] [A Unified Discrete Gradient-SAV Framework for Structure-Preserving Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27795)
*Elena Celledoni,David Martín de Diego,Brynjulf Owren,Miguel Vaquero*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将离散梯度（DG）方法与标量辅助变量（SAV）方法相结合的框架，用于构造耗散系统和保守系统的结构保持积分器。通过SAV二次化将动力学提升到扩展状态空间，在该空间中修正能量具有精确的离散梯度恒等式，从而衍生出三种不同精度和计算成本的积分器：一阶半隐式前向欧拉格式、二阶自伴随中点格式和二阶预测格式（降低隐式性）。该方法可扩展到几乎泊松系统，并在可执行的离散条件下保持选定的Casimir不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构保持积分器对于耗散和保守系统的长时间数值模拟至关重要，但现有的DG方法和SAV方法各有局限，缺乏统一的框架来同时保证能量性质（如能量耗散或守恒）和计算效率。

Method: 核心思想是利用SAV二次化将原始动力学提升到扩展状态空间，在此空间中修正能量满足精确的离散梯度恒等式。基于此构建三种积分器：一阶Forward Euler格式、二阶自伴随Midpoint格式和二阶降低隐式性的Predictive格式。该方法还推广到几乎泊松系统，并给出了保持Casimir不变量的可执行离散条件。

Result: 数值实验验证了该方法在Allen-Cahn方程、Ohta-Kawasaki型非局部梯度流、双阱Hamiltonian振子以及具有非线性三次Casimir的Poisson系统上的有效性，展示了不同格式的精度和计算成本权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的DG-SAV框架为耗散和保守系统提供了一类灵活的结构保持积分器，兼顾能量律保持、精度和计算效率，并可推广至更广泛的几乎泊松动力系统。

Abstract: We present a framework combining discrete gradient (DG) methods with the Scalar Auxiliary Variable (SAV) approach to construct structure-preserving integrators for dissipative and conservative systems. The key observation is that SAV quadratization lifts the dynamics to an extended state space on which the modified energy has an exact discrete-gradient identity. This viewpoint yields three integrators with different accuracy and cost profiles: a first-order semi-implicit Forward Euler scheme, a second-order self-adjoint Midpoint scheme, and a second-order Predictive scheme with reduced implicitness. The construction extends to almost-Poisson systems and preserves selected Casimir invariants under an enforceable discrete condition. Numerical experiments cover the Allen--Cahn equation, an Ohta--Kawasaki-type nonlocal gradient flow, a double-well Hamiltonian oscillator, and a Poisson system with a nonlinear cubic Casimir.

</details>


### [167] [Dynamical Low-Rank Filters for Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27432)
*Yoshihito Kazashi,Youssef Marzouk,Fabio Nobile,Fabio Zoccolan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于动态低秩（DLR）逼近的数据同化滤波器，用于随机微分方程（SDE）描述的问题。论文首先推导了一个同时最小化均值和协方差误差的DLRA滤波器，并提出了一种高效纳入相关正交方向的策略，使主子空间也能根据观测算子演化。这些方法自然扩展到线性漂移下的Kalman-Bucy型滤波器，也适用于集成方法，适合非线性漂移和非高斯分布问题。此外，还提出了一个初步的粒子型DLRA滤波器，在非线性场景中展现出潜力。数值模拟验证了这些方法在相关应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对基于SDE的数据同化问题，现有滤波方法在高维场景下计算成本高昂，而动态低秩逼近方法有望在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算复杂度，但此前缺乏系统的DLR型滤波器设计，特别是在同时优化均值和协方差误差以及高效纳入观测方向方面存在空白。

Method: 1) 推导了一个同时最小化均值和协方差误差的DLRA滤波器；2) 提出一种策略高效纳入相关正交方向，使主子空间也能根据观测算子演化；3) 将上述方法扩展到线性漂移下的Kalman-Bucy型滤波器以及集成方法（DLR-EnKF）；4) 提出粒子型DLRA滤波器以处理非线性漂移和非高斯分布场景。

Result: 数值模拟表明，所提出的DLR型滤波器在相关应用中表现有效，包括线性与非线性漂移设置下的数据同化任务，且粒子型DLRA滤波器在非线性场景中展现出潜力。

Conclusion: DLR型滤波器为基于SDE的数据同化问题提供了一套高效且灵活的框架，能够同时处理线性/非线性漂移和高斯/非高斯分布，为后续在该方向的深入研究打开了空间。

Abstract: We propose dynamical low-rank (DLR) type filters for data-assimilation problems based on stochastic differential equations (SDEs). In detail, first we derive a DLRA filter for minimizing jointly the mean and covariance error, as well as a strategy to efficiently include the relevant orthogonal directions. This last approach allows the main subspace to evolve also according to the observation operator. Those procedures naturally extend to a Kalman-Bucy type filter when dealing with linear drift, and to ensemble methods, too, resulting also suitable for problems described by nonlinear drift and possible non-Gaussian distribution. Moreover, we further propose a preliminary particle-type DLRA filter that shows potentiality in nonlinear settings. Numerical simulations show the efficacy of these procedures in relevant applications, opening up to further studies in these filtering directions.

</details>


### [168] [Comparison of a Parametric Physics-Informed Neural Network and a Tensorial Reduced-Order Model for the Shallow-Water Dam-Break Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27433)
*Anton Myshak,Md Rezwan Bin Mizan,Ilya Timofeyev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两种参数化数据驱动降阶模型——物理信息神经网络（PINN）和非侵入式张量降阶模型（TROM），应用于参数化的二维...（应为）一维浅水溃坝问题。两种模型均无需时间积分，直接学习从空间、时间及溃坝参数到物理状态的映射。在样本外和 extrapolated 参数值上进行了详细比较，并证明了引入冲击感知配点对提升 PINN 模型鲁棒性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对参数化浅水溃坝问题，传统数值方法计算成本高、需时间积分，因此需要开发无需时间积分的直接映射式降阶模型，以高效预测不同参数下的物理状态。

Method: 1) 提出PINN降阶模型，学习空间、时间、溃坝参数到物理状态的直接映射；2) 提出非侵入式张量降阶模型（TROM）作为对比；3) 引入冲击感知配点策略来增强PINN在处理间断解（冲击波）时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在样本外及外推参数值上对两种模型进行了详细比较，结果表明冲击感知配点对提升PINN模型鲁棒性至关重要。

Conclusion: 本文验证了两种无需时间积分的参数化数据驱动降阶模型在浅水溃坝问题中的可行性，并强调了冲击感知配点在处理带间断的物理问题时的必要性。

Abstract: We develop two parametric data-driven reduced models: a physics-informed neural network (PINN) and a non-intrusive tensorial reduced-order model (TROM), and apply both approaches to the parametrized one-dimensional shallow-water dam-break problem. Both reduced models do not require time integration and learn a direct solution map from space, time, and dam-break parameters to the physical state. We present a detailed comparison for out-of-sample and extrapolated parameter values. In addition, we demonstrate that it is essential to introduce shock-aware collocation to improve the robustness of the PINN model.

</details>


### [169] [Dynamical Low-Rank Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27438)
*Youssef Marzouk,Fabio Nobile,Fabio Zoccolan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动态低秩近似（DLRA）的平滑方法，用于高维数据同化问题。将联合均值与协方差优化（JMCO）滤波框架扩展至Rauch–Tung–Striebel递归，推导降阶平滑器，并为仿射漂移动力学建立对应的Kalman–Bucy平滑。算法保持DLRA的自适应性，同时显著降低平滑过程的计算时间和存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据同化中，平滑过程的计算成本往往过高，使得传统平滑方法难以实际应用，因此需要一种计算高效的降阶平滑方法。

Method: 将动态低秩近似（DLRA）引入平滑框架，扩展联合均值与协方差优化（JMCO）滤波设置，通过Rauch–Tung–Striebel递归推导降阶平滑器，并对仿射漂移动力学建立相应的Kalman–Bucy平滑方程。

Result: 所提出的算法在保持DLRA自适应特性的同时，显著降低了整个平滑过程的计算时间和存储开销。

Conclusion: 基于DLRA的降阶平滑方法为解决高维数据同化的计算瓶颈提供了有效途径，兼具自适应性与计算高效性。

Abstract: Computational costs often make smoothing procedures prohibitive for high-dimensional data assimilation problems. To address this challenge, we propose a dynamical low-rank approximation (DLRA) methodology for smoothing concerning frameworks based on stochastic differential equations. We extend the previously developed joint mean-and-covariance optimization (JMCO) filtering setting to derive a reduced-order smoother via the Rauch--Tung--Striebel recursion and establish the corresponding Kalman--Bucy smoothing for affine drift dynamics. The resulting algorithms retain the adaptive nature of DLRA while significantly reducing the computational time and storage of the whole smoothing procedure.

</details>


### [170] [Residual-Driven Lifting Identification for Nonlinear-Manifold Reduced-Order Models of Parametrized Linear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27471)
*Francesco A. B. Silva,Jean C. Ragusa,Theron Guo,Rudy Geelen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于残差驱动的非线性流形降阶模型训练方法，用于参数化线性偏微分方程。该方法在给定潜在空间和提升空间的前提下，无需高保真快照即可识别非线性提升映射，通过最小化可计算的残差上界来确定提升，并实现了高效的离线-在线分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非线性流形降阶模型通常依赖高保真快照来训练非线性提升映射，这需要大量昂贵的仿真数据。作者旨在摆脱对快照数据的依赖，仅通过残差信息即可构造非线性提升，从而降低训练成本并扩展非线性降阶模型的适用性。

Method: 该方法将近似解表示为低维潜在坐标与非线性提升到更丰富约化空间的组合。不通过拟合快照数据训练提升映射，而是通过最小化基于残差的状态误差可计算上界来确定提升。对于仿射参数化算子，训练目标支持高效的离线-在线分解，提升更新可转化为一系列低维加权最小二乘问题。

Result: 在对流-扩散问题和平面应变弹性基准测试上的数值评估表明，所提方法相比线性子空间显著提升了精度。所得到的非线性模型在提升识别阶段无需高保真快照，却能达到与基于快照驱动的非线性流形训练相当的精確度。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过残差驱动的训练策略可以在不依赖高保真快照的情况下构建高质量的非线性流形降阶模型，为参数化PDE的降阶建模提供了一种更经济且实用的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a residual-driven procedure for training nonlinear-manifold reduced-order models for parametrized linear partial differential equations that, given prescribed latent and lifting spaces, identifies the nonlinear lifting without high-fidelity solution snapshots. The approximation is represented by a low-dimensional latent coordinate together with a nonlinear lifting into a richer reduced space. Rather than fitting the lifting to snapshot data, we determine it by minimizing a computable residual-based upper bound for the state error. For affinely parametrized operators, the resulting training objective admits an efficient offline--online decomposition, and the lifting update reduces to a sequence of low-dimensional weighted least-squares problems. Numerical evaluations on an advection--diffusion problem and a plane-strain elasticity benchmark show that the proposed approach substantially improves accuracy over linear subspaces. The resulting nonlinear models achieve accuracy comparable to snapshot-driven nonlinear-manifold training while avoiding high-fidelity snapshots in the lifting-identification stage.

</details>


### [171] [Scalable Graph Coreset Selection via Greedy Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27602)
*Zhaiming Shen,Alexander Cloninger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最小内积贪心选择规则的列选择性图采样算法，每次迭代仅访问少量随机拉普拉斯列子集，无需特征分解或全局图遍历，适用于大规模图采样。在随机块模型下理论分析采样性质，并通过合成与实际数据实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大规模图中采样代表性节点是图信号处理与网络分析的基础问题，但现有方法需要访问完整的图拉普拉斯矩阵，在大规模场景下因内存或计算限制而不可行。

Method: 提出一种基于最小内积贪心选择规则的列选择性图采样算法。每次迭代仅访问拉普拉斯矩阵的一个小随机子集列，无需特征分解、无需全局图遍历，适合无法在内存中存储完整拉普拉斯的大规模图。

Result: 在随机块模型下分析表明：当节点度分布均衡时，算法实现与簇大小成比例的采样；在Paley-Wiener空间的带限图信号下，所得均值估计可控，且误差随簇间连接减弱而衰减。合成与实际数据实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该算法在保持有效性的同时显著降低了对内存和计算资源的需求，为大规模图采样提供了实用且理论有据的解决方案。

Abstract: Sampling representative nodes from large graphs is fundamental to graph signal processing and network analysis, yet existing methods require access to the full graph Laplacian, making them impractical at scale. We propose a simple and effective column-selective graph sampling algorithm based on a minimum inner product greedy selection rule. At each iteration, the algorithm accesses only a small random subset of Laplacian columns, requiring no eigendecomposition or global graph traversal, making it well-suited for large-scale graphs where the full Laplacian cannot be stored in memory. We analyze the algorithm under the stochastic block model and show that, when the degree distribution is balanced across nodes, the algorithm achieves sampling proportional to cluster size, and that the resulting mean estimate is controlled for band-limited graph signals in the Paley-Wiener space, with the error decaying as inter-cluster connectivity weakens. Numerical experiments on both synthetic and real-world data validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [172] [A novel sampling method for reconstruction of a moving point acoustic source in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27621)
*Guanqiu Ma,Shuang Li,Hongxia Guo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的采样方法，仅用五个观测点的到达时间数据即可恢复R^3中运动点源的空间位置和发射时刻轨迹，通过代数与几何理论证明了源位置解的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要更多观测点来定位运动点源，且计算效率、稳定性和抗噪性不足。本文旨在减少观测点数量（降至五个）同时提高算法性能。

Method: 结合代数理论与几何知识，基于到达时间差（TDOA）约束公式的残差在真实源位置处为零的性质构造指示函数，形成采样方法。仅利用五个观测点的到达时间数据即可同时重建运动点源的空间位置和发射时刻。

Result: 算法减少了所需观测点数量，同时提升了计算效率、稳定性和抗噪能力。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法在减少观测点需求的同时保持了良好的重建精度和鲁棒性，为运动点源轨迹恢复提供了一种高效且稳定的新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel sampling method to recovering the trajectory of a moving point source in R^3, where both the spatial location and emission moment of the moving point source are unknown. Combining algebraic theory with geometric knowledge, we prove the uniqueness of the source location by using measured data from five observation points. Our sampling method constructs an indicator function based on the property that the residual of the time difference of arrival constraint formula vanishes at the true source location. It achieves the reconstruction of the spatial positions and emission moments of a moving point source only using data from five observation points and their corresponding arrival times. The algorithm not only reduces the required number of observation points, but also improves computational efficiency, stability, and noise resistance. Numerical experiments verify the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [173] [A spectral-compensated scheme for space-parameter Poisson noise functionals: error bounds and complexity estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27657)
*Yun-Ching Chang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lévy测度驱动下泊松空间噪声泛函的计算与离散化问题，提出通过匹配高斯空间噪声补偿被截断的小振幅项，将Wasserstein-1误差从O(ε^{1-α/2})提升至O(ε)，并将计算复杂度从O(τ^{-2α/(2-α)})降至O(τ^{-α})，并通过Gil-Pelaez反演进行确定性数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松空间噪声泛函在Lévy测度驱动下存在三个离散化参数（小振幅截断、Donsker delta截断和混沌阶数），其中被丢弃的小振幅项会显著影响近似误差和计算复杂度。作者旨在通过补偿策略改善误差阶数并降低计算负担。

Method: 在由Lévy测度λ_β(u)du构建的Gel'fand三重空间中计算泊松空间噪声泛函P'(u)。识别三个离散化参数：小振幅截断ε、Donsker delta截断M和混沌阶数N。针对稳定型强度λ_β(u)=cu^{-1-α}，用匹配的高斯空间噪声替代被丢弃的小振幅项。所有数值实验通过Gil-Pelaez反演进行确定性实现，避免蒙特卡洛噪声干扰。

Result: 补偿策略使Wasserstein-1误差从O(ε^{1-α/2})改善至O(ε)，残差在O(ε^{α/2})阶上渐近正态。计算复杂度从O(τ^{-2α/(2-α)})降至O(τ^{-α})。M截断呈代数收敛，N截断呈超几何收敛；还评估了Gamma边界情形（α=0）和指数缓变情形。所有预测的收敛阶数均被确定性数值实验严格确认。

Conclusion: 高斯空间噪声补偿是一种有效的方差缩减与误差改善策略，能够提升泊松空间噪声泛函离散化的收敛速度并显著降低计算复杂度，且数值实验精确验证了理论预测的收敛率。

Abstract: This paper computes Poisson space noise functionals, $P^{\prime}(u)$, realised in a Gel'fand triple built from a Lévy measure $λ_β(u)du$. We isolate three discretisation parameters: a small-amplitude cut-off, a Donsker delta truncation M, and a chaos order N. For a stable-type intensity $λ_β(u)=cu^{-1-α}$ ($0<α<2$), replacing discarded small amplitudes with matched Gaussian space noise improves the Wasserstein-1 error from $O(ε^{1-α/2})$ to $O(ε)$. The residual is asymptotically normal at $O(ε^{α/2})$. This compensation reduces computational complexity from $O(τ^{-2α/(2-α)})$ to $O(τ^{-α})$. We also evaluate the Gamma-type boundary ($α=0$) and exponential tempering. Truncations converge algebraically (M) and super-geometrically (N). All predicted rates are tightly confirmed by deterministic numerical experiments via Gil-Pelaez inversion, eliminating Monte Carlo noise.

</details>


### [174] [Numerical Spectrum Linking: Identification of Governing PDE via Koopman-Chebyshev Approximation with Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27728)
*Phonepaserth Sisaykeo,Shogo Muramatsu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数值框架，通过切比雪夫谱域建立观测驱动与方程驱动的Koopman算子之间的关联，从而从观测数据中识别偏微分方程（PDE）。该框架利用切比雪夫谱表示构造有限维Koopman算子，并结合统一观测模型与最小二乘系数恢复公式，适用于任意采样网格。数值实验验证了其在直接切比雪夫、均匀和不规则采样下均能准确识别控制PDE，且观测密度充分时可稳定实现识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法（如动态模态分解DMD）虽然能近似Koopman算子，但没有显式地将这些算子与微分算子关联起来，导致无法直接从观测数据中识别出控制PDE。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立观测数据与候选控制PDE之间可比较的数值桥梁。

Method: 在切比雪夫谱域中，分别构造观测驱动和方程驱动的有限维Koopman算子，使两者可直接比较；引入统一的观测模型，通过最小二乘系数恢复方法从任意采样网格的观测中恢复切比雪夫谱系数；基于恢复的谱系数实现观测数据与候选PDE之间的数值一致性对接，进而识别控制方程。

Result: 在直接切比雪夫、均匀和不规则采样三种配置下的数值实验均表明，所提框架能准确地从观测中识别控制PDE；观测密度研究表明，当独立观测数量足够支撑稳定的系数恢复时，PDE识别可一致性地可靠实现。

Conclusion: 该框架为从观测数据中识别PDE提供了一种数值一致且鲁棒的新途径，并给出了关于最小观测数量的实用指导准则，对于数据驱动的科学发现具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: A numerical framework is proposed for identifying governing partial differential equations (PDEs) from observational data by establishing a link between observation-driven and equation-driven Koopman operators in a common Chebyshev spectral domain. In contrast to data-driven approaches such as dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), which approximate Koopman operators without explicitly relating them to differential operators, the proposed framework constructs finite-dimensional Koopman operators using Chebyshev spectral representations, thereby enabling direct comparison between data-derived dynamics and candidate governing PDEs. A unified observation model together with a least-squares coefficient recovery formulation is introduced to recover Chebyshev spectral coefficients from observations obtained on arbitrary sampling grids. This provides a numerically consistent interface between practical observations and Chebyshev-based Koopman analysis. Numerical experiments under direct Chebyshev, uniform, and irregular sampling configurations demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately identifies the governing PDE from observations. An observation-density study shows that reliable PDE identification is consistently achieved once sufficient independent observations are available for stable coefficient recovery, providing a practical guideline.

</details>


### [175] [An Asymptotic-Preserving Dynamical Low-Rank Semi-Lagrangian Method for Multiscale Linear Kinetic Transport Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27736)
*Shun Li,Yan Jiang,Mengping Zhang,Tao Xiong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种渐近保持（AP）的动力低秩半拉格朗日方法，用于求解多尺度线性动力学输运方程，将半拉格朗日的大时间步长能力与低秩表示的存储和计算优势相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度线性动力学输运方程在动力学区域和扩散区域之间切换，传统数值方法在不同区域面临计算代价高或稳定性受限的问题，需要一种能在全尺度范围内高效且稳定的数值格式。

Method: 该方法将近似宏观密度更新与基更新Galerkin（BUG）积分器耦合用于动力学分布求解；半拉格朗日通量导数通过采样角求积策略计算以保持低复杂度；在常数系数情形下建立了全求积低秩格式的无条件稳定性分析，并量化了角采样引入的误差。

Result: 数值实验（包括高维测试）表明该方法具有AP性质，在大时间步长下保持稳定，并在动力学和扩散区域均具有计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的方案在扩散极限下呈现AP性质，能够同时满足无条件稳定性、大时间步长高效性和低秩计算复杂度的要求，是多尺度动力学输运方程的有效数值求解方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an asymptotic-preserving (AP) dynamical low-rank semi-Lagrangian method for multiscale linear kinetic transport equations. The method combines the large-time-step capability of semi-Lagrangian discretizations with the storage and cost reduction provided by low-rank representations. The proposed scheme couples an approximate macroscopic density update with the basis update Galerkin integrator for the kinetic distribution. To retain the reduced complexity in the semi-Lagrangian flux evaluation, the flux derivative is computed through a sampled angular quadrature strategy. We establish an unconditional stability analysis of the full-quadrature low-rank scheme in the constant-coefficient case. The error induced by angular sampling in the flux derivative is quantified. The resulting scheme is shown to be AP in the diffusive limit. Numerical experiments, including high-dimensional test cases, demonstrate that the proposed method is AP, stable under large time steps, and computationally efficient across kinetic and diffusive regimes.

</details>


### [176] [An Adaptive Finite Element Method for Marker-Driven Level-Set Transport on Hierarchical Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28104)
*Eugenio Aulisa,Samuele Baldini,Giacomo Barbi,Andrea Chierici,Sandro Manservisi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的自适应有限元水平集输运框架，通过树状分层网格对零水平集附近进行动态局部细化，结合标记物输运和反向特征追踪，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持与均匀离散化相当的精度，适用于二维和三维的结构化及非结构化网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水平集方法在两相流界面捕捉中广泛应用，但传统均匀离散化在保证精度时计算成本高昂。现有自适应方法在网格灵活性、工程集成兼容性和动态细化/粗化方面的能力仍有不足，需要一种既能高效追踪界面又便于集成到现有有限元求解器中的自适应框架。

Method: 采用基于树的分层网格进行动态局部细化以追踪演变界面，在零水平集周围形成窄带高分辨率区域而其余区域保持粗网格。水平集场通过自适应细化网格上的标记物输运进行更新，实现界面预测和自适应层级构建；同时通过反向特征追踪精确评估平流水平集场。两种操作均利用高效的多层级标记物和点定位算法识别各细化层级中的包含单元。

Result: 在二维和三维的结构化与非结构化网格（包括四边形、单纯形、楔形和六面体网格）上，在严重界面变形条件下进行的数值实验表明：自适应策略在精度上可与均匀离散化相媲美，同时大幅降低计算成本；在强自适应条件下仍保持良好的守恒特性。

Conclusion: 所提方法提供了一个高效灵活的自适应水平集输运框架，可自然集成到基于水平集的多相流求解器中，兼具计算效率与数值精度。

Abstract: This work presents a new adaptive finite-element framework for level-set transport, achieving high accuracy in kinematic interface transport problems relevant to interface-capturing methods for two-phase flows while reducing computational cost. This framework accommodates dynamic refinement and coarsening, and is compatible with standard finite-element data structures. The algorithm is applicable to both structured and unstructured discretizations in two and three dimensions. The method uses a tree-based hierarchical mesh with dynamic local refinement that tracks the evolving interface, concentrating resolution in a narrow band around the zero level set while preserving a coarse discretization elsewhere. The level-set field is updated through marker transport on adaptively refined meshes, enabling interface prediction and the construction of an evolving adaptive hierarchy. Concurrently, backward characteristic tracing provides accurate evaluation of the advected level-set field. Both operations employ an efficient multilevel marker and point-location algorithm to identify containing elements across refinement levels. Numerical experiments on two- and three-dimensional structured and unstructured grids, including quadrilateral, simplicial, wedge, and hexahedral meshes, subjected to severe interface deformations, demonstrate that the adaptive strategy achieves accuracy comparable to uniform discretizations at a substantially lower cost, while maintaining good conservation properties under aggressive adaptivity. Consequently, the proposed approach provides an efficient and flexible framework that can be naturally integrated into level-set-based multiphase flow solvers.

</details>


### [177] [R3MG-C: a high-order algebraic-geometric multilevel preconditioner for continuous finite element discretizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28235)
*Davide Polverino,Marco Feder,Luca Heltai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向连续拉格朗日有限元离散的代数-几何多重网格预条件器，利用有限元支撑点坐标通过R-tree算法递归划分生成凝聚层次，并在凝聚单元上嵌入局部不连续多项式空间作为粗空间，形成Nicolaides粗空间的高阶推广，在高阶有限元求解中保持稳定的迭代次数和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数多重网格（AMG）方法对低阶椭圆偏微分方程离散系统高效鲁棒，但在高阶有限元方法中性能显著退化。现有方法依赖预设网格层次或区域分解，缺乏仅利用代数信息即可处理的通用方案，因此需要开发一种适用于高阶连续拉格朗日有限元离散的自动多级预条件方法。

Method: 本文提出一种代数-几何多层次预条件器，其核心步骤包括：（1）仅利用有限元支撑点坐标，通过基于轴对齐包围盒的R-tree算法递归划分支撑点，生成凝聚层次结构；（2）与经典AMG使用分段常数凝聚粗空间不同，该方法在凝聚盒上嵌入局部不连续多项式空间（次数p'>0），并通过连续节点插值实现，构成Nicolaides粗空间的高阶扩展；（3）在均匀盒正则性、插值稳定性和稳定分解假设下，进行两层分析以量化粗多项式次数对大规模凝聚和高阶细离散的补偿效果。

Result: 二维和三维数值实验表明，该V-cycle作为共轭梯度预条件器时，迭代次数保持稳定，在标准AMG性能退化的高阶领域中依然维持有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将代数凝聚策略与高阶多项式粗空间相结合，该方法有效解决了经典AMG在高阶有限元离散中的性能退化问题，提供了一种无需预设网格层次或区域分解的自动多级预条件框架。

Abstract: Algebraic multigrid (AMG) methods are robust and efficient black-box preconditioners for linear systems arising from low-order discretizations of elliptic partial differential equations, but their performance often deteriorates for high-order methods. We introduce an algebraic-geometric multilevel preconditioner for continuous lagrangian finite element discretizations. The method automatically constructs prolongation operators and a Galerkin hierarchy using only the coordinates of finite element support points, without requiring a prescribed mesh hierarchy or domain decomposition. The support-point are recursively partitioned by an R-tree algorithm based on axis-aligned bounding boxes, producing a hierarchy of agglomerates. In contrast to classical AMG, which typically uses piecewise-constant aggregate coarse spaces, our method embeds local discontinuous polynomial spaces of degree $p'>0$, defined on the agglomerate boxes, through continuous nodal interpolation. This yields a high-order extension of the Nicolaides coarse space. A two-level analysis quantifies how the coarse polynomial degree offsets the effects of large agglomerates and high-order fine discretizations, under uniform box-regularity, interpolation-stability, and stable-decomposition assumptions. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions show that the resulting V-cycle, used as a conjugate-gradient preconditioner, maintains stable iteration counts and remains effective in regimes where standard AMG deteriorates.

</details>


### [178] [A MATLAB Tool for the Stable Generation of Matrix Polynomial Evaluation Schemes with Two-Product Savings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28286)
*J. Ibáñez,J. Sastre,J. M. Alonso,E. Defez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种软件驱动的通用方法，将矩阵多项式求值中的2次矩阵乘法（2M）计算节省从孤立的特例扩展到任意多项式（度m∈{18,21,24,26,27,28}及所有m≥30），并开发了基于可变精度算术的MATLAB实现，通过自动验证例程筛选数值稳定的系数集，在保持或提升数值精度的同时系统性地实现2M理论缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕特森-斯托克迈耶（PS）方法长期主导矩阵多项式求值的计算界，但近期理论证明可以消除两次矩阵乘法（2M）。然而，2M缩减仅在少数孤立案例（如特定阶数的矩阵指数和对数的泰勒近似）中被证实稳定，缺乏针对任意多项式的通用方法，这是本文要解决的核心局限。

Method: 本文提出一个软件驱动的流程：（1）将2M缩减扩展到度数m∈{18,21,24,26,27,28}及所有m≥30的任意多项式，仅需非零首项系数；（2）通过求解非线性方程组（SNEs）确定底层求值系数；（3）引入自动验证例程，用于筛选和验证适用于浮点执行的数值稳定系数集；（4）开发MATLAB实现，利用可变精度算术处理SNEs、验证稳定性并推算精度界。

Result: 数值实验涵盖了多种矩阵函数，结果表明所开发的实现保持了经典PS方法的数值精度，在某些情况下甚至提升了精度，同时系统性地实现了2M的理论缩减。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了2M缩减从孤立特例到一般多项式的鸿沟，提供了一套可自动化的软件解决方案，在保证数值稳定性的前提下实现了计算成本的显著降低，为矩阵函数的高效求值提供了新的实用工具。

Abstract: Computing numerical approximations of matrix functions frequently relies on the efficient evaluation of high-degree matrix polynomials. Although computational bounds are historically governed by the Paterson--Stockmeyer (PS) method, recent theoretical developments have demonstrated the viability of evaluation schemes that eliminate two matrix products ($2M$). Existing literature documents stable instances of this $2M$ reduction only for isolated cases, such as specific degrees of Taylor approximations for the matrix exponential and the matrix logarithm. However, a generalized approach for arbitrary polynomials remains unestablished. To address this limitation, this work presents a software-driven procedure that extends these computational savings to polynomials of degrees $m \in \{18, 21, 24, 26, 27, 28\}$ and all $m \ge 30$, requiring primarily a non-zero leading coefficient. Since the underlying evaluation coefficients must be determined by solving systems of nonlinear equations (SNEs), selecting a numerically stable solution set is critical. We introduce an automated verification routine designed to filter and validate robust coefficient sets for floating-point execution. The primary contribution is a MATLAB implementation leveraging variable precision arithmetic to handle the underlying SNEs, verify stability, and project precision bounds. Numerical experiments involving various matrix functions verify that the developed implementation preserves or, in some instances, enhances the numerical accuracy of the classic PS method, while systematically achieving the theoretical reduction of $2M$.

</details>


### [179] [Galerkin method for asymmetrically-weighted Hermite approximations applied to the Vlasov-Poisson system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28409)
*Ruiyang Dai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究Vlasov-Poisson (VP)系统的数值方法，采用速度空间中非对称加权（AW）Hermite基函数。与传统Petrov-Galerkin方法不同，作者提出Galerkin方法以自然保证L2范数下的稳定性，并给出一种计算成本与Petrov-Galerkin方法相当但稳定的等价形式，最后通过数值模拟验证了该方法的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VP系统是双曲系统，使用AW Hermite基函数进行速度空间谱展开时会出现数值不稳定性。传统Petrov-Galerkin方法无法保证L2稳定性，因此需要寻求一种既能保持计算效率又能确保L2稳定的谱方法。

Method: 分析AW Hermite谱形式VP系统不稳定的原因；放弃经典Petrov-Galerkin方法，采用Galerkin方法以自然获得L2范数稳定性；提出一种等价的实现形式，使计算成本保持在与Petrov-Galerkin方法相当的水平。

Result: 通过数值模拟验证了所提出的Hermite谱方法的L2稳定性，证明该方法在保持较低计算成本的同时能有效抑制不稳定现象。

Conclusion: 采用Galerkin方法替代Petrov-Galerkin方法，结合AW Hermite基函数，可以得到L2稳定的VP系统谱方法，且计算成本可控。

Abstract: We investigate a numerical method for the Vlasov-Poisson (VP) system utilizing asymmetrically-weighted (AW) Hermite bases in velocity space, which is an hyperbolic system. In particular, we concentrate on spectral methods in velocity. For the Hermite spectral form of the VP system, we analyze the resaon that the form with AW Hermite bases can be instable. To obtain L2 stability properties, we consider a Galerkin method intead of the classical Petrov-Galerkin method, which naturally ensures stability with respect to the L2 norm. We also present an equivalent form of the method that maintains a computational cost modest compared to that of the Petrov-Galerkin method. Finally, we present numerical simulations based on the proposed Hermite spectral method, showcasing its stability.

</details>


### [180] [Windowed thinning and query complexity for the bouncy particle and Zigzag samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28413)
*Jianfeng Lu,Yinchen Luo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了windowed thinning方法，用于精确模拟bouncy particle采样器和坐标Zigzag过程，并通过定量混合估计和有限时间界，从高斯冷启动出发建立了查询复杂度的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对强凸且光滑目标分布上的bouncy particle sampler和zigzag过程，缺乏精确模拟方法在总变差误差意义下的查询复杂度保证，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出windowed thinning方法：将轨迹划分为确定性窗口，在每个窗口起始处使用一次梯度评估构造可计算的局部包络来近似事件率，结合定量混合估计和反弹/翻转次数的有限时间界推导查询复杂度。

Result: 从高斯冷启动出发，对总变差误差ε，bouncy particle sampler的期望梯度查询数为O(κ^{1/2}d(d logκ+log(1/ε)))，Zigzag过程的等价全梯度查询数为O(κ d^{1/4}(d logκ+log(1/ε)))（d个坐标偏导查询计为一个等价查询）。

Conclusion: windowed thinning为两种精确采样算法提供了可验证的查询复杂度上界，展示了其在条件数和高维场景下的算法效率特征。

Abstract: Let $μ(d x)\propto e^{-U(x)} d x$ on $\R^d$, where $U$ is $m$-strongly convex and $L$-smooth, and denote by $κ=L/m$ the condition number. We consider windowed thinning, an exact simulation method for the bouncy particle sampler and the coordinate Zigzag process. The method divides a trajectory into deterministic windows and uses a gradient evaluation at the beginning of each window to construct a tractable local envelope for the event rate. Combining this construction with quantitative mixing estimates and finite-time bounds on the expected numbers of bounces and flips yields query complexity guarantees from a Gaussian cold start. For total-variation error $\varepsilon$, the expected query counts are $O(κ^{1/2}d\,(d\logκ+\log\frac1\varepsilon))$ gradient queries for the bouncy particle sampler and $O(κd^{1/4}(d\logκ+\log\frac1\varepsilon))$ full-gradient equivalents for Zigzag, where $d$ coordinate-partial queries count as one equivalent.

</details>


### [181] [Graph Neural Multilevel Preconditioners for Iterative Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28456)
*Zechen Zhang,Rui Peng Li,Yousef Saad*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种图神经多级预条件子（GMP），将代数多重网格（AMG）的层次结构作为先验，在一个统一框架中学习光滑算子、限制算子和插值算子，用于通用稀疏线性系统的Krylov迭代求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典AMG预条件子虽然在大型稀疏线性系统上高度可扩展，但对不定或非对称系统的鲁棒性会因基于椭圆PDE的启发式方法失效而下降。尽管GNN已被用作数据驱动的预条件子，但将AMG式层次结构强加到通用稀疏矩阵上的实际效果尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出图神经多级预条件子（GMP），以AMG层次结构为结构先验，在统一框架中联合学习光滑、限制和插值算子；可作为标准Krylov求解器的即插即用预条件子。

Result: 在超过800个稀疏矩阵的基准上，与经典AMG、单层ILUT以及最先进的GNN预条件子对比，刻画了多级图神经预条件在哪些条件下改善收敛、哪些条件下相对强单层基线引入额外开销。

Conclusion: 结果突显了在学习的预条件子中强加AMG式多级结构既具有潜力也存在局限，为大型科学模拟中的预条件子设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Solving large, sparse linear systems is a core task in scientific computing, and efficient iterative solvers rely critically on effective and robust preconditioning. While classical methods such as algebraic multigrid (AMG) are highly scalable, their robustness can degrade on indefinite or nonsymmetric systems where heuristics originally developed for elliptic PDEs are less reliable. Recently, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as data-driven preconditioners; yet, the practical impact of imposing an AMG-style hierarchy remains underexplored for general sparse matrices. In this work, we propose a Graph Neural Multilevel Preconditioner (GMP) that adopts an AMG hierarchy as a structural prior and learns smoothing, restriction, and interpolation operators in a unified framework. Our method targets general sparse systems and is instantiated as a drop-in preconditioner for standard Krylov solvers. On a benchmark of over 800 sparse matrices, we compare against classical AMG, single-level ILUT, and state-of-the-art GNN preconditioners, and characterize the regimes where multilevel graph neural preconditioning improves convergence or, conversely, introduces overhead relative to strong single-level baselines. These results highlight both the promise and the limitations of enforcing AMG-style multilevel structure in learned preconditioners for large-scale scientific simulations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Coordinate and Momentum Distributions of a Small Composite System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27244)
*Dmitry Naplekov,Yury Bezruk,Vladimir Yanovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由可移动壳体和有限数量内部粒子组成的孤立小系统，在总能量和动量守恒条件下，精确推导了壳体坐标和动量的分布。坐标分布由有限分支组成且可能包含平台区域，动量分布对应能量在系统自由度中的非均匀分配。壳体坐标分布取决于粒子数目但不随其运动维度变化，而动量分布取决于自由度数目，因此可通过比较两者推断系统内部结构。壳体因内部粒子碰撞产生的混沌运动不同于布朗运动，即使在无外部介质时也持续存在，并得到了均方根偏差的普适表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过分析孤立有限系统中的壳体运动统计性质，探索从壳体运动分布推断内部粒子结构（如粒子数量、质量、自由度维度）的可能性，并区分此类混沌运动与经典布朗运动的本质差异。

Method: 在总能量与总动量守恒的约束条件下，对可移动壳体与有限数量内部粒子的系统进行精确统计求解，推导壳体坐标和动量分布的理论表达式，并分析不同内部运动模式下的分布特征。

Result: 获得了壳体坐标分布的封闭形式（具多分支结构含平台区）与动量分布的精确表达式；证明了坐标分布仅依赖粒子数目而非运动维度、动量分布依赖自由度数目；导出了壳体均方根偏差的普适显式表达式，依赖内部粒子数及其质量。

Conclusion: 壳体坐标与动量分布的组合分析可作为探测微小系统内部结构的有效手段；内部粒子驱动的壳体混沌运动是区别于布朗运动的一种固有涨落现象，属于无外部介质下自维持的统计行为。

Abstract: An isolated small system consisting of a movable shell and a finite number of internal particles is considered. Exact distributions of the shell coordinates and momenta are obtained for various types of internal particle motion under the conditions of conservation of the total energy and momentum of the system. The coordinate distribution consists of a finite number of branches and may contain plateau regions. The momentum distribution corresponds to a non-uniform distribution of energy among the degrees of freedom of the system. It is shown that the coordinate distribution depends on the number of internal particles and remains unchanged when the dimensionality of their motion varies, whereas the momentum distribution depends on the number of degrees of freedom. Consequently, comparison of the coordinate and momentum distributions of the shell makes it possible to draw conclusions about the internal structure of the system. The chaotic motion of the shell caused by impacts from the internal particles is analyzed; unlike Brownian motion, it persists even in the absence of an external medium. A universal expression for the root-mean-square deviation of the shell is obtained explicitly, depending on the number of internal particles and their masses.

</details>


### [183] [When trajectory-based bounds fail: information thermodynamics under noisy feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27299)
*Natalia Ruiz-Pino,Ludovic Bellon,Antonio Prados*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文实验与理论结合，研究了在欠阻尼反馈控制系统中，三种信息论热力学界（传递熵、不可得信息、马尔可夫互信息）在含噪测量与非马尔可夫控制序列下的表现，发现没有任何一种界普遍最优，轨迹依赖型界对测量噪声更敏感而瞬时相关型界更鲁棒，并观测到随噪声增大马尔可夫互信息界反超不可得信息界的交叉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息引擎可利用反馈从热涨落中提取功，多个信息论界已被提出（传递熵、不可得信息、马尔可夫互信息等），但在真实条件下（测量有噪、反馈时间相关）各界的相对性能尚不清楚。理解这一问题是统一信息热力学框架、指导实际信息引擎设计的核心动机。

Method: 采用欠阻尼反馈控制系统进行实验与理论研究：系统具有马尔可夫测量但非马尔可夫控制序列（即控制依赖过往历史）。在系统中系统性地比较三种代表性信息论界——基于传递熵的界、基于不可得信息的界、基于马尔可夫互信息的界，通过引入不同程度的测量噪声，评估各界的松紧程度及对噪声的敏感度。

Result: 发现没有任何一种界在所有条件下普遍最优。测量噪声优先损害依赖详细轨迹统计的信息量（传递熵、不可得信息），而基于瞬时相关的马尔可夫互信息界相对鲁棒。因此轨迹依赖的界随噪声快速退化，出现交叉现象：在较宽的噪声范围内，马尔可夫互信息界比不可得信息界更紧。

Conclusion: 研究表明在现实（非理想化）反馈协议中，依赖详细轨迹统计的信息论描述存在普遍局限，而基于瞬时相关的信息量更具鲁棒性。这为信息热力学提供了超越理想化反馈协议的统一视角，对实际信息引擎的设计和理论界的选择具有指导意义。

Abstract: Information engines exploit feedback to extract work from thermal fluctuations, extending the second law of thermodynamics through information-theoretic bounds. While several such bounds have been proposed, their relative performance under realistic conditions---where measurements are noisy and feedback is temporally correlated---remains largely unclear. Here, we experimentally and theoretically investigate this problem in an underdamped feedback-controlled system with Markovian measurements but non-Markovian control sequences. We compare three representative bounds derived from transfer entropy, unavailable information, and Markovian mutual information, and find that none is universally optimal. Instead, measurement noise preferentially affects information measures that rely on detailed trajectory statistics, while leaving quantities based on instantaneous correlations comparatively robust. As a consequence, trajectory-dependent bounds deteriorate rapidly, giving rise to a crossover in which the Markovian mutual-information bound becomes tighter than the unavailable-information bound over a broad range of measurement noise. Our results reveal a general limitation of information-theoretic descriptions that rely on detailed trajectory statistics in realistic settings and provide a unified perspective on information thermodynamics beyond idealised feedback protocols.

</details>


### [184] [From pink to brown: Interaction-driven noise color shift in the Aubry-Andr{é} Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27340)
*M. Jiménez-Valdez,S. A. Montes-Camacho,E. J. Torres-Herrera*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新审视一维晶格中受在位准周期势调控的金属-绝缘体相变，采用具有可调近邻相互作用的spin-1/2粒子来建模。作者通过测量连续能级间距的傅里叶变换模平方（即功率谱）来刻画该相变。结果表明：无相互作用时，功率谱在各区域均呈褐噪声行为，无法捕捉清晰的相变信号；而在有相互作用时，金属相呈现粉噪声谱涨落，绝缘相则表现为褐噪声。能级间距分布、能级数方差以及本征态结构均提供了可靠的相变判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视一维准周期势模型中的金属-绝缘体相变，特别是利用功率谱这一较少被采用的谱统计工具，考察其在无相互作用与有相互作用两种情况下的区分能力，以寻找更可靠的相变指示器。

Method: 采用一维晶格上具有可调近邻相互作用的spin-1/2粒子模型，施加on-site准周期势。对连续能级间距进行傅里叶变换并计算功率谱（模平方），同时辅以能级间距分布和能级数方差的统计分析，并在有/无相互作用两种情形下对比这些谱统计量的行为。

Result: 无相互作用时，功率谱在所有区域均呈现布朗噪声（褐噪声）行为，无法区分金属相与绝缘相；而在引入相互作用后，功率谱发生明显改变——金属相表现为粉噪声，绝缘相表现为褐噪声。能级间距分布和能级数方差的分析进一步支持了这一结论，且本征态结构在两种场景下均能提供可靠的相变指示。

Conclusion: 相互作用对于功率谱能否灵敏地区分金属-绝缘体相变起着关键作用：仅在引入相互作用后，功率谱才能通过粉噪声/褐噪声的转变来清晰刻画相变。同时，能级统计与本征态结构在有无相互作用的情况下均可作为可靠的相变判据。

Abstract: We revisit the metal-insulator transition in a one-dimensional lattice subjected to an on-site quasiperiodic potential, modeled using spin-1/2 particles with tunable nearest-neighbor interactions. To characterize the transition, we examine the power-spectrum, defined as the squared modulus of the Fourier transform of consecutive level spacings. In the absence of interactions, the power-spectrum fails to capture clear signatures of the transition, consistently exhibiting brown-noise-like behavior across regimes. In contrast, the interacting case reveals a marked change: the metallic phase displays spectral fluctuations consistent with pink noise, while the insulating phase is characterized by brown noise. These results are supported by analyses of the level spacing distribution and the level number variance. Additionally, we demonstrate that both statistics and the structure of energy eigenstates provide reliable indicators of the transition, in both interacting and non-interacting scenarios.

</details>


### [185] [Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28255)
*Denis Bernard,Friedrich Hübner,Stefano Scopa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并求解了相互作用量子对称排斥过程（IQSEP）模型族，该模型描述带电粒子在格点边上随机量子跳跃的随机过程，跳跃幅度依赖邻近占据数。无相互作用时退化为标准量子简单排斥过程，表现出相干扩散输运；一阶相互作用下捕获非相干扩散输运及其涨落；通过适当重标度相互作用强度，定义了一个介观标度机制，在连续热力学极限中保持有限相干长度，在长/短尺度上插值相干与非相干行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准量子对称排斥过程只能描述相干扩散输运，无法刻画相互作用系统中的非相干扩散输运及其涨落。同时，标准涨落流体力学无法捕获量子相干性的涨落。因此需要一个新的模型家族来弥合微观量子动力学与宏观涨落理论之间的鸿沟，并建立能描述量子相干涨落的介观标度理论。

Method: 作者引入IQSEP模型族，其中跳跃幅度依赖邻近格点的占据数。通过分析三种情形：(1)无相互作用时退化为标准量子简单排斥过程，展示相干扩散输运；(2)一阶相互作用时捕获非相干扩散输运及密度依赖的扩散系数和迁移率，与宏观涨落理论建立联系；(3)通过将相互作用强度按格距适当重标度，定义介观标度机制，使连续热力学极限中保持有限相干长度，从而在不同尺度间插值。

Result: 该模型在相互作用为0时保持相干扩散输运；在一阶相互作用下能描述非相干扩散输运及其涨落，给出密度相关的扩散系数和迁移率；通过介观标度重标度，在连续极限中保留有限相干长度，建立了超越标准涨落流体力学范围的量子相干涨落标度理论。

Conclusion: IQSEP提供了一个统一的框架，从微观量子相干输运到宏观非相干扩散输运及其涨落，通过介观标度机制在两者之间搭起桥梁，为相互作用扩散系统中量子相干性的涨落提供了新的理论描述，拓展了标准涨落流体力学的适用范围。

Abstract: We introduce and solve the Interacting Quantum Symmetric Exclusion Process (IQSEP), a family of models describing the stochastic quantum hopping of charged particles along the edges of a lattice, with hopping amplitudes that depend on the occupations of neighbouring sites. In the absence of interactions, they reduce to the standard quantum simple symmetric exclusion process, exhibiting coherent diffusive transport. For interactions of order one, they capture incoherent diffusive transport and its fluctuations, characterized by density-dependent diffusivity and mobility, making contact with the macroscopic fluctuation theory. By rescaling the interaction strength appropriately with the lattice mesh, we define a mesoscopic scaling regime that retains a finite coherence length in the continuous thermodynamic limit. This regime interpolates between coherent behavior at small length scales and incoherent behavior at large scales. The resulting scaling theory accounts for fluctuations of quantum coherences in interacting diffusive systems, going beyond the scope of standard fluctuating hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [186] [Non-reciprocity drives a Brownian dimer out of equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27740)
*Suman Pramanik,Soham Dutta,Arnab Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一个由两个过阻尼单体组成的二维布朗二聚体最小模型，其中包含各向同性谐振子势和非互易弹簧耦合。研究表明，仅靠非互易相互作用就能在没有外部时变驱动和仅接触单一热浴的情况下驱动系统远离平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非互易相互作用能否在缺少外部时变驱动和单一热浴条件下独自将系统推向非平衡态，从而深入理解非互易耦合在微观动力学中的作用。

Method: 构建两个过阻尼单体的二维最小模型，单体被各向同性谐振势约束，并通过违反牛顿第三定律的非互易谐振弹簧耦合。在弹簧零静止长度极限下解析求解稳态概率分布和电流，将其映射到布朗旋动这一非平衡现象；对有限静止长度的情况则采用数值方法计算。

Result: 非互易相互作用本身足以使系统远离平衡态。零静止长度极限下精确计算了稳态概率分布和电流，并建立了与布朗旋动现象的映射关系；有限静止长度情形下通过数值方法获得了相应的量。

Conclusion: 非互易弹簧相互作用是使该二维布朗二聚体系统产生非平衡行为的充分条件，且不需要外部时变驱动或多个热浴的支持。

Abstract: We consider the minimal model of a two dimensional Brownian dimer consisting of two overdamped monomers, trapped in an isotropic harmonic potential and mutually coupled by a non-reciprocal harmonic spring that violates Newton's action-reaction principle. We have shown that the non-reciprocal interaction alone can drive the system far from equilibrium, in the absence of any external time dependent drive and being in contact with a single thermal bath. The exact steady state probability distribution and current are explicitly calculated for the zero-rest-length limit of the spring, which eventually maps our model to another non-equilibrium phenomenon, called Brownian gyration. For a spring with finite rest length, these quantities are calculated numerically.

</details>


### [187] [Ensemble theory of thermodiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27839)
*R. Belousov,J. Elliott,A. Erzberger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用路径系综理论，将热驱动硬核气体中耦合的热输运和扩散输运表示为图上的动力学过程。仅通过单个平衡模拟校准模型，即可在不同温度梯度下准确预测非平衡质量分布，并通过解析表达式估计Soret系数等输运性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热流在维持温度差的同时会产生物质梯度（如硬核气体原子模拟中观测到的Soret效应）。现有的原子模拟方法虽然能观察到这一现象，但缺乏一种能够从平衡信息出发、高效且准确地预测非平衡状态下质量分布的理論框架。

Method: 采用路径系综理论（path-ensemble theory），将耦合的热输运与扩散输运建模为图（graph）上的动力学过程。模型仅需从一次平衡模拟中校准参数，即可用于预测非平衡条件下的物质分布。

Result: 该模型在广泛施加的温度梯度范围内准确预测了硬核气体的非平衡质量分布。此外，利用解析表达式成功估计了Soret系数等输运性质。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种高效、基于平衡模拟数据即可获得非平衡输运预测的理论框架，为研究热-物质耦合输运现象提供了新的工具，且无需针对每个温度梯度单独进行昂贵的非平衡模拟。

Abstract: Heat flow sustained by a temperature difference generates a gradient of matter as observed, for example, in atomistic simulations of hard-core gases. We use path-ensemble theory to represent the coupled thermal and diffusive transport by dynamics on a graph. Calibrated from a single equilibrium simulation, this model accurately predicts nonequilibrium mass distribution in a hard-core gas across a wide range of applied temperature gradients. It also enables estimation of transport properties, such as the Soret coefficient, using analytical expressions.

</details>


### [188] [Mpemba effect in a chemomechanical model of the Kinesin molecular motor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27998)
*Karthik Cheruvary,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文首次在六态化学机械网络模型——Kinesin分子马达中研究了Mpemba效应（远离平衡的体系反而比靠近平衡的体系弛豫更快）。研究发现该效应在化学平衡态下存在且可由自由能景观理解；机械和化学驱动主要重塑Mpemba相图而不改变其弛豫现象学；马达速度的弛豫同样反映了底层随机动力学的异常弛豫，为实验观测提供了可及信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应已在多种物理系统中被广泛研究，但其在生物相关的非平衡过程中的作用几乎未被探索。作者希望将Mpemba效应的研究拓展到生物分子马达等生命系统，建立分子马达作为研究生命系统中异常弛豫的平台。

Method: 采用Kinesin分子马达的六态化学机械网络模型，分别在化学平衡和非平衡条件下研究弛豫行为。首先在化学平衡下确认Mpemba效应的存在并利用自由能景观解释其定性特征；随后考察机械和化学驱动对详细平衡破缺的影响，绘制Mpemba相图；最后分析马达速度的弛豫是否复现底层随机动力学的异常弛豫特征。

Result: （1）在化学平衡条件下确认了Mpemba效应的存在，且其定性特征可由自由能景观解释；（2）机械和化学驱动主要重塑Mpemba相图，但在物理相关参数范围内不根本改变弛豫现象学；（3）马达速度的弛豫与底层随机动力学的异常弛豫相一致，提供了实验可观测的Mpemba效应信号。

Conclusion: 分子马达可作为研究生命系统中异常弛豫的有前景的基准体系，为在非平衡生化网络中探索Mpemba效应提供了更广泛的框架。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, wherein a system prepared farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to equilibrium, has been extensively investigated in a wide range of physical systems. In contrast, its role in biologically relevant non-equilibrium processes remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate anomalous relaxation in the six-state chemomechanical network model of the Kinesin molecular motor under both equilibrium and non-equilibrium conditions. We first establish the existence of the Mpemba effect in chemical equilibrium and show that many of its qualitative features can be understood from the underlying free-energy landscape. We then examine the effects of mechanical and chemical driving, showing that breaking detailed balance primarily reshapes the Mpemba phase diagram without qualitatively altering the relaxation phenomenology over the physically relevant parameter regime. Finally, we demonstrate that the relaxation of the motor velocity also mirrors the anomalous relaxation of the underlying stochastic dynamics, thereby identifying an experimentally accessible signature of the Mpemba effect. Our results establish molecular motors as a promising baseline for studying anomalous relaxation in living systems and suggest a broader framework for exploring the Mpemba effect in non-equilibrium biochemical networks.

</details>


### [189] [Three-Dimensional Kardar--Parisi--Zhang Scaling in Polariton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28106)
*Junhui Cao,Denis Novokreschenov,Artem Alexandrov,Timothy Halpin-Healy,Alexey Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出三维激子-极化激元晶体作为观测三维KPZ（Kardar-Parisi-Zhang）普适性的平台。通过从三维光子晶体下极化激元带中的随机驱动耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发，消除大量密度和库模式，获得凝聚体相位的有效3+1维KPZ方程。数值模拟显示一阶相干性满足KPZ标度行为，指数与3+1维KPZ基准值β=0.1845和χ=0.3135一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ普适性是微观细致平衡破缺产生宏观尺度标度的范例。虽然已在驱动凝聚体和生长界面中探索了一维和二维实现，但在三维空间中展示KPZ标度仍是一个重大挑战。因此，作者旨在寻找一个能够实现三维KPZ普适性观测的平台。

Method: 作者提出三维激子-极化激元晶体作为观测平台。具体方法包括：（1）从三维光子晶体下极化激元带中凝聚体的随机驱动耗散Gross-Pitaevskii方程出发；（2）消除大量密度模式和库模式，推导出凝聚体相位的有效3+1维KPZ方程；（3）对KPZ方程和完整驱动耗散极化激元模型分别进行数值模拟，并检验一阶相干性的标度行为。

Result: 数值模拟显示中间渐近区间中，一阶相干性满足-ln|g(1)(0,Δt)|∝|Δt|^(2β)和-ln|g(1)(Δr,0)|∝|Δr|^(2χ)，指数与3+1维KPZ基准值β=0.1845和χ=0.3135一致，表明KPZ标度行为在三维空间中得以实现。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，三维极化激元晶体是一种可控的量子流体途径，能够实现对更高维非平衡普适性（即三维KPZ普适性）的观测与研究。

Abstract: Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) universality provides an example of macroscopic scaling generated by microscopic violation of detailed balance. While one- and two-dimensional realizations have been explored in driven condensates and growing interfaces, demonstrating KPZ scaling in three spatial dimensions remains a major challenge. Here we propose a three-dimensional exciton-polariton crystal as a platform for observation of 3D KPZ universality. Starting from a stochastic driven-dissipative Gross-Pitaevskii equation for a condensate formed in a three-dimensional photonic-crystal lower-polariton band, we eliminate the massive density and reservoir modes and obtain an effective $3+1$-dimensional KPZ equation for the condensate phase. Numerical simulations of both the KPZ equation and the full driven-dissipative polariton model show an intermediate-asymptotic regime in which the first-order coherence obeys $-\ln |\gone(0,Δt)|\propto |Δt|^{2β}$ and $-\ln |\gone(Δr,0)|\propto |Δr|^{2χ}$, with exponents consistent with the $3+1$ KPZ benchmarks $β= 0.1845$, $χ= 0.3135$. Our results identify three-dimensional polariton crystals as a controllable quantum fluid route to higher-dimensional nonequilibrium universality.

</details>


### [190] [From Exponential to Gaussian Tails: Fractal Wavefront Scaling and the $κ$-Weibull Distribution at Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28172)
*O. V. Pavlovsky,M. Yu. Satleikin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由元胞自动机建模的活性介质中的临界演化特征。系统表现出两种截然不同的动力学状态：活性衰减态和全填充活性波态，每种状态具有独特的分形维数和涨落统计。在临界区间内，系统呈现非平凡的分形维数和介于两状态之间的涨落分布，该桥梁行为源于临界区间的雪崩动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究活性介质中临界演化的本质特征，理解不同动力学状态（衰减态与活性波态）之间的转变机制，以及临界区间内雪崩过程对系统行为的影响。

Method: 采用元胞自动机对活性介质进行建模，系统依据随机规则演化；通过分析系统的分形维数和涨落统计来刻画两种不同的动力学状态，并重点研究临界区间内的雪崩动力学行为。

Result: 确认了两种不同动力学状态（活性衰减态和全填充活性波态）各自具有独立的分形维数和涨落统计特征；发现临界区间内系统展现出非平凡的分形维数，且其涨落分布恰为两种状态的桥梁，这一过渡行为由雪崩过程主导。

Conclusion: 活性介质的临界演化可通过元胞自动机有效建模，临界区间内的雪崩动力学是连接两种动力学状态的关键机制，为理解复杂系统中的临界行为提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The paper examines the features of critical evolution in an active medium modeled by a cellular automaton. The system evolves according to stochastic rules, exhibiting two qualitatively distinct dynamical regimes: one of fading activity and another of all-filling activity waves. Each regime is characterized by its own fractal dimension and fluctuation statistics, both of which are analyzed in detail. Within the critical interval, the system displays a nontrivial fractal dimension and a fluctuation distribution that bridges the two regimes. This bridging behavior arises because, in the critical interval, the dynamics are governed by avalanches.

</details>


### [191] [Synchronization, Kinematic Waves and Spike-Phase-Separation in Feedback Ising Neural Networks on Heterogeneous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28275)
*Anna Poggialini,Irem Topal,Fabrizio Lombardi,Daniele De Martino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由稳态反馈回路驱动的动力学Ising神经网络，通过Curie-Weiss异质平均场近似，解析刻画了异质网络上宏观同步极限环经由Andronov-Hopf分岔涌现的机制，并证明网络度异质性会解耦神经元棘波率与突触棘波率，导致同质系统中不存在的物理现象（如运动学波和相分离态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构异质性约束了复杂系统中的集体动力学，但非平衡态下其解析可处理性仍然有限。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，为理解网络异质性如何调控宏观振荡和远离平衡态的相变提供数学框架。

Method: 采用Curie-Weiss异质平均场近似（由Monte Carlo模拟验证），对具有神经兴奋性—群体放电率之间稳态反馈环路的动力学Ising神经网络进行分析。推导了Andronov-Hopf分岔的闭合形式相边界，并通过度矩比显式刻画振荡涌现与网络异质性的关系，同时通过pitchfork分岔分析相分离不动点的稳定性。

Result: 推导出振荡涌现的闭合形式相边界，证明其由度矩比控制；发现度异质性使每神经元棘波率m与每突触棘波率u解耦，产生两类同质系统不存在的现象：(i) 从网络边缘向枢纽传播的、按度排序的连续激活运动学波；(ii) 经由pitchfork分岔出现的低温相分离态。并证明在高异质拓扑下该相分离不动点稳定化并动态摧毁同步极限环。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解网络异质性如何调控宏观振荡和远离平衡态的相变提供了统一的数学框架，揭示了结构异质性可通过解耦不同层级的棘波率来产生同质网络不可见的非平衡态物理现象。

Abstract: Structural heterogeneity constrains collective dynamics in complex systems. However, its analytical tractability out of equilibrium remains limited. In this work, we study a class of kinetic Ising neural networks driven out of equilibrium by a homeostatic feedback loop between the neuronal excitability and the population firing rate. Using a Curie-Weiss heterogeneous mean-field approximation validated by Monte Carlo simulations, we provide an analytical characterization of how a macroscopic synchronized limit cycle emerges via an Andronov-Hopf bifurcation on heterogeneous networks. We derive closed-form phase boundaries and show that the onset of oscillations is explicitly controlled by network heterogeneity through the degree moment ratio. Degree heterogeneity decouples the spiking rate per neuron m from the spiking rate per synapse u, generating physical phenomena absent in homogeneous systems. These include (i) kinematic waves of sequential, degree-ordered activations propagating from the network periphery to the hubs, and (ii) a low-temperature phase-separated state emerging via a pitchfork bifurcation. We prove that for highly heterogeneous topologies, this phase-separated fixed point stabilizes and dynamically destroys the synchronized limit cycle. These results provide a mathematical framework for understanding how heterogeneity regulates macroscopic oscillations and out-of-equilibrium transitions in neural networks

</details>


### [192] [Convergence to the exact kinetics of the one-dimensional Riviera model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28419)
*Pascal Viot*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对Riviera模型——一种随机顺序沉积模型，提出了一种可通过任意有限阶实现的系统化闭合方案，求解无限阶动力学方程层级，从而获得精确的堵塞密度和完整时间演化解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riviera模型虽然规则简单（一维情况下目标位点为孤立空位时沉积被拒绝），但缺乏精确解析解，需要建立系统的求解方法。

Method: 推导一个无限动力学方程层级，并引入可在任意有限阶实现的系统化闭合方案，逐阶求解所得方程系统。

Result: 动力学随阶数快速收敛到精确行为；在高阶时，方法能给出极其精确的堵塞密度估计，同时保留完整时间演化的显式解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该闭合方案为Riviera模型提供了高效、精确且具解析形式的求解途径，填补了该模型缺乏精确解的空白。

Abstract: The Riviera model is a random sequential deposition model for house building on a lattice, in which a house cannot be built when both nearest-neighbor sites are already occupied. In one dimension, an attempted deposition is therefore rejected whenever the target site is an isolated vacancy. Despite the apparent simplicity of this rule, no exact analytical solution of the original model is currently known. We derive an infinite hierarchy of kinetic equations governing its dynamics and introduce a systematic closure scheme that can be implemented at arbitrary finite order. Solving the resulting systems order by order, we show that the corresponding kinetics converges rapidly toward the exact behavior. At high order, the method yields an exceptionally accurate estimate of the jamming density while retaining explicit analytical expressions for the full time-dependent evolution.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum Chaos and Diffusive Transport from Geometric Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28579)
*Bibek Saha,Abhishek Dhar,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明了几何随机性（而非微观无序或多体相互作用）本身即可产生量子混沌和扩散输运。通过研究具有均匀耦合的随机局部树状分层图上的无相互作用量子粒子，发现图的几何随机性和有效维度决定了混沌动力学的存在与否：广泛的层尺寸产生稳健的量子混沌、能级排斥和扩散输运；而在准一维极限下，则出现局域态与扩展态共存的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，量子混沌和扩散输运被认为需要微观无序或多体相互作用作为必要条件。本文旨在探索几何随机性是否可以作为产生这些现象的独立且基本的机制，从而扩展对量子混沌起源的理解。

Method: 研究了具有均匀耦合的随机局部树状分层图上的无相互作用量子粒子。这些图可以视为规则方格晶格或梯子的结构无序广义化，或等价于具有随机链接的多分量一维链。通过分析不同层尺寸（广泛的层尺寸vs准一维极限）下的动力学行为来系统研究几何随机性的影响。

Result: 发现广泛的层尺寸能产生稳健的量子混沌、能级排斥和扩散输运；而在准一维极限下，局域态和扩展态大量共存，导致能级排斥被抑制，同时扩展态驱动弹道输运。

Conclusion: 几何随机性是一种产生和调控量子混沌的独立且基本的新机制，其效果由几何随机性和有效图维度共同决定。这一发现为量子混沌研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The physics of quantum chaos and diffusive transport is typically studied in settings with microscopic disorder or many-body interactions. In this Letter, we demonstrate that these phenomena can arise purely from geometric randomness. By studying non-interacting quantum particles on random locally tree-like layered graphs with uniform couplings, we show that the geometric randomness and effective graph dimensionality dictates the presence of chaotic dynamics or lack thereof. These graphs can be considered as structurally disordered generalisations of regular square lattices or ladders, or equivalently as multi-component one-dimensional chains with random links between the components. We find that an extensive layer size yields robust quantum chaos, level repulsion, and diffusive transport. Conversely, in the quasi-one-dimensional limit, we find the coexistence of extensive number of localised and delocalised states -- this leads to suppressed level repulsion accompanied by the latter driving ballistic transport. These results establish geometric randomness as a fundamental and independent mechanism for generating and tuning quantum chaos.

</details>
